# 🤖 AGENTS.md — Panduan Khusus AI Agent (UI/Frontend Development)

> **Peran Agent:** Senior Frontend / UI / UX Engineer  
> **Tujuan Proyek:** Mengembangkan, mempercantik, dan menyempurnakan tampilan antarmuka (UI/UX) portal **Dashboard Alumni STMM MMTC Yogyakarta** agar modern, responsif, elegan, dan berstandar industri tanpa merusak logika backend yang sudah berjalan.

---

## 🛑 ATURAN MUTLAK: JANGAN SENTUH BACKEND (Strict Guardrails)

Backend (Firebase Auth, Cloud Firestore, Firebase Storage, dan Security Rules) **SUDAH SELESAI, STABIL, DAN SUDAH DI-AUDIT SECARA KETAT**. 

### ❌ File yang DILARANG DIBUBAH / DIHAPUS:
1. **`src/lib/*`** (`firebase.ts`, `firestoreRest.ts`, `auth.ts`, `jobs.ts`, `media.ts`)  
   👉 Dilarang mengubah alur data fetching, login/register logic, REST query, atau endpoint API.
2. **`firestore.rules` & `storage.rules`**  
   👉 Dilarang mengedit atau merelaksasi security rules.
3. **`src/context/AuthContext.tsx`**  
   👉 Dilarang mengubah state management auth, session tracking, atau fallback logic.
4. **`src/types/index.ts`**  
   👉 Dilarang menghapus atau mengubah interface inti (`UserProfile`, `JobPosting`, `JobApplication`). Jika butuh interface untuk prop komponen UI murni, definisikan di file komponen masing-masing atau buat file types UI terpisah.
5. **`.env*` & `.env.local`**  
   👉 Jangan pernah membuat perubahan yang mengekspos environment variables ke git.

### ❌ Larangan Teknis & Anti-Pattern:
- **JANGAN** mengubah nama `field` yang dikirim ke form submit (seperti `nomorAlumni`, `prodi`, `angkatan`, `openToWork`, dll.) karena terhubung langsung ke skema Firestore.
- **JANGAN** menggunakan manipulasi DOM langsung (`document.getElementById`, `parent.appendChild`, atau event handler imperatif) yang melanggar siklus render React 19.
- **JANGAN** menginstal library UI raksasa yang tidak perlu. Gunakan **Tailwind CSS v4** dan **Lucide React** yang sudah terpasang.

---

## 🎨 WILAYAH KERJA UTAMA (UI / Frontend Focus)

Agent dipersilakan berkreasi dan memoles bagian-bagian berikut:

### 1. Komponen & Styling
- **`src/components/layout/`**: `Navbar.tsx`, `Footer.tsx` (desain navigasi, menu mobile, animasi dropdown, state active).
- **`src/components/profile/`**: `AlumniAvatar.tsx` (komponen avatar deklaratif), card showreel, badge status.
- **`src/components/ui/`**: Buat komponen baru modular jika diperlukan (e.g. `Modal.tsx`, `Button.tsx`, `Card.tsx`, `Skeleton.tsx`, `Badge.tsx`, `Tabs.tsx`).
- **`src/app/globals.css`**: Pengaturan tema Tailwind v4, custom utility classes, styling scrollbar, glassmorphism, animasi fade-in/scale.

### 2. Halaman-Halaman (Layout, Typography, & UX Flow)
- **`src/app/page.tsx`**: Landing page (Hero banner, statistik alumni, carousel/grid talenta showreel, call-to-action).
- **`src/app/open-to-work/page.tsx`**: Katalog talenta alumni (Card design, search bar, filter tabs prodi, responsive grid, empty state).
- **`src/app/lowongan/page.tsx`**: Bursa kerja (Card lowongan, detail pekerjaan, modal submit lamaran yang rapi & estetik).
- **`src/app/post-lowongan/page.tsx`**: Formulir pasang lowongan kerja (Form layout, label input, helper text, styling tombol).
- **`src/app/profil/page.tsx`**: Editor profil alumni (Avatar uploader visual, showreel gallery grid 2-kolom, form section cards).
- **`src/app/profil/[uid]/page.tsx`**: Halaman profil alumni publik (Portfolio view, link kontak, embed YouTube responsif).
- **`src/app/lamaran/page.tsx`**: Dashboard tracking pelamar & lamaran terkirim (Status badge aesthetic, table/card list, visual CTA).
- **`src/app/login/page.tsx` & `src/app/register/page.tsx`**: Tampilan autentikasi (Card form bersih, visual error alerts yang ramah, tombol aksi kontras).

---

## 🎯 PEDOMAN DESAIN (Design System Guidelines)

1. **Palet Warna STMM MMTC:**
   - **Background Utama:** Bersih & terang (`bg-white` / `bg-slate-50`).
   - **Warna Aksen:** MMTC Blue (`#0284c7` Sky-600, `#0369a1` Sky-700, `#024E82` MMTC Deep Blue).
   - **Teks:** Kontras tinggi dan mudah dibaca (`text-slate-900`, `text-slate-700`, secondary `text-slate-500`).
   - **Status Badge:** Emerald (Accepted/Open to Work), Amber (Pending/Review), Red (Expired/Rejected), Sky (Prodi Badge).
2. **Layout Portofolio / Showreel:**
   - Pertahankan susunan responsif **2 kolom** pada desktop untuk video YouTube embed dan portfolio cards agar tidak memanjang ke bawah sendirian.
3. **Responsivitas:**
   - Mobile-First design: wajib dites pada resolusi **Mobile (375px–420px)**, **Tablet (768px)**, dan **Desktop (1024px–1440px)**.
   - Hindari overflow horizontal (`overflow-x-hidden` jika diperlukan).
4. **Feedback & Loading States:**
   - Ganti spinner polos dengan **Skeleton UI** saat data sedang dimuat agar layout tidak jumping (Cumulative Layout Shift = 0).

---

## 🔄 WORKFLOW GIT (Ambil Repo, Branching, & Pull Request)

Collaborator & Agent WAJIB mengikuti alur Git berikut:

### 1. Setup Awal (Pilih Salah Satu Sesuai Hak Akses)

#### Opsi A: Jika Ditambahkan Sebagai Collaborator Repo
```bash
# 1. Clone repository
git clone https://github.com/farhanditof-max/WebsiteAlumniSTMM.git
cd WebsiteAlumniSTMM

# 2. Pastikan branch utama up-to-date
git checkout main
git pull origin main

# 3. Buat branch baru untuk fitur UI yang dikerjakan
# Format nama: feat/ui-<nama-fitur> atau fix/ui-<nama-fitur>
git checkout -b feat/ui-navbar-redesign
```

#### Opsi B: Jika Menggunakan Fork (External Contributor)
```bash
# 1. Fork repository di GitHub (https://github.com/farhanditof-max/WebsiteAlumniSTMM)
# 2. Clone fork lo
git clone https://github.com/<username-lo>/WebsiteAlumniSTMM.git
cd WebsiteAlumniSTMM

# 3. Hubungkan upstream repo asli
git remote add upstream https://github.com/farhanditof-max/WebsiteAlumniSTMM.git

# 4. Buat branch kerja
git checkout -b feat/ui-hero-banner
```

### 2. Persiapan Environment Lokal
```bash
# Install dependensi
npm install

# Buat file env lokal dari template
cp .env.example .env.local
# (Minta isi kredensial Firebase testing ke owner jika butuh preview data riil)

# Jalankan dev server
npm run dev
```

### 3. Checklist Verifikasi SEBELUM Commit / Push
Sebelum membuat commit, Agent **WAJIB** menjalankan 3 perintah ini dan memastikan **0 ERROR**:
```bash
# 1. Type check
npx tsc --noEmit

# 2. Linter check
npm run lint

# 3. Production build test (memastikan tidak ada SSR / hydration crash)
npm run build
```

### 4. Push & Buat Pull Request (PR)
```bash
# Stage & commit perubahan dengan pesan deskriptif
git add .
git commit -m "feat(ui): redesign card talenta open-to-work dan tambah skeleton loader"

# Push ke branch
git push origin feat/ui-<nama-fitur>

# Buka GitHub dan buat Pull Request ke branch `main` di repo utama:
# farhanditof-max/WebsiteAlumniSTMM (base: main) <- (compare: feat/ui-...)
```

> ⚠️ **PERINGATAN:** JANGAN PERNAH force push (`git push -f`) atau push langsung ke branch `main`! Selalu gunakan Pull Request agar perubahan UI bisa di-review bersama.

# 🎓 Dashboard Alumni MMTC Yogyakarta

Portal web resmi jejaring alumni, direktori talenta kreatif (*Open to Work*), dan bursa lowongan kerja bagi lulusan **Sekolah Tinggi Multi Media (STMM "MMTC") Yogyakarta** — Kementerian Komunikasi dan Digital RI.

---

## 🚀 Fitur Utama

- **Directory Alumni & Open to Work**: Eksplorasi profil alumni dari seluruh Program Studi MMTC (Animasi, Game Design, MIK, Matekstosi, Manarita, Manaprodsi) lengkap dengan badge keahlian dan status kesiapan kerja.
- **Portofolio Kreatif & Showreel Embed**: Integrasi responsif 2-kolom untuk video YouTube Showreel, Behance, Artstation, Pinterest, dan tautan portofolio eksternal.
- **Bursa Lowongan Kerja & Rekrutmen Internal**: Publikasi lowongan kerja oleh alumni untuk sesama alumni dengan filter program studi dan validasi deadline.
- **Pelacakan Lamaran & Kontak Cepat**: Ajukan lamaran dengan cover letter & CV PDF, pelacakan status lamaran real-time, serta tombol direct WhatsApp (`wa.me`) untuk recruiter.
- **Verifikasi Nomor Alumni & Autentikasi Aman**: Validasi format identitas alumni MMTC (`MMTC-YYYY-XXX`) dengan proteksi keamanan Firebase Auth, Firestore Security Rules, dan Storage Rules.

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack

- **Framework**: [Next.js 16 (App Router)](https://nextjs.org/)
- **UI Library**: [React 19](https://react.dev/) & [Tailwind CSS v4](https://tailwindcss.com/)
- **Icons**: [Lucide React](https://lucide.dev/)
- **Backend & Database**: [Google Firebase v12](https://firebase.google.com/)
  - **Firebase Authentication** (Email & Password)
  - **Cloud Firestore** (Database dokumen profil, lowongan, dan lamaran)
  - **Firebase Storage** (Penyimpanan CV PDF & foto avatar)

---

## 📦 Panduan Instalasi Lokal

### 1. Clone Repository
```bash
git clone https://github.com/farhanditof-max/WebsiteAlumniSTMM.git
cd WebsiteAlumniSTMM
```

### 2. Pasang Dependensi
```bash
npm install
```

### 3. Konfigurasi Environment Variables
Salin file `.env.example` menjadi `.env.local`:
```bash
cp .env.example .env.local
```
Lalu lengkapi konfigurasi kredensial Firebase web app Anda di `.env.local`:
```env
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY=AIzaSy...
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN=dashboard-alumni-mmtc.firebaseapp.com
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID=dashboard-alumni-mmtc
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET=dashboard-alumni-mmtc.firebasestorage.app
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID=...
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID=...
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MEASUREMENT_ID=...
```

### 4. Jalankan Development Server
```bash
npm run dev
```
Buka [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) pada browser Anda.

---

## 🔒 Firebase Security Rules

Project ini sudah dilengkapi security rules siap-pakai:
- `firestore.rules`: Membatasi pembacaan dan penulisan dokumen berdasarkan kepemilikan UID pengguna.
- `storage.rules`: Membatasi pengunggahan file CV (maksimal 5MB, format PDF) dan foto profil avatar (maksimal 3MB).

Deploy aturan ke Firebase via Firebase CLI:
```bash
firebase deploy --only firestore:rules,storage
```

---

## 🌐 Deployment (Vercel)

Proyek ini dioptimasi penuh untuk dideploy langsung ke **Vercel**:
1. Push repository ini ke GitHub.
2. Buka dashboard [Vercel](https://vercel.com) dan pilih **Add New Project**.
3. Hubungkan repository GitHub Anda.
4. Masukkan Environment Variables sesuai isi file `.env.local`.
5. Klik **Deploy**.

---

// ============================================================================
// FILE: firestore.rules
// ============================================================================
rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {

    function signedIn()   { return request.auth != null; }
    function isAdmin()    {
      return signedIn() && (
        ('admin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.admin == true) ||
        ('role' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.role == 'admin')
      );
    }
    function isOwner(uid) { return signedIn() && request.auth.uid == uid; }
    function locked(keys) {
      return !request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasAny(keys);
    }

    // Profil Alumni Privat (F-01, F-02, F-08, F-20, F-22):
    // Data privat dibaca hanya oleh pemilik atau admin. Data publik dilayani melalui public_profiles.
    match /users/{userId} {
      allow read: if isOwner(userId) || isAdmin();
      allow create: if isOwner(userId)
                    && request.resource.data.uid == userId
                    && (!request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['role']) || request.resource.data.role != 'admin')
                    && request.resource.data.namaLengkap is string
                    && request.resource.data.nomorAlumni is string
                    && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/alumni_registry/$(request.resource.data.nomorAlumni))
                    && getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/alumni_registry/$(request.resource.data.nomorAlumni)).data.isRegistered == true
                    && getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/alumni_registry/$(request.resource.data.nomorAlumni)).data.registeredUid == userId
                    && request.resource.data.prodi == getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/alumni_registry/$(request.resource.data.nomorAlumni)).data.prodi
                    && request.resource.data.angkatan == getAfter(/databases/$(database)/documents/alumni_registry/$(request.resource.data.nomorAlumni)).data.angkatan;
      allow update: if (isOwner(userId)
                    && locked(['role', 'uid', 'nomorAlumni', 'createdAt', 'prodi', 'angkatan'])
                    && request.resource.data.namaLengkap is string
                    && (request.resource.data.openToWork is bool || !request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['openToWork']))
                    && (!request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['portfolio']) || (request.resource.data.portfolio is list && request.resource.data.portfolio.size() <= 12))
                    ) || isAdmin();
      allow delete: if isAdmin();
    }

    // Profil Publik Mirror (F-02 Whitelist):
    // Dibaca publik, dimutakhirkan oleh pemilik akun (hanya whitelist field non-PII) atau Admin/Cloud Function.
    match /public_profiles/{userId} {
      allow read: if true;
      allow create, update: if isOwner(userId)
                    && (!request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['uid']) || request.resource.data.uid == userId)
                    && request.resource.data.namaLengkap is string
                    && (!request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['email', 'phone', 'nomorHp', 'cvUrl', 'cvFileUrl']))
                    && (request.resource.data.openToWork is bool || !request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['openToWork']))
                    && (!request.resource.data.keys().hasAny(['portfolio']) || (request.resource.data.portfolio is list && request.resource.data.portfolio.size() <= 12));
      allow write: if isAdmin();
    }

    // Lowongan Kerja (F-05, F-20, F-22):
    // postedBy terikat permanen ke request.auth.uid, validasi tipe data ketat.
    match /jobs/{jobId} {
      allow read: if true;
      allow create: if signedIn()
                    && request.resource.data.postedBy == request.auth.uid
                    && request.resource.data.judul is string
                    && request.resource.data.perusahaan is string
                    && request.resource.data.lokasi is string
                    && request.resource.data.deskripsi is string
                    && request.resource.data.prodiTarget is list
                    && request.resource.data.kualifikasi is list;
      allow update: if (signedIn()
                    && resource.data.postedBy == request.auth.uid
                    && locked(['postedBy', 'createdAt'])
                    && request.resource.data.judul is string
                    && request.resource.data.perusahaan is string
                    && request.resource.data.lokasi is string
                    && request.resource.data.deskripsi is string
                    && request.resource.data.prodiTarget is list
                    && request.resource.data.kualifikasi is list
                    ) || isAdmin();
      allow delete: if (signedIn() && resource.data.postedBy == request.auth.uid) || isAdmin();
    }

    // Lamaran Kerja (F-06, F-21, F-22, F4-02):
    // Deterministic ID: jobId_applicantUid untuk mencegah duplicate spam & race condition.
    match /applications/{applicationId} {
      allow read: if signedIn() && (
        resource.data.applicantUid == request.auth.uid || 
        resource.data.posterUid == request.auth.uid ||
        isAdmin()
      );
      allow create: if signedIn() 
                    && applicationId == request.resource.data.jobId + '_' + request.auth.uid
                    && request.resource.data.applicantUid == request.auth.uid
                    && (!exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/jobs/$(request.resource.data.jobId)) || request.resource.data.posterUid == get(/databases/$(database)/documents/jobs/$(request.resource.data.jobId)).data.postedBy)
                    && request.resource.data.status == 'pending'
                    && request.resource.data.pesan is string;
      allow update: if signedIn() && (
        (
          resource.data.posterUid == request.auth.uid
          && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'updatedAt'])
          && request.resource.data.status in ['reviewed', 'accepted', 'rejected']
        ) || isAdmin()
      );
      allow delete: if (signedIn() && resource.data.applicantUid == request.auth.uid && resource.data.status == 'pending') || isAdmin();
    }

    // Permintaan Akses Kontak (F-10, F-21, F-22, F4-02):
    // Deterministic ID: requesterUid_targetUid
    match /contact_requests/{requestId} {
      allow read: if signedIn() && (
        resource.data.requesterUid == request.auth.uid || 
        resource.data.targetUid == request.auth.uid ||
        isAdmin()
      );
      allow create: if signedIn() 
                    && requestId == request.auth.uid + '_' + request.resource.data.targetUid
                    && request.resource.data.requesterUid == request.auth.uid
                    && request.resource.data.targetUid != request.auth.uid
                    && request.resource.data.status == 'pending'
                    && request.resource.data.message is string;
      allow update: if signedIn() && (
        (
          resource.data.targetUid == request.auth.uid
          && (
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'updatedAt']) ||
            request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'updatedAt', 'targetPhone', 'targetEmail'])
          )
          && request.resource.data.status in ['approved', 'rejected']
        ) || isAdmin()
      );
      allow delete: if (signedIn() && resource.data.requesterUid == request.auth.uid && resource.data.status == 'pending') || isAdmin();
    }

    // Basis Data Master Alumni (Registry Whitelist F-04, F-24, F2-03):
    match /alumni_registry/{alumniId} {
      allow get: if signedIn();
      allow list, create, delete: if isAdmin();

      allow update: if isAdmin() || (
        signedIn()
        && resource.data.isRegistered == false
        && request.resource.data.isRegistered == true
        && request.resource.data.registeredUid == request.auth.uid
        && locked(['nomorAlumni', 'namaLengkap', 'prodi', 'angkatan', 'createdAt'])
        && !exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid))
      );
    }

    // Feed Jejaring Sosial Komunitas:
    // Baca terbuka untuk publik, kreasi/interaksi wajib login
    match /feed_posts/{postId} {
      allow read: if true;
      allow create: if signedIn()
                    && request.resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid
                    && request.resource.data.content is string
                    && request.resource.data.content.size() > 0
                    && request.resource.data.content.size() <= 3000
                    && request.resource.data.category in ['showreel', 'kolaborasi', 'diskusi', 'pencapaian']
                    && request.resource.data.likesCount == 0
                    && request.resource.data.comments is list;
      allow update: if signedIn() && (
                      // Pemilik post memperbarui konten
                      (resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid && !request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasAny(['authorUid', 'createdAt']))
                      ||
                      // Pengguna lain yang login memberikan Like atau Komentar
                      (request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['likesCount', 'likedBy', 'comments', 'commentsCount', 'updatedAt']))
                    );
      allow delete: if signedIn() && (resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
    }

    // Percakapan Pesan Antar-Alumni:
    match /conversations/{convId} {
      allow read, update, delete: if signedIn() && request.auth.uid in resource.data.participants;
      allow create: if signedIn() && request.auth.uid in request.resource.data.participants;
    }

    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// FILE: storage.rules
// ============================================================================
rules_version = '2';
service firebase.storage {
  match /b/{bucket}/o {

    function signedIn() { return request.auth != null; }
    function isOwner(userId) { return signedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId; }
    function isAdmin() {
      return signedIn() && (
        ('admin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.admin == true) ||
        ('role' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.role == 'admin')
      );
    }

    // Foto Profil (Avatars):
    // F-03 & F-23: Pisahkan operasi delete dari create/update.
    // Menolak SVG (vektor XSS), batasi ukuran maksimal 3MB, dan nama file tetap untuk mencegah storage flooding.
    match /avatars/{userId}/{fileName} {
      allow read: if true;
      allow delete: if isOwner(userId);
      allow create, update: if isOwner(userId)
                    && fileName.matches('^avatar\\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$')
                    && request.resource.size < 3 * 1024 * 1024
                    && request.resource.contentType.matches('image/(jpeg|png|webp)');
    }

    // Dokumen CV (CV Documents):
    // F-03, F-23, F2-04: Membatasi izin baca hanya untuk pemilik akun dan admin, format PDF maks 5MB dengan nama tetap cv.pdf.
    match /cv_documents/{userId}/{fileName} {
      allow read: if isOwner(userId) || (signedIn() && (
        ('admin' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.admin == true) ||
        ('role' in request.auth.token && request.auth.token.role == 'admin')
      ));
      allow delete: if isOwner(userId);
      allow create, update: if isOwner(userId)
                    && fileName == 'cv.pdf'
                    && request.resource.size < 5 * 1024 * 1024
                    && request.resource.contentType == 'application/pdf';
    }

    // Lampiran Media Feed Komunitas (Foto & PDF):
    // F-03, F-12: Menolak SVG (vektor XSS), batas ukuran maks 10MB, menerima jpeg/png/webp dan PDF.
    match /feed_media/{userId}/{fileName} {
      allow read: if true;
      allow delete: if isOwner(userId) || isAdmin();
      allow create, update: if isOwner(userId)
                    && request.resource.size < 10 * 1024 * 1024
                    && (
                      request.resource.contentType.matches('image/(jpeg|png|webp)') ||
                      request.resource.contentType == 'application/pdf'
                    );
    }

    match /{allPaths=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }
  }
}

// FILE: functions\src\index.ts
// ============================================================================
import * as functions from "firebase-functions";
import * as admin from "firebase-admin";

admin.initializeApp();
const db = admin.firestore();

/**
 * Normalizes Alumni Registration Number
 */
function normalizeNomorAlumni(raw: string): string {
  return raw.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, "");
}

/**
 * Sanitizes URLs to prevent XSS (javascript: or data: injections)
 */
function isSafeUrl(url?: string): boolean {
  if (!url) return false;
  const trimmed = url.trim();
  return trimmed.startsWith("https://") || trimmed.startsWith("http://");
}

/**
 * Callable Function: claimAlumni
 * Implements F-01, F-04, F-24:
 * - Server-side atomic validation against `alumni_registry`
 * - Validates second-factor (name matching)
 * - Prevents race conditions (TOCTOU) via Firestore Transaction
 * - Creates private `users/{uid}` and mirrored `public_profiles/{uid}` with official data
 */
export const claimAlumni = functions.https.onCall(async (data, context) => {
  if (!context.auth) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
      "unauthenticated",
      "Pengguna harus masuk terlebih dahulu untuk mengklaim akun alumni."
    );
  }

  const uid = context.auth.uid;
  const userEmail = context.auth.token.email || "";
  const rawNomor = data?.nomorAlumni;
  const inputNama = data?.namaLengkap;

  if (!rawNomor || typeof rawNomor !== "string" || !inputNama || typeof inputNama !== "string") {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
      "invalid-argument",
      "Nomor alumni dan nama lengkap wajib diisi."
    );
  }

  const normalizedNomor = normalizeNomorAlumni(rawNomor);
  const trimmedNama = inputNama.trim().toLowerCase();

  const registryRef = db.collection("alumni_registry").doc(normalizedNomor);
  const userRef = db.collection("users").doc(uid);
  const publicProfileRef = db.collection("public_profiles").doc(uid);

  return await db.runTransaction(async (transaction) => {
    // 1. Cek apakah user sudah terdaftar di users/{uid}
    const userDoc = await transaction.get(userRef);
    if (userDoc.exists && userDoc.data()?.nomorAlumni) {
      throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
        "already-exists",
        "Akun Anda sudah terhubung dengan data alumni."
      );
    }

    // 2. Baca data master registry
    const registryDoc = await transaction.get(registryRef);
    if (!registryDoc.exists) {
      throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
        "not-found",
        "Nomor alumni tidak ditemukan dalam data master institusi."
      );
    }

    const masterData = registryDoc.data()!;

    // 3. Cek apakah nomor alumni sudah pernah diklaim oleh UID lain
    if (masterData.claimed && masterData.claimedBy && masterData.claimedBy !== uid) {
      throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
        "permission-denied",
        "Nomor alumni ini sudah terdaftar oleh pengguna lain. Silakan hubungi bagian administrasi."
      );
    }

    // 4. Verifikasi faktor kedua: Pencocokan nama resmi dengan nama input
    const masterNama = String(masterData.namaLengkap || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (masterNama !== trimmedNama) {
      throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
        "invalid-argument",
        "Nama lengkap yang Anda masukkan tidak cocok dengan arsip data alumni resmi."
      );
    }

    const now = admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();

    // 5. Tandai nomor alumni sebagai diklaim (Atomic Lock)
    transaction.update(registryRef, {
      claimed: true,
      claimedBy: uid,
      claimedAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    });

    // 6. Tulis dokumen privat users/{uid}
    const userData = {
      uid,
      email: userEmail,
      namaLengkap: masterData.namaLengkap, // Menggunakan nama resmi master
      nomorAlumni: normalizedNomor,
      prodi: masterData.prodi,
      angkatan: masterData.angkatan,
      role: "alumni",
      openToWork: false,
      portfolio: [],
      emailJobAlerts: true,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    };
    transaction.set(userRef, userData, { merge: true });

    // 7. Tulis dokumen profil publik public_profiles/{uid} (hanya field whitelist)
    const publicProfileData = {
      uid,
      namaLengkap: masterData.namaLengkap,
      prodi: masterData.prodi,
      angkatan: masterData.angkatan,
      openToWork: false,
      portfolio: [],
      updatedAt: now,
    };
    transaction.set(publicProfileRef, publicProfileData, { merge: true });

    return {
      success: true,
      message: "Verifikasi alumni berhasil. Profil resmi telah diaktifkan.",
      alumni: {
        namaLengkap: masterData.namaLengkap,
        prodi: masterData.prodi,
        angkatan: masterData.angkatan,
        nomorAlumni: normalizedNomor,
      },
    };
  });
});

/**
 * Background Trigger: syncPublicProfile
 * Memastikan setiap pembaruan pada `users/{uid}` hanya mengekspos field aman (whitelist)
 * ke `public_profiles/{uid}` tanpa pernah mengekspos email, no telpon, atau CV pribadi.
 */
export const syncPublicProfile = functions.firestore
  .document("users/{uid}")
  .onWrite(async (change, context) => {
    const uid = context.params.uid;
    const publicProfileRef = db.collection("public_profiles").doc(uid);

    // Jika user dihapus, hapus juga profil publik
    if (!change.after.exists) {
      await publicProfileRef.delete();
      return;
    }

    const userData = change.after.data() || {};

    // Filter sanitasi portfolio (maksimal 12 dan validasi HTTP/HTTPS)
    const rawPortfolio = Array.isArray(userData.portfolio) ? userData.portfolio : [];
    const sanitizedPortfolio = rawPortfolio.slice(0, 12).map((item: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
      judul: typeof item?.judul === "string" ? item.judul.slice(0, 150) : "",
      deskripsi: typeof item?.deskripsi === "string" ? item.deskripsi.slice(0, 500) : "",
      url: isSafeUrl(item?.url) ? item.url : "",
      platform: typeof item?.platform === "string" ? item.platform : "other",
    }));

    // Data whitelist yang aman dibaca publik
    const publicData = {
      uid,
      namaLengkap: typeof userData.namaLengkap === "string" ? userData.namaLengkap : "",
      prodi: typeof userData.prodi === "string" ? userData.prodi : "",
      angkatan: typeof userData.angkatan === "number" || typeof userData.angkatan === "string" ? userData.angkatan : "",
      bio: typeof userData.bio === "string" ? userData.bio.slice(0, 500) : "",
      keahlian: Array.isArray(userData.keahlian) ? userData.keahlian.slice(0, 20) : [],
      portfolio: sanitizedPortfolio,
      openToWork: Boolean(userData.openToWork),
      avatarUrl: isSafeUrl(userData.avatarUrl) ? userData.avatarUrl : null,
      domisili: typeof userData.domisili === "string" ? userData.domisili.slice(0, 100) : "",
      updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    };

    await publicProfileRef.set(publicData, { merge: true });
  });

/**
 * Callable Function: setAdminRole
 * Memberikan custom claims { admin: true } oleh super-admin yang sah (F-01).
 */
export const setAdminRole = functions.https.onCall(async (data, context) => {
  // Hanya admin dengan custom claim admin == true yang dapat memberikan hak admin ke akun lain
  if (!context.auth || context.auth.token.admin !== true) {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError(
      "permission-denied",
      "Hanya administrator resmi institusi yang memiliki otoritas ini."
    );
  }

  const targetUid = data?.targetUid;
  const makeAdmin = Boolean(data?.isAdmin);

  if (!targetUid || typeof targetUid !== "string") {
    throw new functions.https.HttpsError("invalid-argument", "targetUid wajib diisi.");
  }

  // Set Firebase Auth Custom Claims
  await admin.auth().setCustomUserClaims(targetUid, { admin: makeAdmin });

  // Update role di dokumen Firestore pengguna
  await db.collection("users").doc(targetUid).update({
    role: makeAdmin ? "admin" : "alumni",
    updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    targetUid,
    role: makeAdmin ? "admin" : "alumni",
  };
});

const now = new Date();
const dateStr = now.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').substring(0, 19);

let output = `================================================================================
CODEBASE AUDIT & SECURITY REVISION - DASHBOARD ALUMNI STMM MMTC
Generated: ${dateStr}
Branch: fix/security-audit-comprehensive-patches
================================================================================\n\n`;

for (const relPath of files) {
  const fullPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), relPath);
  if (!fs.existsSync(fullPath)) {
    console.warn(`File missing: ${relPath}`);
    continue;
  }
  const content = fs.readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf8');
  const normalizedRel = relPath.replace(/\//g, '\\');
  output += `================================================================================
FILE: ${normalizedRel}
================================================================================
${content.trimEnd()}\n\n`;
}

const rootOut = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'codebase.txt');
const auditOut = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'audit', 'codebase.txt');

fs.writeFileSync(rootOut, output, 'utf8');
fs.writeFileSync(auditOut, output, 'utf8');

console.log(`Successfully updated codebase.txt (${output.length} bytes, ${files.length} files) in root and audit/`);

/**
 * Cloud Presentation Seeder for Dashboard Alumni STMM MMTC
 * Seeds official presentation personas, jobs, master alumni registry, and feed posts
 * directly into Cloud Firestore or local environment using Firebase Client SDK.
 *
 * Usage:
 *   npx ts-node --project tests/tsconfig.json scripts/seed-cloud-presentation.ts
 */

// Allow connection through Windows corporate proxies with local TLS inspection
process.env.NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED = "0";

import { initializeApp, getApps, getApp } from "firebase/app";
import { getFirestore, doc, setDoc } from "firebase/firestore";
import * as path from "path";
import * as fs from "fs";

// Load environment variables from .env.local if present
const envLocalPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".env.local");
if (fs.existsSync(envLocalPath)) {
  const content = fs.readFileSync(envLocalPath, "utf8");
  content.split("\n").forEach((line) => {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed && !trimmed.startsWith("#")) {
      const idx = trimmed.indexOf("=");
      if (idx > 0) {
        const key = trimmed.substring(0, idx).trim();
        const val = trimmed.substring(idx + 1).trim();
        if (!process.env[key]) {
          process.env[key] = val;
        }
      }
    }
  });
}

const firebaseConfig = {
  apiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY,
  authDomain: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN,
  projectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID || "dashboard-alumni-mmtc",
  storageBucket: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET,
  messagingSenderId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID,
  appId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID,
};

const app = !getApps().length ? initializeApp(firebaseConfig) : getApp();
const db = getFirestore(app);

async function seedCloud() {
  console.log(`Starting Cloud Presentation Seeding for project [${firebaseConfig.projectId}]...`);

  const runWithTimeout = async <T>(promise: Promise<T>, timeoutMs = 8000, label = "Operation"): Promise<T> => {
    return Promise.race([
      promise,
      new Promise<T>((_, reject) =>
        setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`${label} timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms (TLS proxy/gRPC network block)`)), timeoutMs)
      ),
    ]);
  };

  try {
    // 1. Seed alumni_registry
    console.log("1. Seeding master alumni registry...");
    for (const a of MASTER_ALUMNI) {
      await runWithTimeout(
        setDoc(doc(db, "alumni_registry", a.nomorAlumni), {
          ...a,
          isRegistered: false,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
        }, { merge: true }),
        6000,
        `Seeding registry ${a.nomorAlumni}`
      );
    }

    // 2. Seed feed_posts
    console.log("2. Seeding curated feed posts...");
    for (const post of CURATED_FEED_POSTS) {
      await runWithTimeout(
        setDoc(doc(db, "feed_posts", post.id), post, { merge: true }),
        6000,
        `Seeding feed post ${post.id}`
      );
    }

    // 3. Seed jobs
    console.log("3. Seeding presentation jobs...");
    for (const job of CURATED_JOBS) {
      await runWithTimeout(
        setDoc(doc(db, "jobs", job.id), job, { merge: true }),
        6000,
        `Seeding job ${job.id}`
      );
    }

    console.log("✅ Cloud Presentation Seeding completed successfully!");
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const error = err as Error;
    console.warn("\n⚠️ Cloud Presentation Seeding could not connect directly from Node CLI:");
    console.warn(`   Reason: ${error.message}`);
    console.warn("\n💡 CATATAN DEMO PRESENTASI TANGGAL 10:");
    console.warn("   1. Data kurasi awal (5 postingan feed, master alumni, dan lowongan) sudah otomatis");
    console.warn("      tersedia secara lokal melalui fallback service di src/lib/feed.ts & components.");
    console.warn("   2. Untuk men-deploy rules dan seed ke Cloud Firebase riil, jalankan:");
    console.warn("      npx firebase-tools login --reauth");
    console.warn("      npx firebase-tools deploy --only firestore:rules,storage");
  } finally {
    process.exit(0);
  }
}

seedCloud().catch(err => {
  console.error("Fatal error:", err);
  process.exit(1);
});

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import ScrollReveal from '@/components/ui/ScrollReveal';
import { PRODI_LIST } from '@/types';
import { 
  ArrowRight, 
  ArrowUpRight,
  Video, 
  Users, 
  Briefcase,
  CheckCircle2,
  Award,
  ShieldCheck,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  GraduationCap,
  Play,
  Calendar,
  Handshake,
  BookOpen
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function HomePage() {
  const getProdiCardImage = (id: string) => {
    switch (id) {
      case 'Animasi':
        return '/card_prodi/animasi.svg';
      case 'Matekstosi':
        return '/card_prodi/matekstosi.svg';
      case 'Manarita':
        return '/card_prodi/manarita.svg';
      case 'Manaprodsi':
        return '/card_prodi/manaprodsi.svg';
      case 'MIK':
        return '/card_prodi/mik.svg';
      case 'Game Design':
        return '/card_prodi/game-design.svg';
      default:
        return '/card_prodi/animasi.svg';
    }
  };

  const LOOP_PRODI_LIST = React.useMemo(() => [
    ...PRODI_LIST.map((p) => ({ ...p, loopKey: `set-0-${p.id}` })),
    ...PRODI_LIST.map((p) => ({ ...p, loopKey: `set-1-${p.id}` })),
    ...PRODI_LIST.map((p) => ({ ...p, loopKey: `set-2-${p.id}` })),
    ...PRODI_LIST.map((p) => ({ ...p, loopKey: `set-3-${p.id}` })),
  ], []);

  const sliderRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = React.useState(false);
  const [isRolling, setIsRolling] = React.useState(false);
  const isDraggingRef = React.useRef(false);
  const dragDistanceRef = React.useRef(0);
  const velocityRef = React.useRef(0);
  const lastXRef = React.useRef(0);
  const lastTimeRef = React.useRef(0);
  const animFrameIdRef = React.useRef<number | null>(null);

  const checkWrap = React.useCallback(() => {
    const el = sliderRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const singleWidth = el.scrollWidth / 4;
    if (singleWidth <= 10) return;

    if (el.scrollLeft >= singleWidth * 2.5) {
      el.scrollLeft -= singleWidth;
    } else if (el.scrollLeft <= singleWidth * 0.5) {
      el.scrollLeft += singleWidth;
    }
  }, []);

  const stopInertia = React.useCallback(() => {
    if (animFrameIdRef.current) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);
      animFrameIdRef.current = null;
    }
    setIsRolling(false);
  }, []);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const el = sliderRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const singleWidth = el.scrollWidth / 4;
    if (singleWidth > 0) {
      el.scrollLeft = singleWidth;
    }
    return () => {
      if (animFrameIdRef.current) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameIdRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const startFling = (initialVelocity: number) => {
    if (Math.abs(initialVelocity) < 0.12 || !sliderRef.current) return;
    stopInertia();
    setIsRolling(true);

    let currentVel = Math.max(-3.8, Math.min(3.8, initialVelocity));
    const friction = 0.958;
    let lastFrameTime = performance.now();

    const step = (now: number) => {
      const dt = Math.min(32, now - lastFrameTime);
      lastFrameTime = now;

      if (!sliderRef.current || Math.abs(currentVel) < 0.03) {
        stopInertia();
        return;
      }

      const frameRatio = dt / 16.6;
      sliderRef.current.scrollLeft -= currentVel * 16 * frameRatio;
      checkWrap();

      currentVel *= Math.pow(friction, frameRatio);
      animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(step);
    };

    animFrameIdRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(step);
  };

  const scrollSlider = (direction: 'left' | 'right') => {
    if (sliderRef.current) {
      stopInertia();
      const scrollAmount = direction === 'left' ? -340 : 340;
      sliderRef.current.scrollBy({ left: scrollAmount, behavior: 'smooth' });
      setTimeout(checkWrap, 450);
    }
  };

  const handleMouseDown = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    stopInertia();
    isDraggingRef.current = true;
    setIsDragging(true);
    lastXRef.current = e.pageX;
    lastTimeRef.current = performance.now();
    velocityRef.current = 0;
    dragDistanceRef.current = 0;
  };

  const handleMouseMove = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (!isDraggingRef.current || !sliderRef.current) return;
    e.preventDefault();

    const currentX = e.pageX;
    const currentTime = performance.now();
    const deltaX = currentX - lastXRef.current;
    const deltaTime = Math.max(1, currentTime - lastTimeRef.current);

    dragDistanceRef.current += Math.abs(deltaX);
    const instVel = deltaX / deltaTime;
    velocityRef.current = velocityRef.current * 0.3 + instVel * 0.7;

    sliderRef.current.scrollLeft -= deltaX;
    checkWrap();

    lastXRef.current = currentX;
    lastTimeRef.current = currentTime;
  };

  const handleMouseUp = () => {
    if (!isDraggingRef.current) return;
    isDraggingRef.current = false;
    setIsDragging(false);

    if (performance.now() - lastTimeRef.current > 80) {
      velocityRef.current = 0;
      return;
    }
    startFling(velocityRef.current);
  };

  const handleMouseLeave = () => {
    if (isDraggingRef.current) handleMouseUp();
  };

  const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    stopInertia();
    isDraggingRef.current = true;
    setIsDragging(true);
    lastXRef.current = e.touches[0].pageX;
    lastTimeRef.current = performance.now();
    velocityRef.current = 0;
    dragDistanceRef.current = 0;
  };

  const handleTouchMove = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (!isDraggingRef.current || !sliderRef.current) return;
    const currentX = e.touches[0].pageX;
    const currentTime = performance.now();
    const deltaX = currentX - lastXRef.current;
    const deltaTime = Math.max(1, currentTime - lastTimeRef.current);

    dragDistanceRef.current += Math.abs(deltaX);
    const instVel = deltaX / deltaTime;
    velocityRef.current = velocityRef.current * 0.3 + instVel * 0.7;

    sliderRef.current.scrollLeft -= deltaX;
    checkWrap();

    lastXRef.current = currentX;
    lastTimeRef.current = currentTime;
  };

  const handleTouchEnd = () => {
    if (!isDraggingRef.current) return;
    isDraggingRef.current = false;
    setIsDragging(false);

    if (performance.now() - lastTimeRef.current > 80) {
      velocityRef.current = 0;
      return;
    }
    startFling(velocityRef.current);
  };

  const handleCardClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (dragDistanceRef.current > 8 || isRolling) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col selection:bg-[#0284c7] selection:text-white font-sans overflow-x-hidden">
      <Navbar />

      {/* HERO SECTION */}
      <section className="relative w-full min-h-[calc(100vh-74px)] sm:min-h-[calc(100vh-80px)] flex flex-col justify-between py-10 sm:py-14 lg:py-16 overflow-hidden bg-white border-b border-slate-200/80">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none select-none overflow-hidden">
          <img 
            src="/landing_page_hero_raw.jpg" 
            alt="Komunitas Alumni STMM MMTC Yogyakarta" 
            className="w-full h-full object-cover object-[center_top] sm:object-[68%_center] lg:object-[center_center]"
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-white/95 via-white/85 to-transparent sm:via-white/55 lg:via-transparent pointer-events-none" />
        </div>

        <div className="relative max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 w-full my-auto z-10">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-center">
            <div className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-6 text-left">
              <ScrollReveal direction="up" delayMs={60} durationMs={700}>
                <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900/5 border border-slate-900/10 text-[#1e3a8a] text-xs font-semibold">
                  <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                  <span>Ikatan Alumni STMM &ldquo;MMTC&rdquo; Yogyakarta</span>
                </div>

                {/* Headline bersih tanpa stabilo miring -rotate-1 ala AI */}
                <h1 className="text-4xl sm:text-5xl lg:text-[54px] font-extrabold text-slate-900 leading-[1.12] tracking-tight mt-5">
                  Tetap Terhubung, <br />
                  <span className="text-[#0284c7]">Terus Berkarya</span> <br />
                  Bersama.
                </h1>

                <p className="text-base text-slate-700 max-w-lg leading-relaxed mt-4">
                  Ruang kolaborasi alumni Sekolah Tinggi Multi Media lintas angkatan—tempat berbagi peluang produksi, memamerkan showreel, dan membangun jejaring industri.
                </p>

                <div className="pt-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-3.5">
                  <Link 
                    href="/open-to-work" 
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-[#024E82] hover:bg-[#1e3a8a] text-white font-semibold text-sm shadow-sm transition-all duration-200 active:scale-[0.99]"
                  >
                    <span>Eksplorasi Talenta</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </Link>

                  <Link 
                    href="/feed" 
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-white/95 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 font-semibold text-sm border border-slate-300 transition-all duration-200 active:scale-[0.99]"
                  >
                    <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current text-[#0284c7]" />
                    <span>Buka Kabar &amp; Karya Alumni</span>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </ScrollReveal>
            </div>

            <div className="lg:col-span-6 hidden lg:block min-h-[380px] pointer-events-none" />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* BOTTOM FEATURE BAR */}
        <div className="relative max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 w-full pt-8 sm:pt-10 pb-2 z-10">
          <ScrollReveal direction="up" delayMs={180} durationMs={700}>
            <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl py-4 px-6 sm:px-8 shadow-sm grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4 sm:gap-6 divide-y sm:divide-y-0 sm:divide-x divide-slate-200/70">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 px-2 py-1 sm:py-0">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <Users className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div className="text-left">
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">Jejaring Lintas Angkatan</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Koneksi rekan seprofesi</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 px-2 sm:pl-6 py-1 sm:py-0">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <Handshake className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div className="text-left">
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">Bursa Kerja &amp; Kru</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Lowongan studio &amp; proyek</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 px-2 sm:pl-6 py-1 sm:py-0">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <Calendar className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div className="text-left">
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">Agenda &amp; Screening</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Jadwal temu &amp; pameran</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 px-2 sm:pl-6 py-1 sm:py-0">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div className="text-left">
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">Kabar Produksi</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Diskusi alur kerja &amp; riset</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </ScrollReveal>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* PROGRAM STUDI SLIDER */}
      <section id="prodi-section" className="py-20 sm:py-24 bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200/70 scroll-mt-12 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <ScrollReveal direction="up" delayMs={60} durationMs={650}>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end justify-between gap-6 mb-10">
              <div className="text-left max-w-2xl">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#0284c7] block mb-2">
                  6 Program Studi STMM MMTC
                </span>
                <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">
                  Telusuri Talenta Berdasarkan Keahlian
                </h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
                  Pilih program studi untuk melihat direktori alumni dan lowongan kerja yang relevan dengan bidang produksi.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => scrollSlider('left')}
                  className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs flex items-center justify-center transition cursor-pointer"
                  aria-label="Geser ke kiri"
                >
                  <ChevronLeft className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => scrollSlider('right')}
                  className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs flex items-center justify-center transition cursor-pointer"
                  aria-label="Geser ke kanan"
                >
                  <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </ScrollReveal>

          <div className="relative -mx-4 px-4 sm:-mx-6 sm:px-6 lg:-mx-8 lg:px-8">
            <div
              ref={sliderRef}
              onScroll={checkWrap}
              onMouseDown={handleMouseDown}
              onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
              onMouseUp={handleMouseUp}
              onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}
              onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
              onTouchMove={handleTouchMove}
              onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
              className={`flex items-stretch gap-5 overflow-x-auto py-4 px-1 select-none scrollbar-none ${
                isDragging || isRolling ? 'cursor-grabbing' : 'cursor-grab'
              }`}
              style={{ scrollbarWidth: 'none', msOverflowStyle: 'none' }}
            >
              {LOOP_PRODI_LIST.map((prodi) => {
                const cardImg = getProdiCardImage(prodi.id);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={prodi.loopKey}
                    className="w-[260px] sm:w-[280px] lg:w-[300px] shrink-0 h-full"
                  >
                    <Link
                      href={`/lowongan?prodi=${encodeURIComponent(prodi.id)}`}
                      onClick={handleCardClick}
                      className="group relative block bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-1 transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer select-none"
                      draggable={false}
                    >
                      <div className="relative aspect-[4/5] w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-100">
                        <img
                          src={cardImg}
                          alt={`Program Studi ${prodi.label} STMM MMTC`}
                          className="w-full h-full object-cover select-none pointer-events-none transition-transform duration-500 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.02]"
                          draggable={false}
                        />
                        <div className="absolute bottom-3.5 right-3.5 w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/95 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-slate-800 group-hover:bg-[#024E82] group-hover:text-white group-hover:border-[#024E82] transition-colors duration-200 pointer-events-none">
                          <ArrowUpRight className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* SPOTLIGHT BANNER */}
      <section className="py-16 bg-white">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <ScrollReveal direction="up" delayMs={80} durationMs={750}>
            <div className="bg-[#024E82] rounded-2xl p-8 sm:p-12 text-white shadow-sm">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-center">
                <div className="lg:col-span-8 space-y-4">
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-sky-200 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                    Ekosistem Profesi STMM MMTC Yogyakarta
                  </span>
                  <h3 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold leading-snug">
                    Jembatan Kolaborasi Industri Penyiaran &amp; Kreatif Digital
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-sky-100/90 max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">
                    Berawal dari produksi studio kampus hingga memimpin ruang siar, proyek animasi, dan pengembangan gim nasional. Pajang showreel terbaikmu dan terhubung langsung dengan industri.
                  </p>
                  <div className="pt-2">
                    <Link
                      href="/open-to-work"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl bg-white text-[#024E82] font-bold text-sm hover:bg-sky-50 transition"
                    >
                      Lihat Direktori Talenta
                      <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="lg:col-span-4 space-y-3.5 bg-white/10 p-6 rounded-xl border border-white/15">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                    <Award className="w-5 h-5 text-sky-300 shrink-0" />
                    <span>6 Program Studi Penyiaran &amp; Multimedia</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                    <Video className="w-5 h-5 text-sky-300 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Kurasi Showreel &amp; Portofolio Karya</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                    <Briefcase className="w-5 h-5 text-sky-300 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Akses Langsung ke Perekrut Alumni</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                    <Users className="w-5 h-5 text-sky-300 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Jejaring Lulusan di Studio Nasional</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </ScrollReveal>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* BOTTOM CTA: Ganti gaya "SaaS 100% Gratis" menjadi Kartu Klaim Akun Institusi */}
      <section className="py-16 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200/80">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <ScrollReveal direction="up" delayMs={80} durationMs={750}>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-center">
              <div className="md:col-span-7 space-y-4">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#0284c7] uppercase tracking-wider block">
                  Pendaftaran &amp; Akses Alumni
                </span>
                <h2 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                  Bangun Portofolio &amp; Rekrut Rekan Sealmamater
                </h2>
                <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed max-w-xl">
                  Kelola halaman showreel pribadimu, lamar posisi di berbagai rumah produksi, atau pasang info rekrutmen kru untuk proyek studiomu berikutnya.
                </p>
                <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Terverifikasi khusus civitas &amp; alumni resmi STMM MMTC</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Integrasi pemutar showreel YouTube &amp; dokumen CV PDF</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Publikasi lowongan langsung ke program studi terkait</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="md:col-span-5">
                <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-7 sm:p-8 space-y-6 shadow-sm">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 block">
                        Portal Resmi AlumniHub
                      </span>
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">
                        Klaim Akun Alumni
                      </h3>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center">
                      <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Gunakan Nomor Alumni resmi (format <code className="text-xs font-mono bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-slate-800">MMTC-YYYY-XXX</code>) untuk memverifikasi profilmu secara instan.
                  </p>

                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <Link
                      href="/register"
                      className="w-full py-3.5 px-6 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white font-semibold text-sm shadow-2xs transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                    >
                      <span>Verifikasi &amp; Buat Akun</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </Link>

                    <Link
                      href="/post-lowongan"
                      className="w-full py-3.5 px-6 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 font-semibold text-sm border border-slate-200 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                    >
                      <Briefcase className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-600" />
                      <span>Pasang Info Lowongan / Kru</span>
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </ScrollReveal>
        </div>
      </section>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}
