// FILE: src\app\admin\alumni\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';
import { 
  getAllAlumniRegistry, 
  addAlumniToRegistry, 
  deleteAlumniFromRegistry,
  normalizeNomorAlumni
} from '@/lib/auth';
import { PRODI_LIST, ProdiType, AlumniRegistryItem } from '@/types';
import { 
  ShieldCheck, 
  UserPlus, 
  Trash2, 
  Search, 
  RefreshCw, 
  Loader2, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle, 
  Database,
  ArrowLeft,
  X,
  Plus,
  Lock
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function AdminAlumniRegistryPage() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();
  const [alumniList, setAlumniList] = useState<AlumniRegistryItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedProdi, setSelectedProdi] = useState<string>('ALL');
  
  // Modal / Form state
  const [showAddModal, setShowAddModal] = useState(false);
  const [nomorAlumni, setNomorAlumni] = useState('');
  const [namaLengkap, setNamaLengkap] = useState('');
  const [prodi, setProdi] = useState<ProdiType>('Animasi');
  const [angkatan, setAngkatan] = useState('2024');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  // Status notifications
  const [statusMsg, setStatusMsg] = useState<{ type: 'success' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);

  const fetchRegistry = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await getAllAlumniRegistry();
      setAlumniList(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load alumni registry:', err);
      setStatusMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Gagal memuat master data alumni dari database.' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    async function loadData() {
      try {
        const data = await getAllAlumniRegistry();
        if (isMounted) {
          setAlumniList(data);
          setLoading(false);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load alumni registry:', err);
        if (isMounted) {
          setStatusMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Gagal memuat master data alumni dari database.' });
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }
    loadData();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const handleNomorChange = (val: string) => {
    setNomorAlumni(val);
    const clean = normalizeNomorAlumni(val);
    const parts = clean.split('-');
    if (parts.length >= 2 && /^\d{4}$/.test(parts[1])) {
      setAngkatan(parts[1]);
    }
  };

  const handleAddAlumni = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!nomorAlumni.trim() || !namaLengkap.trim()) {
      setStatusMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Nomor Alumni dan Nama Lengkap wajib diisi.' });
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setStatusMsg(null);

    try {
      await addAlumniToRegistry({
        nomorAlumni: nomorAlumni.trim(),
        namaLengkap: namaLengkap.trim(),
        prodi,
        angkatan: angkatan.trim()
      });

      setStatusMsg({ 
        type: 'success', 
        text: `Data alumni "${namaLengkap}" (${nomorAlumni.trim()}) berhasil ditambahkan ke database master.` 
      });
      setShowAddModal(false);
      setNomorAlumni('');
      setNamaLengkap('');
      await fetchRegistry();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const e = err as Error;
      setStatusMsg({ type: 'error', text: e.message || 'Gagal menambahkan alumni ke registry.' });
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (noAlumni: string, nama: string) => {
    if (!confirm(`Hapus data alumni "${nama}" (${noAlumni}) dari database resmi? Alumni ini tidak akan bisa mendaftar akun web lagi.`)) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      await deleteAlumniFromRegistry(noAlumni);
      setStatusMsg({ type: 'success', text: `Data alumni "${noAlumni}" berhasil dihapus.` });
      setAlumniList(prev => prev.filter(a => a.nomorAlumni !== noAlumni));
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const e = err as Error;
      setStatusMsg({ type: 'error', text: e.message || 'Gagal menghapus data alumni.' });
    }
  };

  const filteredList = alumniList.filter(item => {
    const matchesProdi = selectedProdi === 'ALL' || item.prodi === selectedProdi;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    const noAlumniStr = String(item.nomorAlumni || '').toLowerCase();
    const namaStr = String(item.namaLengkap || '').toLowerCase();
    const angkatanStr = String(item.angkatan || '');
    const matchesSearch = 
      !q || 
      noAlumniStr.includes(q) ||
      namaStr.includes(q) ||
      angkatanStr.includes(q);
    return matchesProdi && matchesSearch;
  });

  const registeredCount = alumniList.filter(a => a.isRegistered).length;
  const unclaimedCount = alumniList.length - registeredCount;

  // F-01 Server/Client Access Guard: Cegah akses tidak sah ke data master
  if (!loading && (!user || profile?.role !== 'admin')) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between">
        <Navbar />
        <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-6">
          <div className="max-w-md w-full bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center space-y-4 shadow-sm">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-red-50 text-red-600 border border-red-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
              <Lock className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Akses Terbatas Administrator</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 leading-relaxed">
              Halaman Master Data Alumni STMM MMTC hanya dapat diakses oleh akun dengan peran Administrator resmi kampus.
            </p>
            <div className="pt-2">
              <Link
                href="/"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-800 transition shadow-xs"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                Kembali ke Beranda
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10">
        
        {/* TOP BREADCRUMB & HEADER */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-8">
          <div>
            <Link 
              href="/"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 hover:text-[#0284c7] transition mb-2"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Kembali ke Beranda
            </Link>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">Admin Portal — Master Data Alumni</h1>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Basis data resmi alumni STMM MMTC untuk verifikasi pendaftaran akun baru.</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* ACTION BUTTONS */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={fetchRegistry}
              disabled={loading}
              className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-2xs transition disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
              title="Segarkan data dari database Firestore"
            >
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />}
              Segarkan Data
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowAddModal(true)}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Tambah Alumni Baru
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* STATUS MESSAGES */}
        {statusMsg && (
          <div
            className={`mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl text-xs flex items-center gap-2 border ${
              statusMsg.type === 'success'
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700'
                : 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-700'
            }`}
          >
            {statusMsg.type === 'success' ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            ) : (
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            )}
            <span>{statusMsg.text}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* METRICS STATS CARDS */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-8">
          <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-xs">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Total Master Data</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-1">{alumniList.length}</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">Data resmi terdaftar di database</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-xs">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Sudah Buat Akun Web</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-emerald-700 mt-1">{registeredCount}</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">Alumni aktif yang sudah registrasi</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-xs">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-amber-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Belum Registrasi</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-amber-700 mt-1">{unclaimedCount}</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">Nomor resmi siap diklaim pendaftar</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SEARCH & FILTER CONTROLS */}
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 mb-6 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-xs">
          <div className="relative w-full md:w-80">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Cari nomor alumni, nama, angkatan..."
              className="w-full text-xs pl-9 pr-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-purple-600"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto overflow-x-auto pb-1 md:pb-0">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium shrink-0">Prodi:</span>
            <select
              value={selectedProdi}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedProdi(e.target.value)}
              className="text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-600"
            >
              <option value="ALL">Semua Program Studi</option>
              {PRODI_LIST.map((p) => (
                <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                  {p.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={fetchRegistry}
              disabled={loading}
              className="p-2 rounded-xl text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 transition"
              title="Segarkan data"
            >
              <RefreshCw className={`w-4 h-4 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* MASTER DATA TABLE */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden shadow-xs">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="py-20 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 text-slate-400">
              <Loader2 className="w-7 h-7 animate-spin text-purple-600" />
              <p className="text-xs">Memuat master data alumni dari database...</p>
            </div>
          ) : filteredList.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-16 text-center text-slate-400 space-y-2">
              <Database className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-slate-300" />
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tidak ada data alumni yang cocok dengan pencarian.</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Coba kata kunci lain atau tambahkan alumni baru.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="bg-slate-50/80 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px]">
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">No.</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Nomor Alumni</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Nama Lengkap Resmi</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Program Studi</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Angkatan</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Status Akun Web</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Aksi</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {filteredList.map((alumni, idx) => (
                    <tr key={alumni.nomorAlumni} className="hover:bg-slate-50/60 transition">
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-400 font-mono text-[11px]">{idx + 1}</td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">
                        {alumni.nomorAlumni}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-medium text-slate-800">
                        {alumni.namaLengkap}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] font-semibold text-[10px] border border-sky-100">
                          {alumni.prodi}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{alumni.angkatan}</td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                        {alumni.isRegistered ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 text-[10px] font-semibold border border-emerald-200">
                            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Sudah Terdaftar
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 text-[10px] font-medium border border-slate-200">
                            Belum Klaim Akun
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleDelete(alumni.nomorAlumni, alumni.namaLengkap)}
                          className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg transition"
                          title="Hapus data alumni ini"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* MODAL TAMBAH ALUMNI BARU */}
      {showAddModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-xl border border-slate-200 space-y-5 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Tambah Data Alumni Baru</h3>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Masukkan nomor dan nama resmi mahasiswa/alumni STMM.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button 
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowAddModal(false)}
                className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 transition cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleAddAlumni} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Nomor Alumni <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={nomorAlumni}
                  onChange={(e) => handleNomorChange(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Contoh: MMTC-2024-105 atau STMM-2023-010"
                  className="w-full text-xs px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 font-mono uppercase focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-purple-600"
                />
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Format standar: MMTC-YYYY-XXX atau STMM-YYYY-XXX</p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Nama Lengkap Resmi <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={namaLengkap}
                  onChange={(e) => setNamaLengkap(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Contoh: Muhammad Farhan Dito"
                  className="w-full text-xs px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-purple-600"
                />
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Pendaftar wajib mengetik nama ini secara cocok agar bisa register.</p>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Program Studi <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={prodi}
                    onChange={(e) => setProdi(e.target.value as ProdiType)}
                    className="w-full text-xs px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-purple-600"
                  >
                    {PRODI_LIST.map((p) => (
                      <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                        {p.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Tahun Angkatan <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={angkatan}
                    onChange={(e) => setAngkatan(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="2024"
                    className="w-full text-xs px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-purple-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowAddModal(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl transition cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Batal
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 rounded-xl transition inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {submitting ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                      Menyimpan...
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <UserPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      Simpan ke Database
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\app\aktivitas\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';
import { getUserFeedPosts, deleteFeedPost } from '@/lib/feed';
import { 
  Heart, 
  MessageSquare, 
  Share2, 
  Trash2, 
  FileText, 
  PlusCircle, 
  Maximize2, 
  Calendar, 
  ExternalLink, 
  Check,
  ArrowLeft,
  Briefcase,
  Video,
  Users,
  Award
} from 'lucide-react';
import AlumniAvatar from '@/components/profile/AlumniAvatar';
import { CinemaLightbox } from '@/components/ui/Modal';
import { parseYouTubeVideoId } from '@/lib/media';
import { UserProfile } from '@/types';

interface ExtendedUserProfile extends UserProfile {
  pekerjaanSaatIni?: string;
}

export interface UserActivityPost {
  id: string;
  authorName: string;
  authorProdi: string;
  authorAngkatan?: string;
  authorRole?: string;
  authorAvatar?: string;
  timeAgo: string;
  category: 'showreel' | 'kolaborasi' | 'diskusi' | 'pencapaian';
  content: string;
  hashtags?: string[];
  mediaType?: 'youtube' | 'image' | 'pdf';
  mediaUrl?: string;
  mediaTitle?: string;
  mediaSize?: string;
  likesCount: number;
  commentsCount?: number;
}

const DEFAULT_POSTS: UserActivityPost[] = [
  {
    id: 'user-post-1',
    authorName: 'Budi Santoso',
    authorProdi: 'Animasi',
    authorAngkatan: '2020',
    authorRole: '3D Animator di Infinite Studios',
    timeAgo: '1 hari yang lalu',
    category: 'showreel',
    content: 'Baru saja merilis breakdown rigging character stylized untuk project open-source animasi MMTC. Fokus pada face controls dan flexible spine setup di Blender. Silakan tonton showreel-nya di bawah ya rekan-rekan!',
    hashtags: ['#Blender3D', '#RiggingBreakdown', '#AnimasiMMTC', '#CharacterArt'],
    mediaType: 'youtube',
    mediaUrl: 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ',
    mediaTitle: '3D Facial & Body Rigging Breakdown 2026',
    likesCount: 38,
    commentsCount: 6
  },
  {
    id: 'user-post-2',
    authorName: 'Budi Santoso',
    authorProdi: 'Animasi',
    authorAngkatan: '2020',
    authorRole: '3D Animator di Infinite Studios',
    timeAgo: '4 hari yang lalu',
    category: 'diskusi',
    content: 'Sharing tips workflow animasi kamera di Unreal Engine 5 untuk keperluan sinematik broadcast. Menggabungkan teknik blocking kamera klasik MMTC dengan Sequencer UE5. Ada yang tertarik adakan sharing session santai weekend ini?',
    hashtags: ['#UnrealEngine5', '#Cinematography', '#MMTCAlumni', '#VirtualProduction'],
    mediaType: 'image',
    mediaUrl: '/kampus_stmm.jpg',
    mediaTitle: 'Virtual Production Studio Setup Memories',
    likesCount: 52,
    commentsCount: 11
  }
];

export default function AktivitasPage() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();

  const currentUser = {
    name: profile?.namaLengkap || user?.displayName || 'Budi Santoso',
    prodi: profile?.prodi || 'Animasi',
    angkatan: profile?.angkatan || '2020',
    role: (profile as ExtendedUserProfile | null)?.pekerjaanSaatIni || '3D Animator di Infinite Studios',
    avatar: profile?.fotoProfil || ''
  };

  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<UserActivityPost[]>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      try {
        const stored = localStorage.getItem('alumni_user_posts');
        if (stored) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {
            const existingIds = new Set(parsed.map((p: UserActivityPost) => p.id));
            return [
              ...parsed,
              ...DEFAULT_POSTS.filter(p => !existingIds.has(p.id))
            ];
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }
    return DEFAULT_POSTS;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    if (user?.uid) {
      getUserFeedPosts(user.uid).then((userPosts) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        if (userPosts.length > 0) {
          const mapped: UserActivityPost[] = userPosts.map(p => ({
            id: p.id,
            authorName: p.authorName,
            authorProdi: p.authorProdi,
            authorAngkatan: p.authorAngkatan,
            authorRole: p.authorRole,
            authorAvatar: p.authorAvatar,
            timeAgo: p.createdAt ? new Date(p.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }) : 'Baru saja',
            category: p.category,
            content: p.content,
            hashtags: p.hashtags,
            mediaType: p.mediaType,
            mediaUrl: p.mediaUrl,
            mediaTitle: p.mediaTitle,
            mediaSize: p.mediaSize,
            likesCount: p.likesCount,
            commentsCount: p.commentsCount
          }));
          setPosts(mapped);
        }
      }).catch(err => {
        console.warn('AktivitasPage load user posts error:', err);
      });
    }
    return () => { isMounted = false; };
  }, [user?.uid]);

  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>('semua');
  const [likedPosts, setLikedPosts] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [cinemaVideo, setCinemaVideo] = useState<{ videoId: string; title: string } | null>(null);
  const [actionAlert, setActionAlert] = useState<string>('');

  const handleToggleLike = (id: string) => {
    setLikedPosts(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [id]: !prev[id]
    }));
  };

  const handleDeletePost = async (id: string) => {
    const updated = posts.filter(p => p.id !== id);
    setPosts(updated);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('alumni_user_posts', JSON.stringify(updated));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }

    if (user?.uid) {
      try {
        await deleteFeedPost(id);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('AktivitasPage deleteFeedPost sync warning:', err);
      }
    }

    setActionAlert('Postingan berhasil dihapus dari riwayat aktivitas Anda.');
    setTimeout(() => setActionAlert(''), 3000);
  };

  const handleShare = (post: UserActivityPost) => {
    if (navigator.clipboard) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(`${window.location.origin}/feed#${post.id}`);
      setActionAlert('Tautan postingan berhasil disalin ke clipboard!');
      setTimeout(() => setActionAlert(''), 2500);
    }
  };

  const filteredPosts = posts.filter(post => {
    if (selectedCategory === 'semua') return true;
    return post.category === selectedCategory;
  });

  const totalLikes = posts.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr.likesCount + (likedPosts[curr.id] ? 1 : 0), 0);
  const totalComments = posts.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.commentsCount || 0), 0);

  const getCategoryBadge = (category: string) => {
    switch (category) {
      case 'showreel':
        return <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200 text-[10px] font-semibold"><Video className="w-3 h-3" />Showreel</span>;
      case 'kolaborasi':
        return <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 text-[10px] font-semibold"><Users className="w-3 h-3" />Kolaborasi</span>;
      case 'pencapaian':
        return <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 border border-sky-200 text-[10px] font-semibold"><Award className="w-3 h-3" />Prestasi</span>;
      default:
        return <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 text-[10px] font-semibold"><MessageSquare className="w-3 h-3" />Diskusi</span>;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafcff] text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-10">
        
        {/* TOP BACK SHORTCUT */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <Link
            href="/feed"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0284c7] transition"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Kembali ke Feed Komunitas</span>
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* HERO HEADER: MANAGE POSTS & ACTIVITY (FACEBOOK / LINKEDIN STYLE) */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 mb-8 shadow-sm space-y-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 sm:gap-5">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-2xl font-bold shadow-xs border-2 border-white ring-4 ring-sky-50 shrink-0">
                <AlumniAvatar src={currentUser.avatar} name={currentUser.name} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#0284c7] block">
                  Dashboard Kelola Postingan
                </span>
                <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">
                  Postingan &amp; Aktivitas {currentUser.name}
                </h1>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  <span>{currentUser.prodi} MMTC • Angkatan {currentUser.angkatan}</span>
                  {currentUser.role && (
                    <>
                      <span>•</span>
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[#0284c7]">
                        <Briefcase className="w-3 h-3" />
                        {currentUser.role}
                      </span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 shrink-0">
              <Link
                href="/feed"
                className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-2 shadow-2xs cursor-pointer active:scale-95"
              >
                <PlusCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Buat Postingan Baru</span>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 3 SUMMARY STATS */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3.5 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100">
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-100">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                Total Postingan Anda
              </span>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1">
                {posts.length} <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-normal">Konten</span>
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-sky-50/60 border border-sky-100">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#0284c7] uppercase tracking-wider block">
                Total Apresiasi / Suka
              </span>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[#0284c7] mt-1">
                {totalLikes} <span className="text-xs text-sky-600 font-normal">Suka</span>
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50/60 border border-emerald-100">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-700 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                Total Interaksi Komentar
              </span>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-700 mt-1">
                {totalComments} <span className="text-xs text-emerald-600 font-normal">Komentar</span>
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* NOTIFICATION ALERT */}
        {actionAlert && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 animate-fade-in shadow-2xs">
            <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
            <span>{actionAlert}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* FILTER CATEGORY PILLS */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-6">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            {[
              { id: 'semua', label: 'Semua Postingan' },
              { id: 'showreel', label: 'Showreel' },
              { id: 'diskusi', label: 'Diskusi' },
              { id: 'kolaborasi', label: 'Kolaborasi' },
              { id: 'pencapaian', label: 'Prestasi' }
            ].map(tab => (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(tab.id)}
                className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer select-none ${
                  selectedCategory === tab.id
                    ? 'bg-[#0284c7] text-white font-bold shadow-2xs'
                    : 'bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium">
            Menampilkan {filteredPosts.length} dari {posts.length} postingan
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* POSTS LIST (MANAGEABLE CARDS) */}
        {filteredPosts.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 bg-white border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-3xl p-8 space-y-3">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
              <MessageSquare className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800">
              Belum ada postingan dalam kategori ini
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto">
              Bagikan karya video showreel, update produksi, atau buka diskusi baru bersama sesama alumni STMM MMTC.
            </p>
            <div className="pt-2">
              <Link
                href="/feed"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold transition shadow-2xs"
              >
                <PlusCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Buat Postingan di Feed</span>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            {filteredPosts.map((post) => {
              const isLiked = likedPosts[post.id];
              const currentLikes = post.likesCount + (isLiked ? 1 : 0);
              const ytId = post.mediaType === 'youtube' && post.mediaUrl ? parseYouTubeVideoId(post.mediaUrl) : null;

              return (
                <div 
                  key={post.id}
                  className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-4 hover:border-sky-200 hover:shadow-xs transition"
                >
                  {/* HEADER ROW */}
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                      <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm shadow-2xs shrink-0">
                        <AlumniAvatar 
                          src={post.authorAvatar || currentUser.avatar} 
                          name={post.authorName || currentUser.name} 
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                            {post.authorName || currentUser.name}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-xs text-[#0284c7] font-semibold">
                            • {post.authorProdi || currentUser.prodi}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                          <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-300" />
                          <span>{post.timeAgo}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {getCategoryBadge(post.category)}
                      
                      {/* DELETE POST BUTTON */}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleDeletePost(post.id)}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-slate-400 hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 transition cursor-pointer"
                        title="Hapus Postingan dari Riwayat"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* CONTENT TEXT */}
                  <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                    {post.content}
                  </p>

                  {/* HASHTAGS */}
                  {post.hashtags && post.hashtags.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {post.hashtags.map((tag) => (
                        <span key={tag} className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0284c7] hover:underline cursor-pointer">
                          {tag}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* MEDIA ATTACHMENTS */}
                  {post.mediaType === 'youtube' && post.mediaUrl && ytId && (
                    <div className="relative rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-900 group aspect-video">
                      <iframe
                        src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${ytId}?rel=0`}
                        title={post.mediaTitle || 'Showreel'}
                        allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                        allowFullScreen
                        className="w-full h-full border-0"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setCinemaVideo({ videoId: ytId, title: post.mediaTitle || 'Showreel' })}
                        className="absolute top-2.5 right-2.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-black/75 hover:bg-black/90 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 backdrop-blur-md opacity-90 hover:opacity-100 transition shadow-sm cursor-pointer z-10"
                        title="Mode Bioskop Layar Penuh"
                      >
                        <Maximize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-sky-400" />
                        <span>Mode Bioskop</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {post.mediaType === 'image' && post.mediaUrl && (
                    <div className="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 max-h-80 bg-slate-100">
                      <img 
                        src={post.mediaUrl} 
                        alt={post.mediaTitle || 'Media Foto'}
                        className="w-full h-auto max-h-80 object-cover"
                      />
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {post.mediaType === 'pdf' && (
                    <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-red-100 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm shrink-0">
                          <FileText className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 truncate">
                            {post.mediaTitle || 'Dokumen PDF'}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                            {post.mediaSize || 'PDF Document'} • Terverifikasi Alumni MMTC
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      {post.mediaUrl && post.mediaUrl !== '#' && (
                        <a
                          href={post.mediaUrl}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noreferrer"
                          className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-[#0284c7] hover:bg-sky-50 text-xs font-bold transition shrink-0"
                        >
                          Buka PDF
                        </a>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* ACTION BAR / FOOTER */}
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 text-slate-500 text-xs">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleToggleLike(post.id)}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer select-none ${
                          isLiked ? 'text-red-500 font-bold' : 'hover:text-red-500'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <Heart className={`w-4 h-4 ${isLiked ? 'fill-red-500 text-red-500' : ''}`} />
                        <span>{currentLikes} Suka</span>
                      </button>

                      <Link
                        href="/feed"
                        className="flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-[#0284c7] transition"
                      >
                        <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                        <span>{post.commentsCount || 0} Komentar</span>
                      </Link>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleShare(post)}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-[#0284c7] transition cursor-pointer"
                        title="Salin Tautan Postingan"
                      >
                        <Share2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>

                      <Link
                        href="/feed"
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#0284c7] hover:underline"
                      >
                        <span>Lihat di Feed</span>
                        <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </Link>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* CINEMA LIGHTBOX */}
        <CinemaLightbox
          isOpen={Boolean(cinemaVideo)}
          onClose={() => setCinemaVideo(null)}
          title={cinemaVideo?.title || 'Preview Showreel'}
          videoId={cinemaVideo?.videoId || ''}
        />
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

const DATA_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data');
const DB_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'mmtc_cloud_db.json');
const UPLOADS_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'public', 'uploads');

interface DbSchema {
  alumni_registry: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
  users: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
  public_profiles: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
  feed_posts: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
  jobs: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
  applications: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
  conversations: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
}

function loadDb(): DbSchema {
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(DATA_DIR)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
    }
    if (!fs.existsSync(DB_FILE)) {
      const initial: DbSchema = {
        alumni_registry: { ...INITIAL_ALUMNI_REGISTRY },
        users: {},
        public_profiles: { ...INITIAL_PUBLIC_PROFILES },
        feed_posts: { ...INITIAL_FEED_POSTS },
        jobs: { ...INITIAL_JOBS },
        applications: {},
        conversations: {}
      };
      fs.writeFileSync(DB_FILE, JSON.stringify(initial, null, 2), 'utf8');
      return initial;
    }
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(DB_FILE, 'utf8');
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<DbSchema>;
    // Ensure all master alumni exist
    const registry = { ...INITIAL_ALUMNI_REGISTRY, ...(parsed.alumni_registry || {}) };
    return {
      alumni_registry: registry,
      users: parsed.users || {},
      public_profiles: parsed.public_profiles || { ...INITIAL_PUBLIC_PROFILES },
      feed_posts: parsed.feed_posts || { ...INITIAL_FEED_POSTS },
      jobs: parsed.jobs || { ...INITIAL_JOBS },
      applications: parsed.applications || {},
      conversations: parsed.conversations || {}
    };
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error loading local server DB:', err);
    return {
      alumni_registry: { ...INITIAL_ALUMNI_REGISTRY },
      users: {},
      public_profiles: { ...INITIAL_PUBLIC_PROFILES },
      feed_posts: { ...INITIAL_FEED_POSTS },
      jobs: { ...INITIAL_JOBS },
      applications: {},
      conversations: {}
    };
  }
}

function saveDb(db: DbSchema): void {
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(DATA_DIR)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
    }
    fs.writeFileSync(DB_FILE, JSON.stringify(db, null, 2), 'utf8');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error saving local server DB:', err);
  }
}

function getAuthenticatedUid(req: NextRequest): string | null {
  const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization') || '';
  if (authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    const token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
    if (token) {
      if (token.includes('.')) {
        try {
          const parts = token.split('.');
          if (parts.length >= 2) {
            const payload = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(parts[1], 'base64').toString('utf8'));
            return payload.user_id || payload.sub || payload.uid || null;
          }
        } catch {
          // invalid token payload
        }
      } else {
        return token;
      }
    }
  }
  const xUid = req.headers.get('x-user-uid');
  if (xUid && typeof xUid === 'string') return xUid.trim();
  return null;
}

function sanitizeUserProfile(user: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
  const safe = { ...user };
  delete safe.email;
  delete safe.phone;
  delete safe.nomorHp;
  delete safe.cvFileUrl;
  delete safe.cvExternalLink;
  delete safe.cvUrl;
  delete safe.alamat;
  delete safe.address;
  return safe;
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  // SEC.API.UNAUTHENTICATED_DATABASE_ROUTE.01: Production guard
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Endpoint disabled in production' }, { status: 404 });
  }

  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const collectionName = searchParams.get('collection') as keyof DbSchema | null;
  const id = searchParams.get('id');
  const nomorAlumni = searchParams.get('nomorAlumni');
  const authorUid = searchParams.get('authorUid');
  const posterUid = searchParams.get('posterUid');
  const applicantUid = searchParams.get('applicantUid');

  const db = loadDb();

  if (!collectionName || !(collectionName in db)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid collection' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const col = db[collectionName];
  const callerUid = getAuthenticatedUid(req);
  const caller = callerUid ? db.users[callerUid] : null;
  const isAdmin = caller?.role === 'admin';

  // 1. Applications collection protection (SEC.API.PII_EXPOSURE_USERS_APPLICATIONS.01)
  // Must reject unfiltered dumps. Query parameter posterUid or applicantUid is required.
  if (collectionName === 'applications') {
    if (!posterUid && !applicantUid) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Filter posterUid atau applicantUid wajib disertakan untuk mengakses lamaran' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (id) {
      const item = col[id] || null;
      if (!item) {
        return NextResponse.json({ item: null, doc: null });
      }
      const isAuthorized =
        (posterUid && item.posterUid === posterUid) ||
        (applicantUid && item.applicantUid === applicantUid) ||
        (callerUid && (callerUid === item.posterUid || callerUid === item.applicantUid || isAdmin));

      if (!isAuthorized) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Akses lamaran ditolak' }, { status: 403 });
      }
      return NextResponse.json({ item, doc: item });
    }

    let items = Object.values(col);
    if (posterUid && applicantUid) {
      items = items.filter((doc) => doc.posterUid === posterUid && doc.applicantUid === applicantUid);
    } else if (posterUid) {
      items = items.filter((doc) => doc.posterUid === posterUid);
    } else if (applicantUid) {
      items = items.filter((doc) => doc.applicantUid === applicantUid);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ items });
  }

  // 2. Users collection protection (SEC.API.PII_EXPOSURE_USERS_APPLICATIONS.01)
  // Reject unauthenticated unfiltered dumps; sanitize contact PII unless authenticated owner.
  if (collectionName === 'users') {
    if (id) {
      const item = col[id] || null;
      if (!item) {
        return NextResponse.json({ item: null, doc: null });
      }
      const isOwner = Boolean(callerUid && (callerUid === item.uid || callerUid === item.id || callerUid === id));
      const returnedItem = isOwner ? item : sanitizeUserProfile(item);
      return NextResponse.json({ item: returnedItem, doc: returnedItem });
    }

    if (nomorAlumni) {
      const clean = nomorAlumni.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/[/._]/g, '-').toUpperCase();
      const found = Object.values(col).find(
        (doc) => String(doc.nomorAlumni || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/[/._]/g, '-').toUpperCase() === clean
      ) || null;
      if (!found) {
        return NextResponse.json({ item: null, doc: null });
      }
      const isOwner = Boolean(callerUid && (callerUid === found.uid || callerUid === found.id));
      const returnedItem = isOwner ? found : sanitizeUserProfile(found);
      return NextResponse.json({ item: returnedItem, doc: returnedItem });
    }

    // Unfiltered retrieval of users collection:
    // Requires authorization header (rejects anonymous scrapers / unauthorized network callers)
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization');
    if (!authHeader) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized: Authentication required to query users' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const items = Object.values(col).map((doc) => {
      const isOwner = Boolean(callerUid && (callerUid === doc.uid || callerUid === doc.id));
      return isOwner ? doc : sanitizeUserProfile(doc);
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ items });
  }

  // 3. Alumni registry collection protection (prevent anonymous scraping of master records)
  if (collectionName === 'alumni_registry') {
    if (id) {
      const clean = id.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/[/._]/g, '-').toUpperCase();
      const item = col[clean] || col[id] || null;
      if (!item) {
        return NextResponse.json({ item: null, doc: null });
      }
      return NextResponse.json({ item, doc: item });
    }

    // Unfiltered retrieval requires authorization header
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization');
    if (!authHeader) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized: Authentication required to query alumni_registry' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const items = Object.values(col);
    return NextResponse.json({ items });
  }

  // 4. General collections (public_profiles, jobs, feed_posts, etc.)
  if (id) {
    const item = col[id] || null;
    return NextResponse.json({ item, doc: item });
  }

  if (nomorAlumni) {
    const clean = nomorAlumni.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/[/._]/g, '-').toUpperCase();
    const found = Object.values(col).find(
      (doc) => String(doc.nomorAlumni || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/[/._]/g, '-').toUpperCase() === clean
    ) || null;
    return NextResponse.json({ item: found, doc: found });
  }

  let items = Object.values(col);

  if (authorUid) {
    items = items.filter((doc) => doc.authorUid === authorUid);
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ items });
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  // SEC.API.UNAUTHENTICATED_DATABASE_ROUTE.01: Production guard
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Endpoint disabled in production' }, { status: 404 });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { action } = body;
    const db = loadDb();
    const callerUid = getAuthenticatedUid(req);
    const caller = callerUid ? db.users[callerUid] : null;
    const isAdmin = caller?.role === 'admin';

    if (action === 'setDoc') {
      const { collection: colName, id, data, merge } = body as {
        collection: keyof DbSchema;
        id: string;
        data: Record<string, unknown>;
        merge?: boolean;
      };
      if (!colName || !(colName in db) || !id) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing collection or id' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      // Safeguard against privilege escalation (SEC.API.UNAUTHENTICATED_DATABASE_ROUTE.01):
      // Prevent unauthorized setting of role to 'admin'
      if (data && typeof data === 'object' && 'role' in data && data.role === 'admin') {
        if (!isAdmin) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Role elevation to admin is prohibited' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      // Safeguard alumni_registry: only administrators can create or mutate master registry records
      if (colName === 'alumni_registry') {
        if (!isAdmin) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Modifying alumni registry requires administrative privileges' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      // Safeguard applications: prevent applicants or unauthenticated callers from changing status
      if (colName === 'applications') {
        const existing = db.applications[id];
        if (existing && data && typeof data === 'object' && 'status' in data && data.status !== 'pending' && data.status !== existing.status) {
          const isJobPoster = callerUid && (callerUid === existing.posterUid || isAdmin);
          if (!isJobPoster) {
            return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Only the job poster can change application status' }, { status: 403 });
          }
        }
      }

      // Safeguard users: if callerUid is present and updating an existing user, verify ownership
      if (colName === 'users') {
        if (callerUid && callerUid !== id && !isAdmin) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Cannot modify another user profile' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      const existing = db[colName][id] || {};
      const updated: Record<string, unknown> = merge ? { ...existing, ...data, id } : { ...data, id };
      db[colName][id] = updated;

      // If updating public_profiles with a nomorAlumni, remove duplicate synthetic showcase card with same nomorAlumni
      if (colName === 'public_profiles') {
        const userNomor = String(updated.nomorAlumni || db.users[id]?.nomorAlumni || '').toUpperCase();
        if (userNomor) {
          updated.nomorAlumni = userNomor;
          for (const key of Object.keys(db.public_profiles)) {
            if (key !== id && key.startsWith('showcase-') && String(db.public_profiles[key]?.nomorAlumni || '').toUpperCase() === userNomor) {
              delete db.public_profiles[key];
            }
          }
        }
      }

      saveDb(db);
      return NextResponse.json({ item: updated });
    }

    if (action === 'addDoc') {
      const { collection: colName, data, customId } = body as {
        collection: keyof DbSchema;
        data: Record<string, unknown>;
        customId?: string;
      };
      if (!colName || !(colName in db)) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid collection' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      // Safeguard against privilege escalation
      if (data && typeof data === 'object' && 'role' in data && data.role === 'admin') {
        if (!isAdmin) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Role elevation to admin is prohibited' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      // Safeguard alumni_registry
      if (colName === 'alumni_registry') {
        if (!isAdmin) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Adding to alumni registry requires administrative privileges' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      const newId = customId || `${colName.replace(/s$/, '')}_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`;
      const item = { ...data, id: newId };
      db[colName][newId] = item;
      saveDb(db);
      return NextResponse.json({ item, id: newId });
    }

    if (action === 'deleteDoc') {
      const { collection: colName, id } = body as {
        collection: keyof DbSchema;
        id: string;
      };
      if (!colName || !(colName in db) || !id) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing collection or id' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      // Safeguard users collection from arbitrary deletion
      if (colName === 'users') {
        if (!isAdmin) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Cannot delete user records' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      // Safeguard alumni_registry
      if (colName === 'alumni_registry') {
        if (!isAdmin) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Cannot delete alumni registry records' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      // Safeguard jobs collection: only poster or admin can delete
      if (colName === 'jobs') {
        const job = db.jobs[id];
        if (!isAdmin && (!job || !callerUid || callerUid !== job.postedBy)) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Only the job poster or admin can delete this job' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      // Safeguard applications: only applicant, poster, or admin
      if (colName === 'applications') {
        const app = db.applications[id];
        if (!isAdmin && (!app || !callerUid || (callerUid !== app.applicantUid && callerUid !== app.posterUid))) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Cannot delete application' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      // Safeguard feed_posts: only author or admin
      if (colName === 'feed_posts') {
        const post = db.feed_posts[id];
        if (!isAdmin && (!post || !callerUid || callerUid !== post.authorUid)) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden: Only the author or admin can delete this post' }, { status: 403 });
        }
      }

      delete db[colName][id];
      saveDb(db);
      return NextResponse.json({ deleted: true });
    }

    if (action === 'registerAlumni') {
      const { uid, email, nomorAlumni, profileData, publicProfileData } = body as {
        uid: string;
        email: string;
        nomorAlumni?: string;
        profileData?: Record<string, unknown>;
        publicProfileData?: Record<string, unknown>;
      };

      if (!nomorAlumni || !uid || !profileData) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Parameter registrasi tidak lengkap' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const cleanNomor = nomorAlumni.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/[/._]/g, '-').toUpperCase();
      const registryEntry = db.alumni_registry[cleanNomor];

      // SEC.AUTH.REGISTRY_BYPASS_SERVER_DB.01:
      // Do NOT auto-create arbitrary unregistered student numbers.
      // Check that cleanNomor already exists in db.alumni_registry and isRegistered !== true.
      if (!registryEntry) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Nomor alumni tidak terdaftar dalam database institusi' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      if (registryEntry.isRegistered === true) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Nomor alumni ini sudah terdaftar sebelumnya' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const now = new Date().toISOString();

      db.alumni_registry[cleanNomor] = {
        ...registryEntry,
        nomorAlumni: cleanNomor,
        namaLengkap: profileData.namaLengkap || registryEntry.namaLengkap,
        prodi: registryEntry.prodi || profileData.prodi,
        angkatan: registryEntry.angkatan || profileData.angkatan,
        isRegistered: true,
        registeredUid: uid,
        registeredEmail: email,
        registeredAt: now
      };

      db.users[uid] = { ...profileData, uid, id: uid };
      db.public_profiles[uid] = { ...(publicProfileData || {}), uid, id: uid, nomorAlumni: cleanNomor };

      // Deduplicate synthetic showcase profile if same nomorAlumni
      for (const key of Object.keys(db.public_profiles)) {
        if (key !== uid && key.startsWith('showcase-') && String(db.public_profiles[key]?.nomorAlumni || '').toUpperCase() === cleanNomor) {
          delete db.public_profiles[key];
        }
      }

      saveDb(db);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, profile: db.users[uid] });
    }

    if (action === 'toggleLike') {
      const { postId, userUid } = body as { postId: string; userUid: string };
      const post = db.feed_posts[postId];
      if (!post) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Post not found' }, { status: 404 });
      }
      const likedBy = Array.isArray(post.likedBy) ? [...(post.likedBy as string[])] : [];
      const alreadyLiked = likedBy.includes(userUid);
      const updatedLikedBy = alreadyLiked
        ? likedBy.filter((u) => u !== userUid)
        : [...likedBy, userUid];
      const currentLikes = Number(post.likesCount || 0);
      const updatedLikes = alreadyLiked ? Math.max(0, currentLikes - 1) : currentLikes + 1;

      post.likedBy = updatedLikedBy;
      post.likesCount = updatedLikes;
      post.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      db.feed_posts[postId] = post;
      saveDb(db);
      return NextResponse.json({ item: post });
    }

    if (action === 'addComment') {
      const { postId, comment } = body as { postId: string; comment: Record<string, unknown> };
      const post = db.feed_posts[postId];
      if (!post) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Post not found' }, { status: 404 });
      }
      const comments = Array.isArray(post.comments) ? [...(post.comments as Record<string, unknown>[])] : [];
      comments.push(comment);
      post.comments = comments;
      post.commentsCount = comments.length;
      post.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      db.feed_posts[postId] = post;
      saveDb(db);
      return NextResponse.json({ item: post, comment });
    }

    if (action === 'uploadFile') {
      // SEC.API.UPLOAD_FILE_UNDEFINED_BASE64_CRASH.01:
      // Handle both dataUrl and base64Data parameter aliases and defensively verify string presence
      const rawData = body.base64Data || body.dataUrl;
      if (!rawData || typeof rawData !== 'string' || rawData.trim() === '') {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Data berkas tidak valid atau kosong' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      // SEC.UPLOAD.UNRESTRICTED_FILE_WRITE_XSS.01:
      // Strictly validate file extensions against a safe whitelist (reject .html, .svg, .js, etc.)
      const rawFileName = typeof body.fileName === 'string' ? body.fileName : 'file';
      const extMatch = rawFileName.match(/\.([a-zA-Z0-9]+)$/);
      const ext = extMatch ? extMatch[1].toLowerCase() : '';
      const ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'webp', 'pdf'];

      if (!ext || !ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS.includes(ext)) {
        return NextResponse.json({
          error: `Ekstensi berkas .${ext || 'unknown'} tidak diizinkan. Hanya format berikut yang diperbolehkan: ${ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS.join(', ')}`
        }, { status: 400 });
      }

      // Check MIME type in dataUrl if provided
      if (rawData.startsWith('data:')) {
        const mimeMatch = rawData.match(/^data:([a-zA-Z0-9.+_-]+\/[a-zA-Z0-9.+_-]+);base64,/);
        if (mimeMatch) {
          const mime = mimeMatch[1].toLowerCase();
          const ALLOWED_MIMES = [
            'image/jpeg',
            'image/jpg',
            'image/png',
            'image/webp',
            'application/pdf'
          ];
          if (!ALLOWED_MIMES.includes(mime)) {
            return NextResponse.json({
              error: `Tipe MIME ${mime} tidak diizinkan demi keamanan`
            }, { status: 400 });
          }
        }
      }

      const base64Clean = rawData.includes(',') ? rawData.split(',')[1] : rawData;
      let buffer: Buffer;
      try {
        buffer = Buffer.from(base64Clean, 'base64');
      } catch {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Gagal menguraikan konten berkas base64' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      if (buffer.length === 0) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Berkas kosong (0 bytes)' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      // Enforce file size bounds (max 10MB)
      const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 10 * 1024 * 1024; // 10MB
      if (buffer.length > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ukuran file melebihi batas maksimal 10MB' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      // Magic byte verification and XML/HTML/SVG inspection
      const headerSnippet = buffer.subarray(0, Math.min(buffer.length, 512)).toString('utf8').toLowerCase();
      if (
        headerSnippet.includes('<svg') ||
        headerSnippet.includes('<?xml') ||
        headerSnippet.includes('<!doctype') ||
        headerSnippet.includes('<html') ||
        headerSnippet.includes('<script')
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Konten berkas terindikasi format XML/HTML/SVG yang tidak diizinkan demi keamanan' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      // Validate magic bytes for format integrity
      if (ext === 'png') {
        // PNG magic bytes: 0x89 0x50 0x4E 0x47
        if (buffer.length < 4 || buffer[0] !== 0x89 || buffer[1] !== 0x50 || buffer[2] !== 0x4e || buffer[3] !== 0x47) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Format berkas PNG tidak valid (magic bytes mismatch)' }, { status: 400 });
        }
      } else if (ext === 'jpg' || ext === 'jpeg') {
        // JPEG magic bytes: 0xFF 0xD8
        if (buffer.length < 2 || buffer[0] !== 0xff || buffer[1] !== 0xd8) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Format berkas JPEG tidak valid (magic bytes mismatch)' }, { status: 400 });
        }
      } else if (ext === 'pdf') {
        // PDF magic bytes: %PDF- (0x25 0x50 0x44 0x46)
        if (buffer.length < 4 || buffer[0] !== 0x25 || buffer[1] !== 0x50 || buffer[2] !== 0x44 || buffer[3] !== 0x46) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Format berkas PDF tidak valid (magic bytes mismatch)' }, { status: 400 });
        }
      }

      const targetFolder = (typeof body.folder === 'string' ? body.folder.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '') : '') || 'media';
      const targetDir = path.join(UPLOADS_DIR, targetFolder);
      if (!fs.existsSync(targetDir)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(targetDir, { recursive: true });
      }

      const rawBase = path.basename(rawFileName, path.extname(rawFileName)).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
      const cleanName = `${Date.now()}_${rawBase || 'upload'}.${ext}`;
      const filePath = path.join(targetDir, cleanName);
      fs.writeFileSync(filePath, buffer);
      const publicUrl = `/uploads/${targetFolder}/${cleanName}`;
      return NextResponse.json({ url: publicUrl });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unknown action' }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const e = err as Error;
    console.error('Error in POST /api/db:', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: e.message || 'Server error' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export interface FeedPostUI {

}

function formatTimeAgo(isoString?: string): string {
  if (!isoString) return 'Baru saja';
  const date = new Date(isoString);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return isoString;
  const now = new Date();
  const diffMs = now.getTime() - date.getTime();
  const diffSec = Math.floor(diffMs / 1000);
  if (diffSec < 60) return 'Baru saja';
  const diffMin = Math.floor(diffSec / 60);
  if (diffMin < 60) return `${diffMin} menit yang lalu`;
  const diffHours = Math.floor(diffMin / 60);
  if (diffHours < 24) return `${diffHours} jam yang lalu`;
  const diffDays = Math.floor(diffHours / 24);
  if (diffDays < 30) return `${diffDays} hari yang lalu`;
  return date.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
}

const DEFAULT_POSTS: FeedPostUI[] = INITIAL_CURATED_POSTS.map(p => ({
  ...p,
  timeAgo: formatTimeAgo(p.createdAt),
  isLiked: false,
  isFollowing: false,
  comments: (p.comments || []).map(c => ({
    ...c,
    timeAgo: formatTimeAgo(c.createdAt)
  }))
}));

export default function FeedPage() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();

  // Simulated logged-in user profile
  const currentUser = {
    uid: user?.uid || '',
    name: profile?.namaLengkap || user?.displayName || (user ? 'Alumni STMM' : 'Pengunjung Tamu'),
    prodi: profile?.prodi || 'Animasi',
    angkatan: profile?.angkatan || '2020',
    role: (profile as ExtendedUserProfile | null)?.pekerjaanSaatIni || (user ? 'Alumni STMM MMTC' : 'Pengunjung Tamu'),
    avatar: profile?.fotoProfil || user?.photoURL || ''
  };

  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<FeedPostUI[]>(DEFAULT_POSTS);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsub = subscribeToFeedPosts((fetched) => {
      if (fetched && fetched.length > 0) {
        const mapped: FeedPostUI[] = fetched.map(p => ({
          ...p,
          timeAgo: formatTimeAgo(p.createdAt),
          isLiked: user ? (p.likedBy?.includes(user.uid) || false) : false,
          comments: (p.comments || []).map(c => ({
            ...c,
            timeAgo: formatTimeAgo(c.createdAt)
          }))
        }));
        setPosts(mapped);
      }
    });
    return () => unsub();
  }, [user]);
  const [selectedFilter, setSelectedFilter] = useState<string>('semua');
  
  // Post Creator State
  const [newContent, setNewContent] = useState('');
  const [newCategory, setNewCategory] = useState<'showreel' | 'kolaborasi' | 'diskusi' | 'pencapaian'>('diskusi');
  const [newMediaUrl, setNewMediaUrl] = useState('');
  const [newMediaType, setNewMediaType] = useState<'youtube' | 'media' | 'none'>('none');
  const [attachedFile, setAttachedFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [attachedPreview, setAttachedPreview] = useState<string>('');
  const [attachedFileType, setAttachedFileType] = useState<'image' | 'pdf'>('image');
  const [isPosting, setIsPosting] = useState(false);
  const [postAlert, setPostAlert] = useState('');

  // Active commenting map: postId -> comment text
  const [commentInputs, setCommentInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [expandedComments, setExpandedComments] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
    'post-1': true
  });

  // Modals state
  const [cinemaVideo, setCinemaVideo] = useState<{ videoId: string; title: string } | null>(null);
  const [shareData, setShareData] = useState<{ title: string; subtitle: string; url: string } | null>(null);
  const [followedAlumni, setFollowedAlumni] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
    'post-2': true
  });
  const [connectedUsers, setConnectedUsers] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  // Custom Category Dropdown State
  const [categoryDropdownOpen, setCategoryDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const categoryDropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
      if (categoryDropdownRef.current && !categoryDropdownRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setCategoryDropdownOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const handleFileAttachment = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setAttachedFile(file);
    const isPdf = file.type === 'application/pdf' || file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf');
    setAttachedFileType(isPdf ? 'pdf' : 'image');
    setAttachedPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
  };

  const handleRemoveAttachment = () => {
    setAttachedFile(null);
    setAttachedPreview('');
    setNewMediaUrl('');
  };

  // Handlers
  const handleCreatePost = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newContent.trim()) return;

    if (!user) {
      setPostAlert('Silakan masuk dengan akun alumni untuk menerbitkan kiriman.');
      setTimeout(() => setPostAlert(''), 4000);
      return;
    }

    setIsPosting(true);

    try {
      let finalMediaType: 'youtube' | 'image' | 'pdf' | undefined = undefined;
      let finalMediaUrl: string | undefined = undefined;
      let finalMediaTitle: string | undefined = undefined;
      let finalMediaSize: string | undefined = undefined;

      if (newMediaType === 'youtube' && newMediaUrl.trim()) {
        finalMediaType = 'youtube';
        finalMediaUrl = newMediaUrl.trim();
        finalMediaTitle = 'Video Showreel';
      } else if (newMediaType === 'media') {
        if (attachedFile) {
          finalMediaType = attachedFileType;
          finalMediaTitle = attachedFile.name;
          finalMediaSize = `${(attachedFile.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
        } else if (newMediaUrl.trim()) {
          const isPdf = newMediaUrl.toLowerCase().includes('.pdf');
          finalMediaType = isPdf ? 'pdf' : 'image';
          finalMediaUrl = newMediaUrl.trim();
          finalMediaTitle = isPdf ? 'Dokumen PDF Alumni' : 'Media Foto';
        }
      }

      const created = await createFeedPost({
        authorUid: user.uid,
        authorName: currentUser.name,
        authorProdi: currentUser.prodi,
        authorAngkatan: currentUser.angkatan,
        authorRole: currentUser.role,
        authorAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
        nomorAlumni: profile?.nomorAlumni || 'MMTC-2020-001',
        category: newCategory,
        content: newContent.trim(),
        hashtags: [`#AlumniMMTC`, `#${currentUser.prodi}MMTC`],
        mediaType: finalMediaType,
        mediaUrl: finalMediaUrl,
        mediaTitle: finalMediaTitle,
        mediaSize: finalMediaSize
      }, attachedFile);

      const optimisticPost: FeedPostUI = {
        ...created,
        timeAgo: 'Baru saja',
        isLiked: false,
        isFollowing: false,
        comments: []
      };

      setPosts(prev => [optimisticPost, ...prev.filter(p => p.id !== optimisticPost.id)]);

      // Save to localStorage so profile history immediately shows this post
      try {
        const stored = localStorage.getItem('alumni_user_posts');
        const existing = stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : [];
        localStorage.setItem('alumni_user_posts', JSON.stringify([optimisticPost, ...existing]));
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }

      setNewContent('');
      setNewMediaUrl('');
      setAttachedFile(null);
      setAttachedPreview('');
      setNewMediaType('none');
      setPostAlert('Kiriman berhasil tayang di beranda komunitas.');
      setTimeout(() => setPostAlert(''), 4000);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Terjadi kesalahan sistem';
      console.error('Error creating post:', err);
      setPostAlert(`Gagal menerbitkan kiriman: ${msg}`);
      setTimeout(() => setPostAlert(''), 4000);
    } finally {
      setIsPosting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleLike = async (postId: string) => {
    if (!user) {
      setPostAlert('Silakan masuk dengan akun alumni untuk menyukai kiriman.');
      setTimeout(() => setPostAlert(''), 3000);
      return;
    }

    const post = posts.find(p => p.id === postId);
    if (!post) return;
    const isCurrentlyLiked = post.isLiked ?? post.likedBy?.includes(user.uid) ?? false;

    setPosts(prev => prev.map(p => {
      if (p.id === postId) {
        const newLiked = !isCurrentlyLiked;
        const newLikedBy = newLiked
          ? [...(p.likedBy || []), user.uid]
