          : (p.likedBy || []).filter(uid => uid !== user.uid);
        return {
          ...p,
          isLiked: newLiked,
          likedBy: newLikedBy,
          likesCount: newLiked ? p.likesCount + 1 : Math.max(0, p.likesCount - 1)
        };
      }
      return p;
    }));

    try {
      await toggleLikePost(postId, user.uid, isCurrentlyLiked);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Like toggle sync error:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleAddComment = async (postId: string) => {
    if (!user) {
      setPostAlert('Silakan masuk dengan akun alumni untuk ikut berdiskusi.');
      setTimeout(() => setPostAlert(''), 3000);
      return;
    }

    const text = commentInputs[postId]?.trim();
    if (!text) return;

    const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
    const optimisticComment = {
      id: `c-${Date.now()}`,
      authorUid: user.uid,
      authorName: currentUser.name,
      authorProdi: currentUser.prodi,
      authorAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
      text,
      timeAgo: 'Baru saja',
      createdAt: nowIso
    };

    setPosts(prev => prev.map(p => {
      if (p.id === postId) {
        return {
          ...p,
          commentsCount: (p.commentsCount || p.comments.length) + 1,
          comments: [...p.comments, optimisticComment]
        };
      }
      return p;
    }));

    setCommentInputs(prev => ({ ...prev, [postId]: '' }));
    setExpandedComments(prev => ({ ...prev, [postId]: true }));

    try {
      await addPostComment(postId, {
        authorUid: user.uid,
        authorName: currentUser.name,
        authorProdi: currentUser.prodi,
        authorAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
        text
      });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Terjadi kesalahan sistem';
      console.error('Error adding comment:', err);
      setPostAlert(`Gagal mengirim komentar: ${msg}`);
      setTimeout(() => setPostAlert(''), 3000);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleFollow = (authorKey: string) => {
    if (!user) {
      setPostAlert('Silakan masuk dengan akun alumni untuk mulai mengikuti.');
      setTimeout(() => setPostAlert(''), 3000);
      return;
    }
    setFollowedAlumni(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [authorKey]: !prev[authorKey]
    }));
  };

  const handleToggleConnect = (userId: string) => {
    if (!user) {
      setPostAlert('Silakan masuk dengan akun alumni untuk mulai terhubung.');
      setTimeout(() => setPostAlert(''), 3000);
      return;
    }
    setConnectedUsers(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [userId]: !prev[userId]
    }));
  };

  const filteredPosts = posts.filter(post => {
    if (selectedFilter === 'semua') return true;
    return post.category === selectedFilter;
  });

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6 sm:py-8">
        
        {/* BANNER PREVIEW / GREETING */}
        <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-50 via-white to-sky-50/50 border border-sky-100 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-2xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg shadow-xs shrink-0">
              <Users className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                Ruang Komunitas Alumni MMTC
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600">
                Bagikan progres produksi, rilis showreel terbaru, atau cari kru lintas angkatan.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            <Link
              href="/open-to-work"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition shadow-2xs"
            >
              <Briefcase className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
              Katalog Talenta
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 3-COLUMN LAYOUT (LINKEDIN / X STYLE) */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-start">
          
          {/* ============================================================== */}
          {/* LEFT SIDEBAR: PROFILE SUMMARY & CATEGORY NAV (COL-SPAN 3)       */}
          {/* ============================================================== */}
          <div className="hidden lg:block lg:col-span-3 space-y-5 sticky top-24">
            
            {/* MINI PROFILE CARD */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-2xs text-center space-y-4">
              {user ? (
                <>
                  <div className="relative mx-auto w-18 h-18 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-white shadow-xs bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center text-white text-2xl font-bold">
                    <AlumniAvatar src={currentUser.avatar} name={currentUser.name} />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{currentUser.name}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#0284c7] font-semibold mt-0.5">
                      {currentUser.prodi} • Angkatan {currentUser.angkatan}
                    </p>
                    {currentUser.role && (
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 font-medium mt-1.5 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                        <Briefcase className="w-3 h-3 text-[#0284c7] shrink-0" />
                        <span className="truncate">{currentUser.role}</span>
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <OpenToWorkBadge />
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
                    <Link
                      href="/profil"
                      className="w-full py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-sky-50 hover:text-[#0284c7] border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition"
                    >
                      Edit Profil Saya
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <div className="w-14 h-14 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xl shadow-xs">
                    <Users className="w-7 h-7" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Portal Alumni MMTC</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                      Masuk untuk mempublikasikan showreel, memberi tanggapan, dan terhubung dengan sesama alumni.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="pt-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
                    <Link
                      href="/login"
                      className="w-full py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold transition shadow-xs"
                    >
                      Masuk Akun
                    </Link>
                    <Link
                      href="/register"
                      className="w-full py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold transition"
                    >
                      Daftar Akun Baru
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* CATEGORY FILTER NAVIGATION */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-2xs space-y-1 text-xs">
              <h4 className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 px-3 py-1">
                Topik Kiriman
              </h4>
              
              {[
                { id: 'semua', label: 'Semua Kiriman', icon: LayoutGrid },
                { id: 'showreel', label: 'Karya & Showreel', icon: Video },
                { id: 'kolaborasi', label: 'Kolaborasi & Kru', icon: Users },
                { id: 'pencapaian', label: 'Kabar & Capaian', icon: Award },
                { id: 'diskusi', label: 'Obrolan & Diskusi', icon: MessageSquare },
              ].map(cat => {
                const IconComponent = cat.icon;
                const active = selectedFilter === cat.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={cat.id}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedFilter(cat.id)}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl font-semibold transition cursor-pointer text-left ${
                      active
                        ? 'bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] font-bold shadow-2xs'
                        : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <IconComponent className={`w-4 h-4 ${active ? 'text-[#0284c7]' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
                    <span>{cat.label}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {/* POPULAR HASHTAGS */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-2xs space-y-2.5 text-xs">
              <h4 className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 px-2 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Hash className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                Topik Hangat MMTC
              </h4>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {TRENDING_TOPICS.map(item => (
                  <div key={item.tag} className="flex items-center justify-between px-2 py-1 hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-lg transition">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:text-[#0284c7] cursor-pointer">
                      {item.tag}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">{item.count}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* ============================================================== */}
          {/* CENTER COLUMN: MAIN FEED & POST CREATOR (COL-SPAN 6)            */}
          {/* ============================================================== */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-6">
            
            {/* POST CREATOR CARD / VISITOR BANNER */}
            {!user ? (
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-2xs flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <Users className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                      Ikut Berbagi Karya &amp; Diskusi
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 mt-0.5">
                      Masuk dengan akun alumni untuk mengunggah showreel, mencari kru produksi, atau mengomentari kiriman rekan.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 w-full sm:w-auto">
                  <Link
                    href="/login"
                    className="flex-1 sm:flex-initial text-center px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold transition shadow-xs"
                  >
                    Masuk Akun
                  </Link>
                  <Link
                    href="/register"
                    className="flex-1 sm:flex-initial text-center px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold transition shadow-2xs"
                  >
                    Daftar
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-2xs space-y-3.5">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shrink-0 bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                    <AlumniAvatar src={currentUser.avatar} name={currentUser.name} />
                  </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    value={newContent}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewContent(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Bagikan karya showreel, update syuting, atau ajak kolaborasi..."
                    className="w-full p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition resize-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* MEDIA ATTACHMENT INPUT (IF TOGGLED) */}
              {newMediaType === 'youtube' && (
                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-red-50/60 border border-red-100 space-y-2">
                  <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 block">
                    Tautan Video YouTube (Showreel)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={newMediaUrl}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewMediaUrl(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=..."
                    className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-red-400"
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              {newMediaType === 'media' && (
                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-sky-50/60 border border-sky-100 space-y-2.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Paperclip className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                      Lampirkan Media (Foto Gambar atau Dokumen PDF)
                    </label>
                    {attachedFile && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleRemoveAttachment}
                        className="text-[11px] text-red-600 hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <X className="w-3 h-3" /> Hapus
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {attachedFile ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-sky-200">
                      {attachedFileType === 'pdf' ? (
                        <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-red-100 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                          <FileText className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <img
                          src={attachedPreview}
                          alt="Preview"
                          className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg object-cover shrink-0 border border-slate-200"
                        />
                      )}
                      <div className="flex-1 truncate">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 truncate">{attachedFile.name}</p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                          {attachedFileType === 'pdf' ? 'Dokumen PDF' : 'Gambar / Foto'} • {(attachedFile.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} MB
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <label className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-sky-300 bg-white hover:bg-sky-50/50 cursor-pointer transition">
                        <UploadCloud className="w-6 h-6 text-[#0284c7] mb-1" />
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Pilih Berkas Foto atau Dokumen PDF</span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Mendukung file Gambar (JPG, PNG, WEBP) atau Dokumen PDF</span>
                        <input
                          type="file"
                          accept="image/*,application/pdf,.pdf"
                          onChange={handleFileAttachment}
                          className="hidden"
                        />
                      </label>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap">atau URL:</span>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={newMediaUrl}
                          onChange={(e) => setNewMediaUrl(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="Paste link gambar atau link PDF..."
                          className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7]"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* ACTION TOOLBAR */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setNewMediaType(newMediaType === 'youtube' ? 'none' : 'youtube')}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer ${
                      newMediaType === 'youtube'
                        ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600 border border-red-200'
                        : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" />
                    <span>Video / Showreel</span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setNewMediaType(newMediaType === 'media' ? 'none' : 'media')}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer ${
                      newMediaType === 'media'
                        ? 'bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] border border-sky-200'
                        : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Paperclip className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                    <span>Media</span>
                  </button>

                  {/* CUSTOM STYLED CATEGORY DROPDOWN */}
                  <div className="relative" ref={categoryDropdownRef}>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setCategoryDropdownOpen(!categoryDropdownOpen)}
                      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition cursor-pointer select-none ${
                        categoryDropdownOpen
                          ? 'bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] border-sky-300 shadow-2xs'
                          : 'bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-200'
                      }`}
                      aria-expanded={categoryDropdownOpen}
                      aria-haspopup="listbox"
                    >
                      <span className="text-[#0284c7]">
                        {(() => {
                          const ActiveIcon = POST_CATEGORIES.find(c => c.id === newCategory)?.icon || MessageSquare;
                          return <ActiveIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />;
                        })()}
                      </span>
                      <span>
                        {POST_CATEGORIES.find(c => c.id === newCategory)?.label || 'Diskusi'}
                      </span>
                      <ChevronDown 
                        className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-200 ${
                          categoryDropdownOpen ? 'rotate-180 text-[#0284c7]' : ''
                        }`} 
                      />
                    </button>

                    {categoryDropdownOpen && (
                      <div className="absolute left-0 mt-2 w-56 sm:w-64 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-slate-200/90 p-1.5 z-30 animate-fade-in">
                        <div className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                          Pilih Kategori Postingan
                        </div>
                        <div className="space-y-1">
                          {POST_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => {
                            const isSelected = newCategory === cat.id;
                            return (
                              <button
                                key={cat.id}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => {
                                  setNewCategory(cat.id);
                                  setCategoryDropdownOpen(false);
                                }}
                                className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl text-left transition cursor-pointer ${
                                  isSelected
                                    ? 'bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] font-bold'
                                    : 'hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700'
                                }`}
                              >
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                                  <span className="flex items-center justify-center w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-[#0284c7] shrink-0">
                                    {(() => {
                                      const ItemIcon = cat.icon;
                                      return <ItemIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />;
                                    })()}
                                  </span>
                                  <div>
                                    <p className="text-xs font-bold leading-tight">{cat.label}</p>
                                    <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 leading-tight mt-0.5">{cat.desc}</p>
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                                {isSelected && (
                                  <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7] shrink-0" />
                                )}
                              </button>
                            );
                          })}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleCreatePost}
                  disabled={!newContent.trim() || isPosting}
                  className="px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] disabled:opacity-40 text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-2xs cursor-pointer active:scale-95"
                >
                  <Send className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Publikasikan</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              {postAlert && (
                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs font-medium animate-in fade-in">
                  {postAlert}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

            {/* FEED FILTER TABS (MOBILE & TABLET VISIBLE) */}
            <div className="flex lg:hidden items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 scrollbar-none">
              {['semua', 'showreel', 'kolaborasi', 'pencapaian', 'diskusi'].map(f => (
                <button
                  key={f}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedFilter(f)}
                  className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold whitespace-nowrap transition cursor-pointer capitalize ${
                    selectedFilter === f
                      ? 'bg-[#0284c7] text-white shadow-2xs'
                      : 'bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  {f}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* FEED LIST */}
            <div className="space-y-5">
              {filteredPosts.map(post => {
                const isPostLiked = post.isLiked;
                const isFollowingPostAuthor = followedAlumni[post.id] ?? post.isFollowing;
                const commentsOpen = Boolean(expandedComments[post.id]);
                const videoId = post.mediaUrl ? parseYouTubeVideoId(post.mediaUrl) : null;

                return (
                  <article
                    key={post.id}
                    className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-2xs space-y-4 transition hover:border-slate-300"
                  >
                    {/* POST HEADER: AUTHOR INFO & CONNECT/FOLLOW BUTTON */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <Link href={`/profil/${encodeURIComponent(post.authorUid || post.nomorAlumni || '')}`} className="shrink-0">
                          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-base shadow-2xs hover:opacity-90 transition">
                            <AlumniAvatar src={post.authorAvatar} name={post.authorName} />
                          </div>
                        </Link>
                        <div>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                            <Link 
                              href={`/profil/${encodeURIComponent(post.authorUid || post.nomorAlumni || '')}`}
                              className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 hover:text-[#0284c7] transition"
                            >
                              {post.authorName}
                            </Link>
                            <ProdiBadge prodi={post.authorProdi} />
                          </div>
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                            {post.authorRole} • Angkatan {post.authorAngkatan}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                            {post.timeAgo}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* CONNECT / FOLLOW BUTTON */}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleToggleFollow(post.id)}
                        className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer ${
                          isFollowingPostAuthor
                            ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
                            : 'bg-sky-50 hover:bg-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] border border-sky-200/70'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {isFollowingPostAuthor ? (
                          <>
                            <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />
                            <span>Mengikuti</span>
                          </>
                        ) : (
                          <>
                            <UserPlus className="w-3 h-3" />
                            <span>+ Ikuti</span>
                          </>
                        )}
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {/* POST CONTENT TEXT */}
                    <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                      {post.content}
                    </p>

                    {/* HASHTAGS */}
                    {post.hashtags && post.hashtags.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-1">
                        {post.hashtags.map(tag => (
                          <span
                            key={tag}
                            className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#0284c7] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            {tag}
                          </span>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* MEDIA EMBED (YOUTUBE / IMAGE) */}
                    {post.mediaType === 'youtube' && videoId && (
                      <div className="space-y-2 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-900 border border-slate-200">
                        <div className="relative aspect-video w-full bg-black">
                          <iframe
                            src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${videoId}`}
                            className="w-full h-full border-0"
                            title={post.mediaTitle || 'Showreel Video'}
                            allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                            allowFullScreen
                          />
                        </div>
                        <div className="p-3 bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className="text-xs font-semibold truncate pr-3">{post.mediaTitle}</span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setCinemaVideo({ videoId, title: post.mediaTitle || 'Showreel Alumni' })}
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-sky-400 text-[11px] font-semibold transition shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            <Maximize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span>Mode Bioskop</span>
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {post.mediaType === 'image' && post.mediaUrl && (
                      <div className="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 max-h-80 bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <img 
                          src={post.mediaUrl} 
                          alt={post.mediaTitle || 'Foto Postingan'}
                          className="w-full h-full max-h-80 object-cover"
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {post.mediaType === 'pdf' && (
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-sky-50/60 border border-slate-200 hover:border-sky-200 transition group">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 truncate pr-3">
                          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-red-100 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-2xs">
                            <FileText className="w-6 h-6" />
                          </div>
                          <div className="truncate text-left">
                            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0284c7] transition truncate">
                              {post.mediaTitle || 'Dokumen PDF Alumni'}
                            </p>
                            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                              Dokumen PDF • {post.mediaSize || 'Berkas Dokumen'}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        {(() => {
                          const rawUrl = (post.mediaUrl || '').trim();
                          const isSafeRelative = rawUrl.startsWith('/') && !rawUrl.startsWith('//') && !rawUrl.startsWith('/\\');
                          const safeUrl = isSafeRelative ? rawUrl : (safeHttpUrl(rawUrl) || '#');
                          const isSafe = safeUrl !== '#';

                          return (
                            <a
                              href={safeUrl}
                              target={isSafe ? '_blank' : undefined}
                              rel="noopener noreferrer"
                              onClick={!isSafe ? (e) => e.preventDefault() : undefined}
                              className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-sky-300 text-xs font-bold text-[#0284c7] shadow-2xs transition inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"
                            >
                              <span>Buka PDF</span>
                              <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            </a>
                          );
                        })()}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* ACTION TOOLBAR: LIKE, COMMENT, SHARE */}
                    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-500">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                        {/* LIKE BUTTON */}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleToggleLike(post.id)}
                          className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 py-1 px-2 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${
                            isPostLiked
                              ? 'text-rose-600 font-bold bg-rose-50'
                              : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-slate-50'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <Heart className={`w-4 h-4 ${isPostLiked ? 'fill-rose-500 text-rose-500' : ''}`} />
                          <span>{post.likesCount} Suka</span>
                        </button>

                        {/* COMMENT BUTTON */}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setExpandedComments(prev => ({ ...prev, [post.id]: !commentsOpen }))}
                          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 py-1 px-2 rounded-lg text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0284c7] hover:bg-slate-50 transition cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4" />
                          <span>{post.comments.length} Komentar</span>
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      {/* SHARE BUTTON */}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setShareData({
                          title: `Postingan dari ${post.authorName} (Alumni STMM MMTC)`,
                          subtitle: post.content.slice(0, 100) + '...',
                          url: typeof window !== 'undefined' ? `${window.location.origin}/feed#${post.id}` : ''
                        })}
                        className="flex items-center gap-1.5 py-1 px-2 rounded-lg text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0284c7] hover:bg-slate-50 transition cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <Share2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                        <span>Bagikan</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {/* EXPANDABLE COMMENTS SECTION */}
                    {commentsOpen && (
                      <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-3 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
                        {/* LIST COMMENTS */}
                        {post.comments.map(c => (
                          <div key={c.id} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-2xl">
                            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[10px] text-slate-600 shrink-0">
                              {c.authorName.charAt(0)}
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex-1 space-y-0.5">
                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">{c.authorName}</span>
                                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">{c.timeAgo}</span>
                              </div>
                              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{c.text}</p>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}

                        {/* INPUT ADD COMMENT */}
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            value={commentInputs[post.id] || ''}
                            onChange={(e) => setCommentInputs({ ...commentInputs, [post.id]: e.target.value })}
                            onKeyDown={(e) => {
                              if (e.key === 'Enter') handleAddComment(post.id);
                            }}
                            placeholder="Tulis tanggapan atau apresiasi..."
                            className="flex-1 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                          />
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleAddComment(post.id)}
                            disabled={!commentInputs[post.id]?.trim()}
                            className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] disabled:opacity-40 text-white transition cursor-pointer"
                            title="Kirim Komentar"
                          >
                            <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </article>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* ============================================================== */}
          {/* RIGHT SIDEBAR: REKOMENDASI ALUMNI & EVENTS (COL-SPAN 3)         */}
          {/* ============================================================== */}
          <div className="hidden lg:block lg:col-span-3 space-y-5 sticky top-24">
            
            {/* PEOPLE YOU MAY KNOW / SUGGESTED CONNECTIONS */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-2xs space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                  Rekomendasi Alumni
                </h3>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3.5">
                {SUGGESTED_CONNECTIONS.map(person => {
                  const isConnected = connectedUsers[person.id];
                  return (
                    <div key={person.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 truncate">
                        <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0">
                          {person.name.charAt(0)}
                        </div>
                        <div className="truncate">
                          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 truncate">{person.name}</p>
                          <p className="text-[10px] text-[#0284c7] font-medium">{person.prodi} &apos;{person.angkatan.slice(2)}</p>
                          <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate">{person.role}</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleToggleConnect(person.id)}
                        className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold transition shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                          isConnected
                            ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200'
                            : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-[#0284c7] hover:text-white text-slate-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {isConnected ? 'Terhubung' : '+ Connect'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
                <Link
                  href="/open-to-work"
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0284c7] hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                >
                  Lihat Semua Talenta Alumni ↗
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* UPCOMING MMTC EVENTS & WORKSHOPS */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-2xs space-y-3.5">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                Agenda &amp; Event Kampus
              </h3>

              <div className="space-y-3 text-xs">
                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-[#0284c7] font-bold">
                    <span>15 November 2026</span>
                    <span className="bg-sky-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-sky-800">Screening</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-xs">MMTC Short Film Showcase 2026</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Auditorium STMM MMTC Yogyakarta</p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-emerald-700 font-bold">
                    <span>22 November 2026</span>
                    <span className="bg-emerald-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-emerald-800">Online</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-xs">Webinar: Karir Game &amp; 3D Engine</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Sharing session bersama Alumni di Industri</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* FOOTER MINI LINKS */}
            <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 text-center space-y-1 p-2">
              <p>© 2026 STMM MMTC Yogyakarta AlumniHub</p>
              <p>Kementerian Komunikasi dan Digital RI</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      <Footer />

      {/* CINEMA LIGHTBOX MODAL */}
      <CinemaLightbox
        isOpen={Boolean(cinemaVideo)}
        onClose={() => setCinemaVideo(null)}
        title={cinemaVideo?.title || 'Showreel Alumni'}
        videoId={cinemaVideo?.videoId || ''}
      />

      {/* SHARE MODAL */}
      <ShareModal
        isOpen={Boolean(shareData)}
        onClose={() => setShareData(null)}
        title={shareData?.title || 'Beranda Alumni STMM MMTC'}
        subtitle={shareData?.subtitle}
        shareUrl={shareData?.url || ''}
      />

      {/* LINKEDIN-STYLE FLOATING MESSAGING DRAWER (Hanya untuk pengguna login) */}
      {user && (
        <LinkedInMessagingWidget 
          userAvatar={currentUser.avatar}
          userName={currentUser.name}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\app\lamaran\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';
import { getMyApplications, getReceivedApplications, updateApplicationStatus } from '@/lib/jobs';
import { JobApplication } from '@/types';
import { formatWhatsAppUrl, ensureAbsoluteUrl } from '@/lib/media';
import { 
  FileText, 
  Inbox, 
  Send, 
  Loader2,
  ExternalLink,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  Phone,
  User,
  Calendar
} from 'lucide-react';
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';

// Sample Mock Applications untuk Mode Preview Tanpa Login
const DEMO_SENT_APPS: JobApplication[] = [
  {
    id: 'demo-sent-1',
    jobId: 'job-1',
    jobTitle: '3D Animator & Modeler',
    applicantUid: 'demo-user',
    applicantName: 'Budi Santoso',
    applicantEmail: 'budi.santoso@alumni.mmtc.ac.id',
    applicantProdi: 'Animasi',
    posterUid: 'studio-infinite',
    pesan: 'Halo tim Infinite Studios, saya alumni Animasi MMTC angkatan 2020. Sangat tertarik berkontribusi pada serial animasi 3D terbaru studio Anda. Berikut link showreel dan portofolio saya.',
    cvExternalLink: 'https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/sample',
    status: 'reviewed',
    appliedAt: '2026-10-22T08:30:00.000Z'
  },
  {
    id: 'demo-sent-2',
    jobId: 'job-2',
    jobTitle: 'Motion Graphic Specialist TV',
    applicantUid: 'demo-user',
    applicantName: 'Budi Santoso',
    applicantEmail: 'budi.santoso@alumni.mmtc.ac.id',
    applicantProdi: 'Animasi',
    posterUid: 'trans-media',
    pesan: 'Saya memiliki pengalaman 2 tahun mengerjakan on-air graphics dan promo package televisi. Siap bergabung full-time di Jakarta.',
    status: 'accepted',
    appliedAt: '2026-10-18T14:15:00.000Z'
  }
];

const DEMO_RECEIVED_APPS: JobApplication[] = [
  {
    id: 'demo-rec-1',
    jobId: 'job-own-1',
    jobTitle: 'Junior Unity 3D Developer',
    applicantUid: 'alumni-siti',
    applicantName: 'Siti Rahma',
    applicantEmail: 'siti.rahma@gmail.com',
    applicantProdi: 'Game Design',
    applicantPhone: '081298765432',
    posterUid: 'demo-user',
    pesan: 'Halo Mas Budi! Saya fresh graduate Game Design MMTC 2024. Mahir C#, Unity Physics, dan UI gameplay. Ini portofolio game jam saya.',
    cvExternalLink: 'https://itch.io',
    status: 'pending',
    appliedAt: '2026-10-24T03:10:00.000Z'
  },
  {
    id: 'demo-rec-2',
    jobId: 'job-own-1',
    jobTitle: 'Junior Unity 3D Developer',
    applicantUid: 'alumni-dimas',
    applicantName: 'Dimas Aditya',
    applicantEmail: 'dimas.aditya@outlook.com',
    applicantProdi: 'Game Design',
    applicantPhone: '081345678910',
    posterUid: 'demo-user',
    pesan: 'Tertarik mengisi posisi Unity Developer untuk proyek game edukasi studio Anda. Pernah magang 6 bulan di Game Studio Yogyakarta.',
    cvExternalLink: 'https://github.com',
    status: 'reviewed',
    appliedAt: '2026-10-23T11:45:00.000Z'
  }
];

export default function LamaranPage() {
  const { user, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
  const isDemo = !user;

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'sent' | 'received'>('sent');
  const [sentApps, setSentApps] = useState<JobApplication[]>(DEMO_SENT_APPS);
  const [receivedApps, setReceivedApps] = useState<JobApplication[]>(DEMO_RECEIVED_APPS);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [updatingId, setUpdatingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [statusMsg, setStatusMsg] = useState<{ type: 'success' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadData() {
      if (!user) {
        setSentApps(DEMO_SENT_APPS);
        setReceivedApps(DEMO_RECEIVED_APPS);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const [sent, received] = await Promise.all([
          getMyApplications(user.uid),
          getReceivedApplications(user.uid)
        ]);
        setSentApps(sent);
        setReceivedApps(received);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load applications:', err);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    if (!authLoading) {
      loadData();
    }
  }, [user, authLoading]);

  const handleStatusChange = async (appId: string, newStatus: 'pending' | 'reviewed' | 'accepted' | 'rejected') => {
    setUpdatingId(appId);
    setStatusMsg(null);

    const statusLabels: Record<string, string> = {
      pending: 'Menunggu Respon',
      reviewed: 'Sedang Ditinjau',
      accepted: 'Diterima',
      rejected: 'Ditolak'
    };

    if (isDemo || !user) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        setReceivedApps((prev) =>
          prev.map((item) => (item.id === appId ? { ...item, status: newStatus } : item))
        );
        setUpdatingId(null);
        setStatusMsg({ type: 'success', text: `[Preview] Status lamaran disimulasikan menjadi "${statusLabels[newStatus]}".` });
        setTimeout(() => setStatusMsg(null), 3000);
      }, 400);
      return;
    }

    try {
      await updateApplicationStatus(appId, newStatus);
      setReceivedApps((prev) =>
        prev.map((item) => (item.id === appId ? { ...item, status: newStatus } : item))
      );
      setStatusMsg({ type: 'success', text: `Status lamaran berhasil diubah menjadi "${statusLabels[newStatus] || newStatus}".` });
      setTimeout(() => setStatusMsg(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const e = err as Error;
      setStatusMsg({ type: 'error', text: e.message || 'Gagal memperbarui status lamaran.' });
    } finally {
      setUpdatingId(null);
    }
  };

  if (authLoading || (user && loading)) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
        <Navbar />
        <main className="flex-1 max-w-5xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
          <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-[#0284c7]" />
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Memuat data pelamar &amp; lamaran...</p>
        </main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafcff] text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-5xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10 sm:py-12">
        
        {/* DEMO / PREVIEW BANNER JIKA DIAKSES TANPA LOGIN */}
        {isDemo && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-2xs">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">Pratinjau Riwayat Lamaran</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  Masuk dengan akun alumni untuk memantau status lamaran aktifmu dan mengelola pelamar yang masuk.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/login"
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-[#0369a1] transition shrink-0 text-center"
            >
              Masuk Akun
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mb-8 space-y-2">
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            Manajemen Lamaran Kerja
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-600 text-xs sm:text-sm">
            Pantau status respon lamaran yang kamu kirimkan dan kelola pelamar yang masuk ke lowongan studio kamu.
          </p>
        </div>

        {statusMsg && (
          <div
            className={`mb-6 p-4 rounded-2xl text-xs flex items-center gap-2.5 border animate-fade-in ${
              statusMsg.type === 'success'
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800'
                : 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-800'
            }`}
          >
            {statusMsg.type === 'success' ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
            ) : (
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 shrink-0" />
            )}
            <span className="font-semibold">{statusMsg.text}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MODERN PILL TABS */}
        <div className="flex border-b border-slate-200 mb-8 gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('sent')}
            className={`pb-3.5 px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold border-b-2 transition flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer ${
              activeTab === 'sent'
                ? 'border-[#0284c7] text-[#0284c7]'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Lamaran Terkirim</span>
            <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-bold">
              {sentApps.length}
            </span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('received')}
            className={`pb-3.5 px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold border-b-2 transition flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer ${
              activeTab === 'received'
                ? 'border-[#0284c7] text-[#0284c7]'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <Inbox className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Pelamar Masuk</span>
            <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] border border-sky-200 font-bold">
              {receivedApps.length}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* TAB SENT */}
        {activeTab === 'sent' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {sentApps.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-16 bg-white border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-3xl p-8 space-y-3 shadow-xs">
                <FileText className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-400 mx-auto" />
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800">Belum Ada Lamaran Terkirim</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Kamu belum pernah melamar pekerjaan apapun di portal alumni.</p>
                <Link
                  href="/lowongan"
                  className="inline-block mt-2 px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-[#0369a1] transition shadow-xs"
                >
                  Jelajahi Lowongan Kerja
                </Link>
              </div>
            ) : (
              sentApps.map((app) => (
                <div key={app.id} className="p-6 rounded-3xl bg-white border border-slate-200/90 shadow-sm space-y-4 hover:border-sky-300 transition">
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <h4 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{app.jobTitle}</h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium">
                        <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                        Dikirim pada: {app.appliedAt ? new Date(app.appliedAt).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { dateStyle: 'long' }) : '-'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <StatusBadge status={app.status} />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-700 bg-slate-50/90 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/70 space-y-1">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-600 block">Pesan Cover Letter:</span>
                    <p className="leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line text-slate-700">{app.pesan}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* TAB RECEIVED */}
        {activeTab === 'received' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {receivedApps.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-16 bg-white border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-3xl p-8 space-y-3 shadow-xs">
                <Inbox className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-400 mx-auto" />
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800">Belum Ada Pelamar Masuk</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Belum ada alumni yang melamar ke lowongan yang kamu terbitkan.</p>
                <Link
                  href="/post-lowongan"
                  className="inline-block mt-2 px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-[#0369a1] transition shadow-xs"
                >
                  Pasang Lowongan Baru
                </Link>
              </div>
            ) : (
              receivedApps.map((app) => (
                <div key={app.id} className="p-6 rounded-3xl bg-white border border-slate-200/90 shadow-sm space-y-4 hover:border-sky-300 transition">
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                        <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <User className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                          {app.applicantName}
                        </h4>
                        <Link
                          href={`/profil/${app.applicantUid}`}
                          target="_blank"
                          className="text-[11px] px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-sky-50 hover:bg-sky-100 border border-sky-200 text-[#0284c7] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition"
                        >
                          Lihat Profil &amp; Showreel <ExternalLink className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
                        </Link>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-[#0284c7] font-bold mt-1">
                        Prodi: {app.applicantProdi} • Melamar untuk: <strong className="text-slate-800">{app.jobTitle}</strong>
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                        Tanggal: {app.appliedAt ? new Date(app.appliedAt).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { dateStyle: 'long' }) : '-'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <StatusBadge status={app.status} />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-700 bg-slate-50/90 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/70 space-y-1">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-600 block">Pesan dari Pelamar:</span>
                    <p className="leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line text-slate-700">{app.pesan}</p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 text-xs">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-slate-600">
                      <span>Email: <strong className="text-slate-900">{app.applicantEmail}</strong></span>
                      {app.applicantPhone && (
                        <a 
                          href={formatWhatsAppUrl(app.applicantPhone)} 
                          target="_blank" 
                          rel="noreferrer"
                          className="text-emerald-700 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-emerald-200"
                        >
                          <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                          Hubungi WhatsApp ({app.applicantPhone}) <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                        </a>
                      )}
                      {app.cvUrl && (
                        <a 
                          href={app.cvUrl} 
                          target="_blank" 
                          rel="noreferrer"
                          className="text-[#0284c7] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1 font-bold bg-sky-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-sky-200"
                        >
                          <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          Buka CV PDF <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                        </a>
                      )}
                      {app.cvExternalLink && (
                        <a 
                          href={ensureAbsoluteUrl(app.cvExternalLink)} 
                          target="_blank" 
                          rel="noreferrer"
                          className="text-slate-700 hover:text-[#0284c7] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1 font-bold"
                        >
                          Portofolio Eksternal <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />
                        </a>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* ACTION BUTTONS FOR JOB POSTER */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-bold">Ubah Status:</span>
                      {updatingId === app.id ? (
                        <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-[#0284c7]" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={app.status === 'reviewed'}
                            onClick={() => handleStatusChange(app.id, 'reviewed')}
                            className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 hover:bg-amber-100 text-[11px] font-bold transition disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            Tinjau
                          </button>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={app.status === 'accepted'}
                            onClick={() => handleStatusChange(app.id, 'accepted')}
                            className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-[11px] font-bold transition disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            Terima
                          </button>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={app.status === 'rejected'}
                            onClick={() => handleStatusChange(app.id, 'rejected')}
                            className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-800 hover:bg-red-100 text-[11px] font-bold transition disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            Tolak
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\app\login\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState, Suspense } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { loginAlumni } from '@/lib/auth';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { LogIn, AlertCircle, Loader2, Mail, Lock, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';

function getSafeRedirect(target: string | null, fallback = '/lowongan'): string {
  if (!target) return fallback;
  const trimmed = target.trim();
  // Validasi agar hanya menerima relative internal path, tolak protokol eksternal atau format //
  if (
    trimmed.startsWith('/') &&
    !trimmed.startsWith('//') &&
    !trimmed.startsWith('/\\') &&
    !trimmed.includes('://') &&
    !trimmed.includes('\\')
  ) {
    return trimmed;
  }
  return fallback;
}

function LoginContent() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const redirectTarget = getSafeRedirect(searchParams.get('redirect'));

  const { user, profile, logout } = useAuth();
  const [identifier, setIdentifier] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');

    if (!identifier.trim() || !password) {
      setError('Mohon isi email / nomor alumni dan kata sandi.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    try {
      await loginAlumni(identifier, password);
      router.push(redirectTarget);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.error('Login error:', err);
      const e = err as Error;
      setError(e.message || 'Gagal masuk. Periksa email/nomor alumni dan password.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4 py-12 sm:py-16">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white border border-slate-200/90 p-8 sm:p-9 rounded-3xl shadow-sm space-y-6">
          <div className="text-center space-y-2">
            <img 
              src="/logo_alumnihub.png" 
              alt="AlumniHub Sekolah Tinggi Multi Media" 
              className="h-11 w-auto mx-auto object-contain mb-3"
            />
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Masuk Portal Alumni</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Gunakan Email atau Nomor Alumni MMTC yang sudah terdaftar
            </p>
          </div>

          {user && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-200 text-xs text-sky-800 space-y-2">
              <p>
                Anda saat ini sudah masuk sebagai <strong>{profile?.namaLengkap || user.email}</strong>.
              </p>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-1">
                <Link
                  href={redirectTarget}
                  className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] text-white font-bold hover:bg-[#0369a1] transition shadow-2xs"
                >
                  Lanjutkan ke Dashboard
                </Link>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => logout()}
                  className="text-xs text-red-600 hover:underline font-semibold"
                >
                  Ganti Akun (Keluar)
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {error && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-2 animate-fade-in">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                Email atau Nomor Alumni
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={identifier}
                onChange={(e) => setIdentifier(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="nama@email.com atau MMTC-2020-103"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                Kata Sandi
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full pl-3.5 pr-10 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute right-3 top-3 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition"
                  aria-label={showPassword ? 'Sembunyikan password' : 'Tampilkan password'}
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-xs transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 disabled:opacity-50 active:scale-[0.98]"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                  Memproses Masuk...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <LogIn className="w-4 h-4" />
                  Masuk ke Portal
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="pt-2 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 border-t border-slate-100">
            Belum punya akun alumni?{' '}
            <Link href="/register" className="text-[#0284c7] font-bold hover:underline">
              Daftar Alumni di sini
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function LoginPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-800">
        <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-[#0284c7]" />
      </div>
    }>
      <LoginContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\app\lowongan\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { getJobs, applyJob, getMyApplications } from '@/lib/jobs';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';
import { JobPosting, PRODI_LIST, ProdiType } from '@/types';
import { 
  Briefcase, 
  MapPin, 
  Search, 
  Filter, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Send, 
  Building2, 
  AlertCircle,
  Loader2,
  X,
  UserCheck,
  Calendar,
  Banknote
} from 'lucide-react';
import { JobCardSkeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeleton';
import { ProdiBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';

function LowonganContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const initialProdi = searchParams.get('prodi') as ProdiType | null;

  const { user, profile } = useAuth();
  const [jobs, setJobs] = useState<JobPosting[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedProdi, setSelectedProdi] = useState<string>(initialProdi || 'ALL');
  const [selectedTipeKerja, setSelectedTipeKerja] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [onlyWithSalary, setOnlyWithSalary] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [appliedJobIds, setAppliedJobIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [nowTimestamp] = useState(() => Date.now());
  
  // Apply Modal State
  const [applyingJob, setApplyingJob] = useState<JobPosting | null>(null);
  const [coverNote, setCoverNote] = useState('');
  const [customCvLink, setCustomCvLink] = useState('');
  const [sharePhone, setSharePhone] = useState(false);
  const [applyLoading, setApplyLoading] = useState(false);
  const [applySuccess, setApplySuccess] = useState(false);
  const [applyError, setApplyError] = useState('');

  // Sync selectedProdi when URL searchParams changes
  const prodiParam = searchParams.get('prodi');
  const [prevProdiParam, setPrevProdiParam] = useState<string | null>(prodiParam);

  if (prodiParam !== prevProdiParam) {
    setPrevProdiParam(prodiParam);
    setSelectedProdi(prodiParam && PRODI_LIST.some(p => p.id === prodiParam) ? prodiParam : 'ALL');
  }

  // Load jobs already applied by current user
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    const currentUid = user.uid;
    let isMounted = true;
    async function loadMyApplications() {
      try {
        const myApps = await getMyApplications(currentUid);
        if (isMounted) {
          setAppliedJobIds(new Set(myApps.map(a => a.jobId)));
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Could not load user applied jobs:', e);
      }
    }
    loadMyApplications();
    return () => { isMounted = false; };
  }, [user]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let ignore = false;
    async function fetchJobs() {
      try {
        const filter = selectedProdi !== 'ALL' ? (selectedProdi as ProdiType) : undefined;
        const data = await getJobs(filter);
        if (!ignore) {
          setJobs(data);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error in fetchJobs:', err);
      } finally {
        if (!ignore) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    fetchJobs();
    return () => {
      ignore = true;
    };
  }, [selectedProdi]);

  const filteredJobs = jobs.filter((job) => {
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    const matchesSearch = 
      !q ||
      job.judul.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      job.perusahaan.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      job.lokasi.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      job.prodiTarget?.some(p => p.toLowerCase().includes(q));

    const jobTipe = job.tipeKerja || (job as unknown as { tipe?: string }).tipe || '';
    const matchesTipe = selectedTipeKerja === 'ALL' || jobTipe === selectedTipeKerja;
    const matchesSalary = !onlyWithSalary || Boolean(job.gajiRange && job.gajiRange.trim().length > 0);

    return matchesSearch && matchesTipe && matchesSalary;
  });

  const handleOpenApplyModal = (job: JobPosting) => {
    if (job.deadline && !isNaN(Date.parse(job.deadline)) && new Date(job.deadline).setHours(23, 59, 59, 999) < nowTimestamp) {
      return;
    }
    setApplyingJob(job);
    setApplyError('');
    setApplySuccess(false);
    setCoverNote('');
    setCustomCvLink(profile?.cvExternalLink || '');
    setSharePhone(false);
  };

  const handleApplySubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!user || !applyingJob) return;

    if (applyingJob.deadline && !isNaN(Date.parse(applyingJob.deadline)) && new Date(applyingJob.deadline).setHours(23, 59, 59, 999) < Date.now()) {
      setApplyError('Batas waktu pendaftaran lowongan ini telah berakhir.');
      return;
    }

    if (applyingJob.postedBy === user.uid) {
      setApplyError('Kamu tidak bisa melamar pada lowongan yang kamu pasang sendiri.');
      return;
    }

    if (!coverNote.trim()) {
      setApplyError('Mohon tuliskan pesan cover letter singkat.');
      return;
    }

    setApplyLoading(true);
    setApplyError('');
    try {
      const applicantName = profile?.namaLengkap || user.displayName || user.email?.split('@')[0] || 'Alumni MMTC';
      const applicantEmail = profile?.email || user.email || '';
      const applicantProdi = profile?.prodi || 'Animasi';

      await applyJob({
        jobId: applyingJob.id,
        jobTitle: applyingJob.judul,
        applicantUid: user.uid,
        applicantName,
        applicantEmail,
        applicantProdi,
        applicantPhone: sharePhone ? profile?.phone : undefined,
        posterUid: applyingJob.postedBy,
        pesan: coverNote.trim(),
        cvUrl: profile?.cvFileUrl,
        cvExternalLink: customCvLink.trim() || profile?.cvExternalLink
      });
      setAppliedJobIds((prev) => new Set([...prev, applyingJob.id]));
      setApplySuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setApplyingJob(null);
        setApplySuccess(false);
        setCoverNote('');
        setCustomCvLink('');
      }, 2000);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const errorObj = err as Error;
      setApplyError(errorObj.message || 'Gagal mengirim lamaran.');
    } finally {
      setApplyLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10 sm:py-12">
        {/* HEADER SECTION */}
        <div className="mb-8 space-y-2">
          <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            Portal Lowongan Kerja Alumni
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">
