            Eksplorasi kesempatan karir di industri kreatif, penyiaran televisi &amp; radio, game studio, serta IT yang diposting oleh sesama alumni dan mitra terpercaya STMM MMTC Yogyakarta.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* SEARCH & FILTER BAR */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/80 p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl mb-8 space-y-4 shadow-2xs">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-3.5" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Cari judul posisi, nama studio/perusahaan, prodi, atau kota..."
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
              />
              {searchQuery && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                  className="absolute right-3 top-3 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"
                  aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
                >
                  <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 md:pb-0 scrollbar-none">
              <Filter className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0 ml-1" />
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setSelectedProdi('ALL');
                  setLoading(true);
                }}
                className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition cursor-pointer ${
                  selectedProdi === 'ALL'
                    ? 'bg-[#0284c7] text-white shadow-2xs'
                    : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 border border-slate-200/80 hover:bg-slate-100'
                }`}
              >
                Semua Jurusan
              </button>
              {PRODI_LIST.map((p) => (
                <button
                  key={p.id}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSelectedProdi(p.id);
                    setLoading(true);
                  }}
                  className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition cursor-pointer ${
                    selectedProdi === p.id
                      ? 'bg-[#0284c7] text-white shadow-2xs'
                      : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 border border-slate-200/80 hover:bg-slate-100'
                  }`}
                >
                  {p.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* SECONDARY FILTER BAR: TIPE KERJA & GAJI TOGGLE */}
          <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 sm:pb-0 scrollbar-none">
              <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold mr-1">Tipe:</span>
              {['ALL', 'Full-time', 'Freelance', 'Magang', 'Part-time'].map((type) => (
                <button
                  key={type}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedTipeKerja(type)}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer ${
                    selectedTipeKerja === type
                      ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-xs'
                      : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600'
                  }`}
                >
                  {type === 'ALL' ? 'Semua Tipe' : type}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 cursor-pointer select-none">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={onlyWithSalary}
                onChange={(e) => setOnlyWithSalary(e.target.checked)}
                className="w-4 h-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-[#0284c7] focus:ring-[#0284c7]"
              />
              <span className="font-medium text-xs">Hanya yang mencantumkan gaji</span>
            </label>

          </div>
        </div>

        {/* JOB LISTINGS */}
        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            <JobCardSkeleton />
            <JobCardSkeleton />
            <JobCardSkeleton />
            <JobCardSkeleton />
            <JobCardSkeleton />
            <JobCardSkeleton />
          </div>
        ) : filteredJobs.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-20 bg-white border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-8 space-y-4">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-slate-400">
              <Briefcase className="w-7 h-7" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Belum Ada Lowongan Ditemukan</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                {searchQuery
                  ? `Tidak ada lowongan yang sesuai dengan kata kunci "${searchQuery}".`
                  : 'Belum ada lowongan untuk program studi ini. Jadilah alumni pertama yang memasang lowongan!'}
              </p>
            </div>
            {user && (
              <Link
                href="/post-lowongan"
                className="inline-block px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-[#0369a1] transition shadow-xs"
              >
                + Pasang Lowongan Baru
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {filteredJobs.map((job) => {
              const isMyJob = Boolean(user && job.postedBy === user.uid);
              const hasApplied = Boolean(user && appliedJobIds.has(job.id));
              const isDeadlinePassed = Boolean(
                job.deadline && !isNaN(Date.parse(job.deadline)) && new Date(job.deadline).setHours(23, 59, 59, 999) < nowTimestamp
              );
              return (
                <div 
                  key={job.id} 
                  className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 hover:border-slate-300 rounded-2xl p-6 flex flex-col justify-between transition hover:shadow-md shadow-2xs group"
                >
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 mb-2.5">
                        <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold">
                          {job.tipeKerja || (job as unknown as { tipe?: string }).tipe}
                        </span>
                        {job.deadline && !isNaN(Date.parse(job.deadline)) && (
                          <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium border ${
                            isDeadlinePassed
                              ? 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-700'
                              : 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-200 text-amber-700'
                          }`}>
                            <Calendar className="w-3 h-3" />
                            {isDeadlinePassed ? 'Batas Lewat: ' : 'Batas: '}
                            {new Date(job.deadline).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0284c7] transition leading-snug">
                        {job.judul}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium">
                        <Building2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                        <span className="truncate">{job.perusahaan}</span>
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                        <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                        {job.lokasi}
                      </span>
                      {job.gajiRange && (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-600 font-medium text-xs">
                          <Banknote className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                          <span>{job.gajiRange}</span>
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed">
                      {job.deskripsi}
                    </p>

                    {/* KUALIFIKASI PREVIEW */}
                    {job.kualifikasi && job.kualifikasi.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
                        <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600">Persyaratan Utama:</p>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                          {job.kualifikasi.slice(0, 2).map((k, i) => (
                            <span key={i} className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/80 text-slate-700">
                              • {k}
                            </span>
                          ))}
                          {job.kualifikasi.length > 2 && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 py-0.5 px-1">
                              +{job.kualifikasi.length - 2} lainnya
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* PRODI TARGET TAGS */}
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-1">
                      {job.prodiTarget?.map((prodi) => (
                        <ProdiBadge key={prodi} prodi={prodi} />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-5 mt-5 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-medium">
                      Oleh {job.posterName}
                    </span>
                    
                    {isMyJob ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 text-xs font-semibold">
                        <UserCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Loker Anda
                      </span>
                    ) : hasApplied ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 text-xs font-semibold">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" /> Sudah Dilamar
                      </span>
                    ) : isDeadlinePassed ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 text-xs font-semibold border border-slate-200">
                        Ditutup
                      </span>
                    ) : user ? (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleOpenApplyModal(job)}
                        className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-2xs transition flex items-center gap-1.5 active:scale-[0.98]"
                      >
                        <Send className="w-3 h-3" />
                        Lamar Posisi
                      </button>
                    ) : (
                      <Link
                        href="/login?redirect=/lowongan"
                        className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold border border-slate-200 transition"
                      >
                        Masuk &amp; Lamar
                      </Link>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* APPLY MODAL */}
        {applyingJob && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in">
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-lg p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-5 relative shadow-xl">
              <button 
                onClick={() => setApplyingJob(null)}
                className="absolute top-5 right-5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 p-1 rounded-lg transition"
                aria-label="Tutup"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>

              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Kirim Lamaran Pekerjaan</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  Posisi: <strong className="text-slate-800">{applyingJob.judul}</strong> ({applyingJob.perusahaan})
                </p>
              </div>

              {applySuccess ? (
                <div className="py-8 text-center space-y-3">
                  <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-emerald-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-emerald-600">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8" />
                  </div>
                  <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Lamaran Berhasil Dikirimkan!</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-xs mx-auto">
                    Profil dan portofoliomu sudah diteruskan ke rekan pengunggah lowongan.
                  </p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <form onSubmit={handleApplySubmit} className="space-y-4">
                  {applyError && (
                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
                      <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                      <span>{applyError}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs space-y-1.5">
                    <p className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Ringkasan Data Pelamar:</p>
                    <p className="text-slate-600">
                      Nama: <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium">{profile?.namaLengkap || user?.displayName || user?.email}</span>
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-slate-600">
                      Prodi &amp; Angkatan: <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium">{profile?.prodi || 'Animasi'} ({profile?.angkatan || '-'})</span>
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-slate-600">
                      CV Terlampir:{' '}
                      <span className="text-[#0284c7] font-semibold">
                        {profile?.cvFileUrl ? 'PDF Resmi Terunggah' : profile?.cvExternalLink ? 'Tautan Portofolio' : 'Belum diunggah di profil'}
                      </span>
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Tautan CV / Portofolio Eksternal (Opsional, e.g. Google Drive/Notion/Dropbox)
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="url"
                      value={customCvLink}
                      onChange={(e) => setCustomCvLink(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="https://drive.google.com/..."
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Pengantar Singkat untuk Perekrut *
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      rows={4}
                      required
                      value={coverNote}
                      onChange={(e) => setCoverNote(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Ceritakan sedikit ketertarikanmu pada posisi ini, pengalaman proyek relevan, atau showreel karya terbaikmu..."
                      className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition leading-relaxed"
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* F-02 & UU PDP Consent: Izin eksplisit berbagi nomor WhatsApp */}
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-slate-700">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      id="sharePhoneCheck"
                      checked={sharePhone}
                      onChange={(e) => setSharePhone(e.target.checked)}
                      className="mt-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded text-[#0284c7] border-slate-300 focus:ring-[#0284c7] cursor-pointer"
                    />
                    <label htmlFor="sharePhoneCheck" className="text-[11px] leading-relaxed cursor-pointer select-none">
                      Sertakan nomor WhatsApp saya pada lamaran ini agar perekrut dapat menghubungi langsung.
                    </label>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setApplyingJob(null)}
                      className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50 transition"
                    >
                      Batal
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={applyLoading}
                      className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-xs flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 transition active:scale-[0.98]"
                    >
                      {applyLoading ? (
                        <>
                          <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                          Mengirim Lamaran...
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          Kirim Lamaran Sekarang
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function LowonganPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-800">
        <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-[#0284c7]" />
      </div>
    }>
      <LowonganContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\app\open-to-work\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { db } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { collection, query, where, getDocs } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { UserProfile, PRODI_LIST } from '@/types';
import { 
  Search, 
  Filter, 
  User, 
  ArrowRight, 
  FileText, 
  Video,
  X,
  Briefcase
} from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchOpenToWorkRest } from '@/lib/firestoreRest';
import AlumniAvatar from '@/components/profile/AlumniAvatar';
import { TalentCardSkeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeleton';
import { extractSkills } from '@/components/profile/skills';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';

interface ExtendedUserProfile extends UserProfile {
  pekerjaanSaatIni?: string;
  pekerjaan?: string;
  domisili?: string;
}

export default function OpenToWorkPage() {
  const { profile: authProfile } = useAuth();
  const [alumniList, setAlumniList] = useState<UserProfile[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedProdi, setSelectedProdi] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    async function fetchOpenToWorkAlumni() {
      setLoading(true);

      // 1. Coba REST API terarah terlebih dahulu (~100ms dari public_profiles)
      try {
        const otUsers = await fetchOpenToWorkRest();
        if (isMounted && otUsers.length > 0) {
          setAlumniList(otUsers);
          setLoading(false);
          return;
        }
      } catch (restErr) {
        console.warn('REST fetch failed in open-to-work, trying SDK:', restErr);
      }

      // 2. Fallback ke Firestore SDK membaca public_profiles whitelist
      try {
        const q = query(collection(db, 'public_profiles'), where('openToWork', '==', true));
        const fetchPromise = getDocs(q);
        const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) =>
          setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('OpenToWork fetch timeout')), 3000)
        );
        const snap = await Promise.race([fetchPromise, timeoutPromise]);
        if (isMounted) {
          const list = snap.docs.map(d => ({ uid: d.id, ...d.data() } as UserProfile));
          setAlumniList(list);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error fetching open to work alumni from public_profiles:', err);
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }
    fetchOpenToWorkAlumni();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  // Self-healing: Pastikan profil pengguna yang sedang login langsung muncul jika openToWork aktif
  const displayedAlumniList = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (!authProfile || !authProfile.openToWork) return alumniList;
    const exists = alumniList.some((a) => a.uid === authProfile.uid);
    if (exists) {
      return alumniList.map((a) => (a.uid === authProfile.uid ? { ...a, ...authProfile } : a));
    }
    return [authProfile, ...alumniList];
  }, [alumniList, authProfile]);

  const filteredAlumni = displayedAlumniList.filter((a) => {
    const matchesProdi = selectedProdi === 'ALL' || a.prodi === selectedProdi;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    const matchesSearch = 
      !q ||
      a.namaLengkap?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      a.bio?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      a.prodi?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      a.nomorAlumni?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      a.angkatan?.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    return matchesProdi && matchesSearch;
  });

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10 sm:py-12">
        {/* HEADER SECTION */}
        <div className="mb-8 space-y-3">
          <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            Alumni MMTC <span className="text-emerald-600">Open to Work</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">
            Direktori rekan alumni yang siap bergabung untuk produksi lepas (freelance), posisi penuh waktu di studio, maupun kolaborasi lintas prodi. Tonton showreel karya mereka dan hubungi langsung.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* SEARCH & FILTER BAR */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/80 p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl mb-8 space-y-4 shadow-2xs">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-3.5" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Cari talenta berdasarkan nama, keahlian, nomor alumni, atau prodi..."
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
              />
              {searchQuery && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                  className="absolute right-3 top-3 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"
                  aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
                >
                  <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1 md:pb-0 scrollbar-none">
              <Filter className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0 ml-1" />
              <button
                onClick={() => setSelectedProdi('ALL')}
                className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition cursor-pointer ${
                  selectedProdi === 'ALL'
                    ? 'bg-[#0284c7] text-white shadow-2xs'
                    : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 border border-slate-200/80 hover:bg-slate-100'
                }`}
              >
                Semua Jurusan ({alumniList.length})
              </button>
              {PRODI_LIST.map((p) => {
                const count = alumniList.filter(a => a.prodi === p.id).length;
                const isSelected = selectedProdi === p.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={p.id}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedProdi(p.id)}
                    className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition cursor-pointer ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-[#0284c7] text-white shadow-2xs'
                        : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 border border-slate-200/80 hover:bg-slate-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {p.label} {count > 0 ? `(${count})` : ''}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* TALENT GRID */}
        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            <TalentCardSkeleton />
            <TalentCardSkeleton />
            <TalentCardSkeleton />
            <TalentCardSkeleton />
            <TalentCardSkeleton />
            <TalentCardSkeleton />
          </div>
        ) : filteredAlumni.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-20 bg-white border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-8 space-y-4">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-slate-400">
              <User className="w-7 h-7" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Belum Ada Talenta di Kategori Ini</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                {searchQuery 
                  ? `Tidak ditemukan talenta alumni yang cocok dengan kata kunci "${searchQuery}".` 
                  : 'Belum ada alumni yang memasang status Open to Work di program studi ini.'}
              </p>
            </div>
            {(searchQuery || selectedProdi !== 'ALL') && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setSearchQuery('');
                  setSelectedProdi('ALL');
                }}
                className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold transition"
              >
                Reset Filter Pencarian
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {filteredAlumni.map((alumni) => (
              <div 
                key={alumni.uid}
                className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 hover:border-slate-300 rounded-2xl p-6 flex flex-col justify-between transition hover:shadow-md group shadow-2xs"
              >
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {/* TOP: AVATAR & PRODI BADGE */}
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                      <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-bold overflow-hidden shadow-2xs border-2 border-white shrink-0">
                        <AlumniAvatar src={alumni.fotoProfil} name={alumni.namaLengkap} />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0284c7] transition leading-snug">
                          {alumni.namaLengkap}
                        </h3>
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                          {alumni.prodi} • Angkatan {alumni.angkatan}
                        </p>
                        {((alumni as ExtendedUserProfile).pekerjaanSaatIni || (alumni as ExtendedUserProfile).pekerjaan) && (
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-[#0284c7] font-semibold mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 truncate max-w-[200px]">
                            <Briefcase className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                            <span className="truncate">{(alumni as ExtendedUserProfile).pekerjaanSaatIni || (alumni as ExtendedUserProfile).pekerjaan}</span>
                          </p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                  </div>

                  {/* BIO */}
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
                    {alumni.bio || 'Alumni aktif Sekolah Tinggi Multi Media Yogyakarta.'}
                  </p>

                  {/* SOFTWARE & SKILL CHIPS */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-1">
                    {extractSkills(alumni.bio, alumni.prodi).map((skill) => (
                      <span 
                        key={skill} 
                        className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200/60"
                      >
                        {skill}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  {/* PORTFOLIO SNAPSHOT BADGES */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-medium bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-100">
                      <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                      {alumni.portfolio?.length || 0} Karya Showreel
                    </span>
                    {(alumni.cvFileUrl || alumni.cvExternalLink) && (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[#0284c7] font-semibold bg-sky-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-sky-100">
                        <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        CV Ready
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* BOTTOM: VIEW PROFILE BUTTON */}
                <div className="pt-5 mt-5 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                    {alumni.nomorAlumni}
                  </span>

                  <Link
                    href={`/profil/${alumni.uid}`}
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-[#0284c7] hover:text-white text-slate-800 text-xs font-semibold border border-slate-200/80 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 group-hover:border-[#0284c7]"
                  >
                    Lihat Profil &amp; Showreel
                    <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7] group-hover:text-white transition" />
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\app\post-lowongan\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';
import { createJob } from '@/lib/jobs';
import { PRODI_LIST, ProdiType } from '@/types';
import { 
  PlusCircle, 
  AlertCircle, 
  Loader2, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Building2, 
  MapPin, 
  Calendar, 
  Banknote, 
  Briefcase,
  FileText, 
  GraduationCap
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function PostLowonganPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, profile, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
  const isDemo = !user;

  const [judul, setJudul] = useState('');
  const [perusahaan, setPerusahaan] = useState('');
  const [lokasi, setLokasi] = useState('');
  const [tipeKerja, setTipeKerja] = useState<'Full-time' | 'Part-time' | 'Freelance' | 'Magang'>('Full-time');
  const [selectedProdi, setSelectedProdi] = useState<ProdiType[]>(['Animasi']);
  const [deskripsi, setDeskripsi] = useState('');
  const [gajiRange, setGajiRange] = useState('');
  const [kualifikasiRaw, setKualifikasiRaw] = useState('');
  const [deadline, setDeadline] = useState('');

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Helper smart format angka atau rentang ke Rupiah
  const formatRupiahString = (val: string): string => {
    const trimmed = val.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return '';
    if (trimmed.includes('-')) {
      const parts = trimmed.split('-');
      const formattedParts = parts.map(part => {
        const digits = part.replace(/\D/g, '');
        return digits ? 'Rp ' + parseInt(digits, 10).toLocaleString('id-ID') : part.trim();
      });
      return formattedParts.join(' - ');
    }
    const cleanNumber = trimmed.replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (cleanNumber) {
      return 'Rp ' + parseInt(cleanNumber, 10).toLocaleString('id-ID');
    }
    return trimmed;
  };

  const handleGajiBlur = () => {
    if (gajiRange) {
      setGajiRange(formatRupiahString(gajiRange));
    }
  };

  const toggleProdi = (p: ProdiType) => {
    if (selectedProdi.includes(p)) {
      if (selectedProdi.length > 1) {
        setSelectedProdi(selectedProdi.filter(item => item !== p));
      }
    } else {
      setSelectedProdi([...selectedProdi, p]);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!judul.trim() || !perusahaan.trim() || !lokasi.trim() || !deskripsi.trim()) {
      setError('Mohon lengkapi semua kolom wajib bertanda bintang (*).');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    if (isDemo || !user) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        setLoading(false);
        setSuccess(true);
        setTimeout(() => {
          router.push('/lowongan');
        }, 1500);
      }, 700);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const kualifikasi = kualifikasiRaw
        .split('\n')
        .map(k => k.trim())
        .filter(k => k.length > 0);

      const posterName = profile?.namaLengkap || user.displayName || user.email?.split('@')[0] || 'Alumni MMTC';
      const posterEmail = profile?.email || user.email || '';

      await createJob({
        postedBy: user.uid,
        posterName,
        posterEmail,
        judul: judul.trim(),
        perusahaan: perusahaan.trim(),
        lokasi: lokasi.trim(),
        tipeKerja,
        prodiTarget: selectedProdi,
        deskripsi: deskripsi.trim(),
        kualifikasi,
        gajiRange: gajiRange.trim() || undefined,
        deadline: deadline || undefined
      });

      setSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        router.push('/lowongan');
      }, 1500);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const errorObj = err as Error;
      setError(errorObj.message || 'Gagal memposting lowongan.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (authLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-800">
        <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-[#0284c7]" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafcff] text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-4xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10 sm:py-12">
        
        {/* DEMO / PREVIEW BANNER JIKA DIAKSES TANPA LOGIN */}
        {isDemo && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-2xs">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <Briefcase className="w-4 h-4" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">Masuk untuk Memasang Lowongan Resmi</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  Gunakan akun alumni agar informasi lowongan kerja atau rekrutmen kru langsung tayang di bursa karir.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/login"
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-[#0369a1] transition shrink-0 text-center"
            >
              Masuk Akun
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mb-8 space-y-2">
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            Pasang Lowongan Kerja Alumni
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-600 text-xs sm:text-sm">
            Bantu sesama alumni dan adik tingkat MMTC mendapatkan kesempatan berkarya di perusahaan, stasiun TV, game studio, atau agensi kreatifmu.
          </p>
        </div>

        {success ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl space-y-3 shadow-sm animate-fade-in">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-emerald-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-emerald-600">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-10 h-10" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Lowongan Berhasil Ditayangkan!</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Mengarahkan Anda ke daftar lowongan kerja...</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            {error && (
              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-2.5 animate-fade-in">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-semibold">{error}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* SECTION 1: INFORMASI DASAR */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-5 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Building2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                Detail Posisi &amp; Perusahaan
              </h3>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Judul Posisi / Pekerjaan *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={judul}
                    onChange={(e) => setJudul(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. 3D Animator, Video Editor, Unity Game Developer"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Nama Perusahaan / Studio *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={perusahaan}
                    onChange={(e) => setPerusahaan(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. Infinite Studios, Trans Media, Gameloft"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                    Lokasi Kerja *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={lokasi}
                    onChange={(e) => setLokasi(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. Jakarta Selatan / Remote / Yogyakarta"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Tipe Pekerjaan *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={tipeKerja}
                    onChange={(e) => setTipeKerja(e.target.value as 'Full-time' | 'Part-time' | 'Freelance' | 'Magang')}
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition font-medium"
                  >
                    <option value="Full-time">Full-time</option>
                    <option value="Part-time">Part-time</option>
                    <option value="Freelance">Freelance / Project-based</option>
                    <option value="Magang">Magang / Internship</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <Banknote className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                    Perkiraan Gaji (Opsional)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={gajiRange}
                    onChange={(e) => setGajiRange(e.target.value)}
                    onBlur={handleGajiBlur}
                    placeholder="Contoh: Rp 7.500.000 atau Rp 5.000.000 - Rp 8.000.000"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition font-medium text-slate-900"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* BATAS WAKTU LAMARAN */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  Batas Waktu Lamaran / Deadline (Opsional)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={deadline}
                  onChange={(e) => setDeadline(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full sm:w-1/2 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* SECTION 2: PILIH JURUSAN TARGET */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-4 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                Target Program Studi Alumni (Bisa pilih lebih dari satu) *
              </h3>
              
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                {PRODI_LIST.map((p) => {
                  const isChecked = selectedProdi.includes(p.id);
                  return (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      key={p.id}
                      onClick={() => toggleProdi(p.id)}
                      className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-left text-xs transition cursor-pointer ${
                        isChecked 
                          ? 'bg-sky-50 border-[#0284c7] text-[#0284c7] font-bold shadow-2xs' 
                          : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200/80 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                        <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{p.label}</span>
                        {isChecked && <span className="text-[10px] bg-[#0284c7] text-white px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full font-bold">✓</span>}
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 line-clamp-1 leading-snug">{p.desc}</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* SECTION 3: DESKRIPSI & PERSYARATAN */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-4 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                Deskripsi &amp; Persyaratan Kualifikasi
              </h3>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  Deskripsi Pekerjaan &amp; Tanggung Jawab *
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={5}
                  required
                  value={deskripsi}
                  onChange={(e) => setDeskripsi(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Jelaskan mengenai proyek atau pekerjaan, tanggung jawab utama, dan workflow di studio..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition leading-relaxed"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  Kualifikasi &amp; Persyaratan (Tulis satu persyaratan per baris)
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={4}
                  value={kualifikasiRaw}
                  onChange={(e) => setKualifikasiRaw(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Menguasai software Blender / Maya&#10;Memiliki portfolio atau showreel animasi aktif&#10;Mampu bekerja secara komunikatif dalam tim"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition leading-relaxed"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="px-8 py-3.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] hover:from-sky-600 hover:to-[#0369a1] text-white text-sm font-bold shadow-md shadow-sky-500/20 transition flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                    Menerbitkan Lowongan...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <PlusCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                    Tayangkan Lowongan Sekarang
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export interface CustomLinkItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  url: string;
}

interface ExtendedUserProfile extends UserProfile {
  pekerjaanSaatIni?: string;
  domisili?: string;
  instagramUrl?: string;
  customLinks?: CustomLinkItem[];
}

function withTimeout<T>(promise: Promise<T>, ms: number, message: string): Promise<T> {
  const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) =>
    setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(message)), ms)
  );
  return Promise.race([promise, timeoutPromise]);
}

    },
    {
      id: 'demo-2',
      title: 'Broadcast Motion Graphic Package',
      type: 'behance' as const,
      url: 'https://behance.net'
    }
  ]
};

const getLocalProfile = (uid?: string): ExtendedUserProfile | null => {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    const key = uid ? `mmtc_profile_${uid}` : 'mmtc_demo_profile';
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key) || localStorage.getItem('mmtc_demo_profile');
    if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
  return null;
};

// Komponen pratinjau portofolio gambar di luar fungsi render ProfilPage (React 19 pattern)
function PortfolioImagePreview({ url, title }: { url: string; title: string }) {
  const [hasError, setHasError] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 max-h-60 flex items-center justify-center p-2">
      {hasError ? (
        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 py-6">Pratinjau gambar tidak dapat dimuat</span>
      ) : (
        <img 
          src={url} 
          alt={title} 
          className="w-full h-full max-h-56 object-contain rounded-lg" 
          onError={() => setHasError(true)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ProfilPage() {
  const { user, profile, loading: authLoading, refreshProfile, setProfileData } = useAuth();
  const isDemo = !user;

  // Local state for profile persistence across preview/demo and offline saves
  const [savedProfile, setSavedProfile] = useState<ExtendedUserProfile | null>(() => getLocalProfile(user?.uid));

  const currentProfile: ExtendedUserProfile = 
    savedProfile || (profile as ExtendedUserProfile | null) || DEMO_PROFILE;

  const [namaLengkap, setNamaLengkap] = useState(() => currentProfile.namaLengkap || DEMO_PROFILE.namaLengkap);
  const [prodi, setProdi] = useState<ProdiType>(() => (currentProfile.prodi as ProdiType) || 'Animasi');
  const [angkatan, setAngkatan] = useState(() => currentProfile.angkatan || DEMO_PROFILE.angkatan);
  const [openToWork, setOpenToWork] = useState(() => currentProfile.openToWork ?? true);
  const [pekerjaanSaatIni, setPekerjaanSaatIni] = useState(() => currentProfile.pekerjaanSaatIni || DEMO_PROFILE.pekerjaanSaatIni);
  const [domisili, setDomisili] = useState(() => currentProfile.domisili || DEMO_PROFILE.domisili);
  const [bio, setBio] = useState(() => currentProfile.bio || DEMO_PROFILE.bio);
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(() => currentProfile.phone || DEMO_PROFILE.phone);
  const [linkedinUrl, setLinkedinUrl] = useState(() => currentProfile.linkedinUrl || DEMO_PROFILE.linkedinUrl);
  const [instagramUrl, setInstagramUrl] = useState(() => currentProfile.instagramUrl || DEMO_PROFILE.instagramUrl);
  const [customLinks, setCustomLinks] = useState<CustomLinkItem[]>(() => currentProfile.customLinks || DEMO_PROFILE.customLinks);
  const [cvExternalLink, setCvExternalLink] = useState(() => currentProfile.cvExternalLink || DEMO_PROFILE.cvExternalLink);

  // Portfolio State
  const [portfolioList, setPortfolioList] = useState<PortfolioItem[]>(() => currentProfile.portfolio || DEMO_PROFILE.portfolio);
  const [portType, setPortType] = useState<'youtube' | 'pinterest' | 'behance' | 'image' | 'link'>('youtube');
  const [portTitle, setPortTitle] = useState('');
  const [portUrl, setPortUrl] = useState('');

  // Upload file state
  const [cvFile, setCvFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [uploadingCV, setUploadingCV] = useState(false);
  const [uploadingAvatar, setUploadingAvatar] = useState(false);

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState({ type: '', text: '' });
  const [cinemaVideo, setCinemaVideo] = useState<{ videoId: string; title: string } | null>(null);

  const handleAddCustomLink = () => {
    const newLink: CustomLinkItem = {
      id: Date.now().toString(),
      title: '',
      url: ''
    };
    setCustomLinks((prev) => [...prev, newLink]);
  };

  const handleUpdateCustomLink = (id: string, field: 'title' | 'url', value: string) => {
    setCustomLinks((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, [field]: value } : item))
    );
  };

  const handleRemoveCustomLink = (id: string) => {
    setCustomLinks((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  };

  // Guard against overwriting local uncommitted form inputs when background profile refreshes for same user
  const initializedUidRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profile && initializedUidRef.current !== profile.uid) {
      initializedUidRef.current = profile.uid;
      const ext = profile as ExtendedUserProfile;
      setNamaLengkap(profile.namaLengkap || '');
      setProdi(profile.prodi || 'Animasi');
      setAngkatan(profile.angkatan || '');
      setOpenToWork(profile.openToWork ?? true);
      setPekerjaanSaatIni(ext.pekerjaanSaatIni || '');
      setDomisili(ext.domisili || '');
      setBio(profile.bio || '');
      setPhone(profile.phone || '');
      setLinkedinUrl(profile.linkedinUrl || '');
      setInstagramUrl(ext.instagramUrl || '');
      setCustomLinks(ext.customLinks || []);
      setCvExternalLink(profile.cvExternalLink || '');
      setPortfolioList(profile.portfolio || []);
    }
  }, [profile]);

  const handleAddPortfolio = async () => {
    if (!portTitle.trim() || !portUrl.trim()) return;
    const newItem: PortfolioItem = {
      id: Date.now().toString(),
      type: portType,
      title: portTitle.trim(),
      url: portUrl.trim(),
    };
    const updatedList = [...portfolioList, newItem];
    setPortfolioList(updatedList);
    setPortTitle('');
    setPortUrl('');

    // Update local cache & state
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      try {
        const storageKey = user ? `mmtc_profile_${user.uid}` : 'mmtc_demo_profile';
        const currentData = savedProfile || currentProfile || DEMO_PROFILE;
        const updated = { ...currentData, portfolio: updatedList };
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(updated));
        localStorage.setItem('mmtc_demo_profile', JSON.stringify(updated));
        setSavedProfile(updated as ExtendedUserProfile);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('LocalStorage error:', err);
      }
    }

    if (isDemo || !user) {
      setMsg({ type: 'success', text: `Berhasil menambahkan "${newItem.title}" ke galeri showreel!` });
      setTimeout(() => setMsg({ type: '', text: '' }), 4000);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
      await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'users',
          id: user.uid,
          data: { portfolio: updatedList, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });
      await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'public_profiles',
          id: user.uid,
          data: { portfolio: updatedList, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });
    } catch {
      // ignore local api error
    }

    try {
      await withTimeout(
        Promise.all([
          setDoc(doc(db, 'users', user.uid), {
            portfolio: updatedList,
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
          }, { merge: true }),
          setDoc(doc(db, 'public_profiles', user.uid), {
            portfolio: updatedList,
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
          }, { merge: true })
        ]),
        6000,
        'Koneksi timeout saat menyimpan portofolio'
      );
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.warn('Cloud Firestore portfolio save fallback:', err);
    }
    await refreshProfile();
    setMsg({ type: 'success', text: `Berhasil menambahkan "${newItem.title}" ke portofolio!` });
    setTimeout(() => setMsg({ type: '', text: '' }), 4000);
  };

  const handleRemovePortfolio = async (id: string) => {
    const updatedList = portfolioList.filter(p => p.id !== id);
    setPortfolioList(updatedList);

    // Update local cache & state
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      try {
        const storageKey = user ? `mmtc_profile_${user.uid}` : 'mmtc_demo_profile';
        const currentData = savedProfile || currentProfile || DEMO_PROFILE;
        const updated = { ...currentData, portfolio: updatedList };
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(updated));
        localStorage.setItem('mmtc_demo_profile', JSON.stringify(updated));
        setSavedProfile(updated as ExtendedUserProfile);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('LocalStorage error:', err);
      }
    }

    if (isDemo || !user) {
      setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Item portofolio berhasil dihapus.' });
      setTimeout(() => setMsg({ type: '', text: '' }), 3000);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
      await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'users',
          id: user.uid,
          data: { portfolio: updatedList, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });
      await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'public_profiles',
          id: user.uid,
          data: { portfolio: updatedList, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }

    try {
      await withTimeout(
        Promise.all([
          setDoc(doc(db, 'users', user.uid), {
            portfolio: updatedList,
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
          }, { merge: true }),
          setDoc(doc(db, 'public_profiles', user.uid), {
            portfolio: updatedList,
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
          }, { merge: true })
        ]),
        5000,
        'Koneksi timeout saat menghapus portofolio'
      );
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.warn('Cloud Firestore portfolio remove fallback:', err);
    }
    await refreshProfile();
    setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Item portofolio berhasil dihapus.' });
    setTimeout(() => setMsg({ type: '', text: '' }), 3000);
  };

  const handleUploadCV = async () => {
    if (!cvFile) return;

    if (cvFile.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Ukuran file CV melebihi batas maksimal 5MB.' });
      return;
    }

    setUploadingCV(true);
    setMsg({ type: '', text: '' });

    if (isDemo || !user) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        setUploadingCV(false);
        const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(cvFile);
        setSavedProfile((prev) => {
          const updated = { ...(prev || currentProfile || DEMO_PROFILE), cvFileUrl: objectUrl };
          if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
            localStorage.setItem('mmtc_demo_profile', JSON.stringify(updated));
          }
          return updated as ExtendedUserProfile;
        });
        setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'File CV PDF berhasil disimpan!' });
        setCvFile(null);
        setTimeout(() => setMsg({ type: '', text: '' }), 4000);
      }, 600);
      return;
    }

    try {
      let downloadUrl = '';
      // 1. Upload via Server DB (/api/db)
      try {
        const dataUrl = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
          const reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result || ''));
          reader.onerror = (e) => reject(e);
          reader.readAsDataURL(cvFile);
        });
        const upRes = await fetch('/api/db', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action: 'uploadFile',
            folder: `cv_${user.uid}`,
            fileName: 'cv.pdf',
            dataUrl
          })
        });
        if (upRes.ok) {
          const upJson = await upRes.json();
          if (upJson.url) downloadUrl = upJson.url;
        }
      } catch {
        // fallback below
      }

      // 2. Fallback / parallel upload to Firebase Storage
      if (!downloadUrl) {
        const storageRef = ref(storage, `cv_documents/${user.uid}/cv.pdf`);
        await withTimeout(uploadBytes(storageRef, cvFile, { contentType: 'application/pdf' }), 15000, 'Koneksi timeout saat upload file CV');
        downloadUrl = await getDownloadURL(storageRef);
      }

      const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
      await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'users',
          id: user.uid,
          data: { cvFileUrl: downloadUrl, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });
      await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'public_profiles',
          id: user.uid,
          data: { hasCv: true, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });

      try {
        await withTimeout(
          Promise.all([
            setDoc(doc(db, 'users', user.uid), {
              cvFileUrl: downloadUrl,
              updatedAt: nowIso
            }, { merge: true }),
            setDoc(doc(db, 'public_profiles', user.uid), {
              hasCv: true,
              updatedAt: nowIso
            }, { merge: true })
          ]),
          6000,
          'Koneksi timeout saat memperbarui data CV di profil'
        );
      } catch {
        // ignore if cloud rules block
      }
      
      setSavedProfile((prev) => {
        const updated = { ...(prev || currentProfile || DEMO_PROFILE), cvFileUrl: downloadUrl };
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          localStorage.setItem(`mmtc_profile_${user.uid}`, JSON.stringify(updated));
          localStorage.setItem('mmtc_demo_profile', JSON.stringify(updated));
        }
        return updated as ExtendedUserProfile;
      });

      await refreshProfile();
      setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'File CV berhasil diunggah dan disimpan!' });
      setCvFile(null);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const e = err as Error;
      setMsg({ type: 'error', text: e.message || 'Gagal mengupload file CV.' });
    } finally {
      setUploadingCV(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAvatarUpload = async (file: File) => {
    if (!file) return;

    if (file.size > 3 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Ukuran foto profil melebihi batas maksimal 3MB.' });
      return;
    }

    setUploadingAvatar(true);
    setMsg({ type: '', text: '' });

    if (isDemo || !user) {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => {
        const dataUrl = reader.result as string;
        setSavedProfile((prev) => {
          const updated = { ...(prev || currentProfile || DEMO_PROFILE), fotoProfil: dataUrl };
          if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
            localStorage.setItem('mmtc_demo_profile', JSON.stringify(updated));
          }
          if (setProfileData) {
            setProfileData(updated as UserProfile);
          }
          return updated as ExtendedUserProfile;
        });
        setUploadingAvatar(false);
        setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Foto profil berhasil diperbarui!' });
        setTimeout(() => setMsg({ type: '', text: '' }), 4000);
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const rawExt = (file.name.split('.').pop() || 'jpg').toLowerCase();
      const cleanExt = ['jpeg', 'png', 'webp'].includes(rawExt) ? rawExt : 'jpg';
      let downloadUrl = '';

      try {
        const dataUrl = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
          const reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result || ''));
          reader.onerror = (e) => reject(e);
          reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        });
        const upRes = await fetch('/api/db', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action: 'uploadFile',
            folder: `avatars_${user.uid}`,
            fileName: `avatar.${cleanExt}`,
            dataUrl
          })
        });
        if (upRes.ok) {
          const upJson = await upRes.json();
          if (upJson.url) downloadUrl = upJson.url;
        }
      } catch {
        // fallback below
      }

      if (!downloadUrl) {
        const storageRef = ref(storage, `avatars/${user.uid}/avatar.${cleanExt}`);
        await withTimeout(
          uploadBytes(storageRef, file, { contentType: file.type || `image/${cleanExt === 'jpg' ? 'jpeg' : cleanExt}` }),
          15000,
          'Koneksi timeout saat upload foto profil'
        );
        downloadUrl = await getDownloadURL(storageRef);
      }

      const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
      await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'users',
          id: user.uid,
          data: { fotoProfil: downloadUrl, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });
      await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'public_profiles',
          id: user.uid,
          data: { fotoProfil: downloadUrl, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });

      try {
        await withTimeout(
          Promise.all([
            setDoc(doc(db, 'users', user.uid), {
              fotoProfil: downloadUrl,
              updatedAt: nowIso
            }, { merge: true }),
            setDoc(doc(db, 'public_profiles', user.uid), {
              fotoProfil: downloadUrl,
              updatedAt: nowIso
            }, { merge: true })
          ]),
          6000,
          'Koneksi timeout saat menyimpan URL foto profil'
        );
      } catch {
        // ignore if cloud rules block
      }

      setSavedProfile((prev) => {
        const updated = { ...(prev || currentProfile || DEMO_PROFILE), fotoProfil: downloadUrl };
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          localStorage.setItem(`mmtc_profile_${user.uid}`, JSON.stringify(updated));
          localStorage.setItem('mmtc_demo_profile', JSON.stringify(updated));
        }
        if (setProfileData) {
          setProfileData(updated as UserProfile);
        }
        return updated as ExtendedUserProfile;
      });

      await refreshProfile();
      setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Foto profil berhasil diperbarui!' });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const e = err as Error;
      setMsg({ type: 'error', text: e.message || 'Gagal mengupload foto profil.' });
    } finally {
      setUploadingAvatar(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveProfile = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setMsg({ type: '', text: '' });

    const trimmedCustomLinks = (customLinks || [])
      .filter((l) => (l.title || '').trim() !== '' || (l.url || '').trim() !== '')
      .map((l) => ({
        id: l.id || Date.now().toString(),
        title: (l.title || '').trim(),
        url: (l.url || '').trim()
      }));

    const cleanNama = (namaLengkap || '').trim() || profile?.namaLengkap || user?.displayName || 'Alumni MMTC';

    // F-08, F-22: Omit locked fields (nomorAlumni, prodi, angkatan, role, uid, createdAt) to comply with rules
    const usersUpdateData: Record<string, unknown> = {
      namaLengkap: cleanNama,
      openToWork: Boolean(openToWork),
      pekerjaanSaatIni: (pekerjaanSaatIni || '').trim(),
      domisili: (domisili || '').trim(),
      instagramUrl: (instagramUrl || '').trim(),
      customLinks: trimmedCustomLinks,
      bio: (bio || '').trim(),
      phone: (phone || '').trim(),
      linkedinUrl: (linkedinUrl || '').trim(),
      cvExternalLink: (cvExternalLink || '').trim(),
      portfolio: portfolioList,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    const fullProfile: ExtendedUserProfile = {
      uid: user ? user.uid : 'demo-alumni-001',
      nomorAlumni: profile?.nomorAlumni || currentProfile?.nomorAlumni || 'MMTC-2020-103',
      prodi: profile?.prodi || prodi || 'Animasi',
      angkatan: profile?.angkatan || angkatan || '2020',
      email: profile?.email || user?.email || 'alumni@mmtc.ac.id',
      fotoProfil: profile?.fotoProfil || currentProfile?.fotoProfil || '',
      cvFileUrl: profile?.cvFileUrl || currentProfile?.cvFileUrl || '',
      ...usersUpdateData
    } as ExtendedUserProfile;

    // 1. Simpan segera ke localStorage agar tersimpan di browser
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      try {
        const storageKey = user ? `mmtc_profile_${user.uid}` : 'mmtc_demo_profile';
        localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(fullProfile));
        localStorage.setItem('mmtc_demo_profile', JSON.stringify(fullProfile));
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event('alumnihub_profile_updated'));
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('LocalStorage save error:', err);
      }
    }

    // 2. Update React State & AuthContext profile data segera
    setSavedProfile(fullProfile);
    if (setProfileData) {
      setProfileData(fullProfile as UserProfile);
    }

    // 3. Jika user login ke Firebase, simpan ke Server DB (/api/db) & Cloud Firestore (users privat & public_profiles whitelist)
    if (user) {
      try {
        const publicProfileData: Record<string, unknown> = {
          uid: user.uid,
          nomorAlumni: fullProfile.nomorAlumni,
          namaLengkap: cleanNama,
          prodi: profile?.prodi || prodi || 'Animasi',
          angkatan: profile?.angkatan || angkatan || '2020',
          openToWork: Boolean(openToWork),
          pekerjaanSaatIni: (pekerjaanSaatIni || '').trim(),
          domisili: (domisili || '').trim(),
          customLinks: trimmedCustomLinks,
          bio: (bio || '').trim(),
          portfolio: portfolioList,
          fotoProfil: profile?.fotoProfil || currentProfile?.fotoProfil || '',
          cvExternalLink: (cvExternalLink || '').trim(),
          hasCv: Boolean(profile?.cvFileUrl || (cvExternalLink || '').trim()),
          updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        };

        try {
          await fetch('/api/db', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              action: 'setDoc',
              collection: 'users',
              id: user.uid,
              data: fullProfile,
              merge: true
            })
          });
          await fetch('/api/db', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              action: 'setDoc',
              collection: 'public_profiles',
              id: user.uid,
              data: {
                ...publicProfileData
              },
              merge: true
            })
          });
        } catch {
          // ignore local api error
        }

        try {
          await withTimeout(
            Promise.all([
              setDoc(doc(db, 'users', user.uid), usersUpdateData, { merge: true }),
              setDoc(doc(db, 'public_profiles', user.uid), publicProfileData, { merge: true })
            ]),
            7000,
            'Koneksi timeout saat menyimpan ke cloud database'
          );
        } catch {
          // Handled by /api/db server persistence
        }

        try {
          await refreshProfile();
        } catch {
          // Abaikan latency refresh REST
        }
        setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Profil alumni berhasil diperbarui dan tersimpan di database.' });
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        console.warn('Cloud save error:', err);
        setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Profil alumni berhasil diperbarui dan tersimpan.' });
      } finally {
        setSaving(false);
        setTimeout(() => setMsg({ type: '', text: '' }), 5000);
      }
    } else {
      setTimeout(() => {
        setSaving(false);
        setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Perubahan profil, kontak, dan portofolio berhasil disimpan.' });
        setTimeout(() => setMsg({ type: '', text: '' }), 5000);
      }, 400);
    }
  };


  const renderEmbed = (item: PortfolioItem) => {
    if (item.type === 'youtube') {
      const videoId = parseYouTubeVideoId(item.url);

      return videoId ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="aspect-video w-full rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-black shadow-xs">
            <iframe
              src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${videoId}`}
              className="w-full h-full border-0"
              title={item.title || 'YouTube video player'}
              allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share"
              referrerPolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"
              allowFullScreen
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-0.5">
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium">YouTube Video</span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCinemaVideo({ videoId, title: item.title || 'Showreel Alumni' })}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-sky-50 text-slate-700 hover:text-[#0284c7] border border-slate-200 hover:border-sky-200 text-[11px] font-semibold transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3 text-[#0284c7]" />
              <span>Preview Mode Bioskop</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 rounded-xl text-xs text-red-600 border border-red-100">
          Format URL YouTube tidak dikenali. Masukkan tautan video YouTube yang valid.
        </div>
      );
    }

    if (item.type === 'image') {
      return <PortfolioImagePreview url={item.url} title={item.title} />;
    }

    return (
      <a 
        href={item.url.startsWith('http') ? item.url : `https://${item.url}`} 
        target="_blank" 
        rel="noopener noreferrer"
        className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-white hover:bg-sky-50 border border-slate-200 hover:border-sky-200 rounded-2xl transition group"
      >
        <div className="space-y-0.5">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-[#0284c7] transition truncate block max-w-[200px]">
            {item.title}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 capitalize block">
            Platform: {item.type}
          </span>
        </div>
        <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-[#0284c7] transition shrink-0" />
      </a>
    );
  };

  if (authLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex flex-col justify-between">
        <Navbar />
        <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10">
          <ProfileHeaderSkeleton />
        </main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafcff] text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-10">
        
        {/* DEMO / PREVIEW BANNER JIKA DIAKSES TANPA LOGIN */}
        {isDemo && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-2xs">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <User className="w-4 h-4" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">Pratinjau Halaman Profil</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  Masuk dengan akun alumni untuk menyimpan perubahan biodata, unggahan CV, dan daftar karya showreel.
