                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/login"
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-[#0369a1] transition shrink-0 text-center"
            >
              Masuk Akun
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* PROFILE HEADER CARD */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 mb-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
            <div className="relative group">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-3xl font-bold shadow-xs overflow-hidden border-2 border-white shrink-0">
                <AlumniAvatar src={currentProfile?.fotoProfil} name={namaLengkap || currentProfile?.namaLengkap} />
              </div>

              <label 
                htmlFor="avatar-upload-input" 
                className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 rounded-2xl opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition duration-200 flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-pointer text-white text-[10px] font-semibold gap-1"
                title="Ganti Foto Profil"
              >
                {uploadingAvatar ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin text-white" />
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Camera className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
                    <span>Ubah Foto</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </label>

              <input
                id="avatar-upload-input"
                type="file"
                accept="image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp"
                className="hidden"
                disabled={uploadingAvatar}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                  if (file) handleAvatarUpload(file);
                }}
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">
                {namaLengkap || currentProfile?.namaLengkap}
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#0284c7] font-bold mt-1">
                {prodi} • Angkatan {angkatan || currentProfile?.angkatan}
              </p>
              {pekerjaanSaatIni && (
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Briefcase className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                  <span>{pekerjaanSaatIni}</span>
                </p>
              )}
              {domisili && (
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />
                  <span>{domisili}</span>
                </p>
              )}
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5">
                Nomor Alumni: {currentProfile?.nomorAlumni}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 w-full sm:w-auto">
            <Link
              href={currentProfile?.uid ? `/profil/${currentProfile.uid}` : '/open-to-work'}
              target="_blank"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 transition shadow-2xs"
            >
              <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
              <span>Lihat Profil Publik</span>
            </Link>

            <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 cursor-pointer text-xs font-bold select-none hover:bg-slate-100/70 transition">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={openToWork}
                onChange={(e) => setOpenToWork(e.target.checked)}
                className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7] rounded bg-white border-slate-300 focus:ring-[#0284c7]"
              />
              <span className={openToWork ? 'text-emerald-700 font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5' : 'text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1.5'}>
                {openToWork ? 'Status: Open to Work' : 'Status: Tidak Mencari'}
              </span>
            </label>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* FEEDBACK TOAST / ALERT */}
        {msg.text && (
          <div className={`mb-6 p-4 rounded-2xl text-xs flex items-center gap-2.5 border animate-fade-in ${
            msg.type === 'success' 
              ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800' 
              : 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-800'
          }`}>
            {msg.type === 'success' ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
            ) : (
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 shrink-0" />
            )}
            <span className="font-semibold">{msg.text}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* MAIN LAYOUT: SIDEBAR PINNED TO THE LEFT, SHOWREELS/PORTFOLIO TO THE RIGHT */}
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-start gap-8">
          
          {/* LEFT SIDEBAR: PINNED TO LEFT (320px fixed) */}
          <div className="w-full lg:w-80 shrink-0 space-y-6">
            
            {/* CV UPLOAD & LINK */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-4 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                Resume &amp; CV Alumni
              </h3>

              {currentProfile?.cvFileUrl ? (
                <div className="p-3.5 bg-sky-50/80 border border-sky-100 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="text-slate-700 truncate max-w-[130px] font-bold">CV PDF Tersimpan</span>
                  <a 
                    href={currentProfile.cvFileUrl} 
                    target="_blank" 
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    className="text-[#0284c7] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1 font-bold"
                  >
                    Buka PDF <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                  </a>
                </div>
              ) : null}

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">Unggah File PDF CV (Maks 5MB)</label>
                <input 
                  type="file" 
                  accept=".pdf"
                  onChange={(e) => setCvFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)}
                  className="block w-full text-xs text-slate-500 file:mr-2 file:py-1.5 file:px-3 file:rounded-full file:border-0 file:text-xs file:font-bold file:bg-[#0284c7] file:text-white hover:file:bg-[#0369a1] cursor-pointer"
                />
                {cvFile && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleUploadCV}
                    disabled={uploadingCV}
                    className="w-full py-2.5 mt-1 rounded-full bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-xs font-bold text-white flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition shadow-xs"
                  >
                    {uploadingCV ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                    Simpan ke Cloud Storage
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">Atau Tautan Google Drive / Cloud</label>
                <input
                  type="url"
                  value={cvExternalLink}
                  onChange={(e) => setCvExternalLink(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="https://drive.google.com/..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* CONTACT, LOCATION & SOCIAL LINKS */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-3.5 text-xs shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 mb-2">
                Kontak &amp; Tautan Profil
              </h3>

              {/* DOMISILI SAAT INI */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500" />
                  Kota Domisili Saat Ini
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={domisili}
                  onChange={(e) => setDomisili(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Contoh: Sleman, D.I. Yogyakarta / Jakarta"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
              </div>
              
              {/* WHATSAPP */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                  Nomor WhatsApp / HP
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={phone}
                  onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="081234567890"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
              </div>

              {/* LINKEDIN */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                  LinkedIn Profil URL
                </label>
                <input
                  type="url"
                  value={linkedinUrl}
                  onChange={(e) => setLinkedinUrl(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="https://linkedin.com/in/..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
              </div>

              {/* INSTAGRAM */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <InstagramIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-pink-600" />
                  Instagram Portofolio / Akun
                </label>
                <input
                  type="url"
                  value={instagramUrl}
                  onChange={(e) => setInstagramUrl(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="https://instagram.com/username"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
              </div>

              {/* TAUTAN KUSTOM TAMBAHAN */}
              <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-sky-600" />
                    Tautan Tambahan ({customLinks.length})
                  </span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleAddCustomLink}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-sky-50 hover:bg-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] font-bold text-[11px] transition cursor-pointer border border-sky-100"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>Tambah Link</span>
                  </button>
                </div>

                {customLinks.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 italic bg-slate-50 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 text-center">
                    Belum ada tautan tambahan. Klik &quot;Tambah Link&quot; di atas untuk menambahkan judul dan tautan.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {customLinks.map((link, idx) => (
                      <div key={link.id || idx} className="p-3 bg-slate-50/80 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 space-y-2 relative group">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            value={link.title}
                            onChange={(e) => handleUpdateCustomLink(link.id, 'title', e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Judul Link (cth: ArtStation, YouTube, GitHub)"
                            className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-900 font-bold placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] transition"
                          />
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleRemoveCustomLink(link.id)}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 transition shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                            title="Hapus Link"
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                        <input
                          type="url"
                          value={link.url}
                          onChange={(e) => handleUpdateCustomLink(link.id, 'url', e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="https://..."
                          className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] transition"
                        />
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT CONTENT: BIO & SHOWREEL EMBEDS (SUSUN 2 2 / 2 COLUMNS) */}
          <div className="flex-1 w-full space-y-6">
            
            {/* PERSONAL DETAILS & BIO FORM */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-4 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900">
                Informasi Biodata &amp; Keahlian
              </h3>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3.5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Nama Lengkap</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={namaLengkap}
                    onChange={(e) => setNamaLengkap(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Nama Lengkap"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition font-medium"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Program Studi</label>
                  <select
                    value={prodi}
                    onChange={(e) => setProdi(e.target.value as ProdiType)}
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition font-medium"
                  >
                    {PRODI_LIST.map((p) => (
                      <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                        {p.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Tahun Angkatan</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={angkatan}
                    onChange={(e) => setAngkatan(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="2020"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition font-medium"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Briefcase className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                  Posisi & Studio / Tempat Kerja Saat Ini
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={pekerjaanSaatIni}
                  onChange={(e) => setPekerjaanSaatIni(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. 3D Animator di Infinite Studios, Broadcast Director di MNC Media, atau Freelance 3D Artist"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition font-medium"
                />
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">
                  Info ini akan tampil di kartu profil Anda pada Beranda, Feed Komunitas, dan Katalog Talenta.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Bio &amp; Ringkasan Portofolio</label>
                <textarea
                  rows={3}
                  value={bio}
                  onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ceritakan keahlianmu, software yang dikuasai (Blender, Premiere, Unity, Maya), dan tipe proyek yang dicari..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition leading-relaxed"
                />

                {/* DETECTED SKILLS CHIPS PREVIEW */}
                <div className="mt-2.5 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-sky-50/50 border border-sky-100">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      Keahlian &amp; Software (Disarikan dari Bio):
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Tampil di katalog Open to Work</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {extractSkills(bio, prodi).map((skill) => (
                      <span
                        key={skill}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-sky-200/80 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#0284c7] shadow-2xs"
                      >
                        {skill}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* PORTFOLIO ITEMS SECTION (SHOWREEL GALLERY 2 KOLOM) */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-6 shadow-sm">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3.5">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Video className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                    Karya &amp; Showreel Pilihan
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                    Tambahkan video YouTube, karya visual, atau tautan portofolio eksternal Anda
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* INPUT FORM ADD ITEM */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-sky-50/60 border border-sky-100 space-y-3">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Platform Karya</label>
                    <select
                      value={portType}
                      onChange={(e) => setPortType(e.target.value as 'youtube' | 'pinterest' | 'behance' | 'image' | 'link')}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7]"
                    >
                      <option value="youtube">YouTube (Showreel)</option>
                      <option value="behance">Behance (Project)</option>
                      <option value="pinterest">Pinterest (Board/Pin)</option>
                      <option value="image">Direct Image Link</option>
                      <option value="link">Website Eksternal</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Judul Karya / Showreel</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={portTitle}
                      onChange={(e) => setPortTitle(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="e.g. 3D Character Animation Reel 2026"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7]"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Tautan / Kode Embed
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={portUrl}
                      onChange={(e) => setPortUrl(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Paste link YouTube (https://youtu.be/...), iframe embed, link Behance, atau Pinterest"
                      className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7]"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleAddPortfolio}
                      disabled={!portTitle.trim() || !portUrl.trim()}
                      className="px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] disabled:opacity-40 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 transition shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      Tambah
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* LIST OF PORTFOLIO: SUSUN 2 2 (2 COLUMNS) */}
              {portfolioList.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-12 border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-2xl text-slate-400 text-xs space-y-1">
                  <p className="font-bold text-slate-600">Belum ada item portofolio yang ditambahkan.</p>
                  <p className="text-slate-400">Masukkan tautan video showreel YouTube atau link portofolio Anda di atas.</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                  {portfolioList.map((item) => (
                    <div key={item.id} className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/90 border border-slate-200/90 space-y-3 shadow-2xs">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200/60 pb-2">
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 truncate" title={item.title}>
                          {item.title}
                        </span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleRemovePortfolio(item.id)}
                          className="text-slate-400 hover:text-red-600 p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-white transition cursor-pointer"
                          title="Hapus Karya"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                      <div className="w-full">
                        {renderEmbed(item)}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* SAVE BUTTON & BOTTOM FEEDBACK */}
            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="flex-1">
                {msg.text && (
                  <div className={`p-3 rounded-2xl text-xs flex items-center gap-2.5 border animate-fade-in ${
                    msg.type === 'success' 
                      ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800' 
                      : 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-800'
                  }`}>
                    {msg.type === 'success' ? (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                    ) : (
                      <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 shrink-0" />
                    )}
                    <span className="font-bold">{msg.text}</span>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleSaveProfile}
                disabled={saving}
                className="px-8 py-3.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] hover:from-sky-600 hover:to-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-sky-500/20 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50 transition active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer shrink-0"
              >
                {saving ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                    Menyimpan Perubahan...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
                    Simpan Semua Perubahan Profil
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CINEMA LIGHTBOX PREVIEW */}
        <CinemaLightbox
          isOpen={Boolean(cinemaVideo)}
          onClose={() => setCinemaVideo(null)}
          title={cinemaVideo?.title || 'Preview Showreel'}
          videoId={cinemaVideo?.videoId || ''}
        />
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\app\profil\[uid]\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, use } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { db } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { doc, getDoc } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { UserProfile, PortfolioItem } from '@/types';
import { parseYouTubeVideoId, formatWhatsAppUrl } from '@/lib/media';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';
import { 
  FileText, 
  ExternalLink, 
  ArrowLeft, 
  Phone, 
  Globe, 
  Mail, 
  Video,
  User,
  Share2,
  Maximize2,
  Briefcase,
  MapPin
} from 'lucide-react';
import AlumniAvatar from '@/components/profile/AlumniAvatar';
import { ProfileHeaderSkeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeleton';
import { OpenToWorkBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';
import { CinemaLightbox } from '@/components/ui/Modal';
import { ShareModal } from '@/components/ui/ShareModal';
import { extractSkills } from '@/components/profile/skills';
import AlumniActivityHistory from '@/components/profile/AlumniActivityHistory';

const InstagramIcon = ({ className = "w-4 h-4 text-pink-600" }: { className?: string }) => (
  <svg className={className} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
    <rect width="20" height="20" x="2" y="2" rx="5" ry="5" />
    <path d="M16 11.37A4 4 0 1 1 12.63 8 4 4 0 0 1 16 11.37z" />
    <line x1="17.5" x2="17.51" y1="6.5" y2="6.5" />
  </svg>
);

export interface CustomLinkItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  url: string;
}

interface ExtendedUserProfile extends UserProfile {
  pekerjaanSaatIni?: string;
  pekerjaan?: string;
  domisili?: string;
  instagramUrl?: string;
  customLinks?: CustomLinkItem[];
}

// Komponen pratinjau portofolio gambar di luar fungsi render (React 19 pattern)
function PortfolioImagePreview({ url, title }: { url: string; title: string }) {
  const [hasError, setHasError] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 max-h-64 flex items-center justify-center p-2">
      {hasError ? (
        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 py-6">Gambar tidak dapat dimuat</span>
      ) : (
        <img 
          src={url} 
          alt={title} 
          className="w-full h-full max-h-60 object-contain rounded-lg" 
          onError={() => setHasError(true)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function PublicProfilePage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ uid: string }> }) {
  const resolvedParams = use(params);
  const rawUid = resolvedParams.uid;

  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isShareOpen, setIsShareOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cinemaVideo, setCinemaVideo] = useState<{ videoId: string; title: string } | null>(null);

  const isOwner = Boolean(user && profile && user.uid === profile.uid);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadProfile() {
      if (!rawUid) return;
      const cleanUid = decodeURIComponent(rawUid).trim();
      setLoading(true);

      try {
        let loaded = false;

        // 0. Coba ambil dari Server DB (/api/db) terlebih dahulu
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          try {
            const resUser = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=users&id=${encodeURIComponent(cleanUid)}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
            if (resUser.ok) {
              const jsonUser = await resUser.json();
              if (jsonUser.doc) {
                setProfile({ uid: cleanUid, ...jsonUser.doc } as UserProfile);
                loaded = true;
              }
            }
            if (!loaded) {
              const resPub = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=public_profiles&id=${encodeURIComponent(cleanUid)}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
              if (resPub.ok) {
                const jsonPub = await resPub.json();
                if (jsonPub.doc) {
                  setProfile({ uid: cleanUid, ...jsonPub.doc } as UserProfile);
                  loaded = true;
                }
              }
            }
          } catch {
            // fallback below
          }
        }

        // 1. Coba lookup langsung berdasarkan Document ID (UID Firebase Auth)
        if (!loaded) {
          try {
            const docRef = doc(db, 'users', cleanUid);
            const timeout = new Promise<never>((_, reject) =>
              setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Profile fetch timeout')), 3500)
            );
            const snap = await Promise.race([getDoc(docRef), timeout]);
            if (snap && snap.exists()) {
              setProfile({ uid: snap.id, ...snap.data() } as UserProfile);
              loaded = true;
            }
          } catch {
            // users fetch blocked by rules if not owner/admin
          }
        }

        // 2. Fallback ke public_profiles whitelist (non-PII publik)
        if (!loaded) {
          try {
            const pubRef = doc(db, 'public_profiles', cleanUid);
            const pubSnap = await getDoc(pubRef);
            if (pubSnap && pubSnap.exists()) {
              setProfile({ uid: pubSnap.id, ...pubSnap.data() } as UserProfile);
              loaded = true;
            }
          } catch (pubErr) {
            console.warn('public_profiles lookup fallback failed:', pubErr);
          }
        }

        // 3. Fallback lookup demo atau cache lokal jika sedang mode demo / offline preview
        if (!loaded && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          try {
            const localCached = localStorage.getItem(`mmtc_profile_${cleanUid}`) || 
              (cleanUid === 'demo-alumni-001' || cleanUid.startsWith('demo') ? localStorage.getItem('mmtc_demo_profile') : null);
            if (localCached) {
              const parsed = JSON.parse(localCached);
              setProfile({ uid: cleanUid, ...parsed } as UserProfile);
              loaded = true;
            }
          } catch {
            // ignore
          }
        }

        if (!loaded) {
          setProfile(null);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error loadProfile:', err);
        setProfile(null);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
    loadProfile();
  }, [rawUid]);


  const renderEmbed = (item: PortfolioItem) => {
    if (item.type === 'youtube') {
      const videoId = parseYouTubeVideoId(item.url);

      return videoId ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="aspect-video w-full rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-black shadow-xs">
            <iframe
              src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${videoId}`}
              className="w-full h-full border-0"
              title={item.title || 'YouTube video player'}
              allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share"
              referrerPolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"
              allowFullScreen
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-0.5">
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium">YouTube Video</span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCinemaVideo({ videoId, title: item.title || 'Showreel Alumni' })}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-sky-50 text-slate-700 hover:text-[#0284c7] border border-slate-200 hover:border-sky-200 text-[11px] font-semibold transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3 text-[#0284c7]" />
              <span>Mode Bioskop</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 rounded-xl text-xs text-red-600 border border-red-100">
          Format URL atau tag Embed YouTube tidak dikenali.
        </div>
      );
    }

    if (item.type === 'image') {
      return <PortfolioImagePreview url={item.url} title={item.title} />;
    }

    return (
      <a 
        href={item.url.startsWith('http') ? item.url : `https://${item.url}`} 
        target="_blank" 
        rel="noopener noreferrer"
        className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-sky-50/60 border border-slate-200 hover:border-sky-200 rounded-xl transition group"
      >
        <div className="space-y-0.5">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-[#0284c7] transition truncate block max-w-[240px]">
            {item.title}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 capitalize block">
            Platform: {item.type}
          </span>
        </div>
        <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-[#0284c7] transition shrink-0" />
      </a>
    );
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex flex-col justify-between">
        <Navbar />
        <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10">
          <ProfileHeaderSkeleton />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            <div className="h-64 bg-white rounded-2xl animate-pulse border border-slate-200" />
            <div className="lg:col-span-2 h-96 bg-white rounded-2xl animate-pulse border border-slate-200" />
          </div>
        </main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!profile) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between">
        <Navbar />
        <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="text-center space-y-4 max-w-md bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-slate-400">
              <User className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Profil Alumni Tidak Ditemukan</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Akun alumni yang kamu cari tidak terdaftar atau telah dinonaktifkan dari sistem.
            </p>
            <Link 
              href="/open-to-work" 
              className="inline-block px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-[#0369a1] transition shadow-xs"
            >
              Kembali ke Katalog Open to Work
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-10">
        {/* TOP NAVIGATION / BREADCRUMB */}
        <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link 
            href="/open-to-work" 
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 hover:text-[#0284c7] transition"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Kembali ke Katalog Open to Work
          </Link>

          <button
            onClick={() => setIsShareOpen(true)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition shadow-2xs cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
            <span>Bagikan Profil</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* PROFILE HEADER CARD */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 mb-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-6 shadow-2xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-3xl font-bold shadow-2xs overflow-hidden border-2 border-white shrink-0">
              <AlumniAvatar src={profile.fotoProfil} name={profile.namaLengkap} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">
                {profile.namaLengkap}
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#0284c7] font-semibold mt-1">
                {profile.prodi} • Angkatan {profile.angkatan}
              </p>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mt-1.5">
                {((profile as ExtendedUserProfile).pekerjaanSaatIni || (profile as ExtendedUserProfile).pekerjaan) && (
                  <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200/80 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">
                    <Briefcase className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                    <span>Bekerja di: {(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).pekerjaanSaatIni || (profile as ExtendedUserProfile).pekerjaan}</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                {(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).domisili && (
                  <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/80 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">
                    <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500" />
                    <span>{(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).domisili}</span>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono mt-1">
                Nomor Alumni: {profile.nomorAlumni}
              </p>

              {/* TECHNICAL SKILL BADGES */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mt-3">
                {extractSkills(profile.bio || '', profile.prodi || '').map((skill) => (
                  <span
                    key={skill}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100 text-[11px] font-medium text-[#0284c7]"
                  >
                    {skill}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            {profile.openToWork ? (
              <OpenToWorkBadge />
            ) : (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-slate-500 text-xs font-semibold">
                Sedang Tidak Mencari Proyek
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* MAIN TWO-COLUMN LAYOUT: SIDEBAR PINNED TO THE LEFT, EMBEDS EXPANDED TO THE RIGHT */}
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-start gap-8">
          
          {/* LEFT SIDEBAR: PINNED TO LEFT (Width fixed: 320px) */}
          <div className="w-full lg:w-80 shrink-0 space-y-6">
            
            {/* CV BOX */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-3.5 shadow-2xs">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                Resume &amp; Dokumen
              </h3>

              {!isOwner && !profile.openToWork ? (
                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Dokumen dan resume disembunyikan karena alumni sedang tidak mencari proyek (Open to Work non-aktif).
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  {profile.cvFileUrl ? (
                    <a 
                      href={profile.cvFileUrl} 
                      target="_blank" 
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="w-full p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 hover:bg-sky-100 border border-sky-100 text-[#0284c7] text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between transition group"
                    >
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
                        Buka CV Resmi (PDF)
                      </span>
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition" />
                    </a>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Alumni belum mengunggah file CV PDF.</p>
                  )}

                  {profile.cvExternalLink && (
                    <a 
                      href={profile.cvExternalLink.startsWith('http') ? profile.cvExternalLink : `https://${profile.cvExternalLink}`} 
                      target="_blank" 
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="w-full p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-between transition group"
                    >
                      <span>Link Portfolio / Google Drive</span>
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition" />
                    </a>
                  )}
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* CONTACT CARD */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-4 text-xs shadow-2xs">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900">
                Kontak Alumni
              </h3>
              
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).domisili && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-700 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                    <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />
                    <span className="truncate font-medium">
                      {(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).domisili}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-600 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                  <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7] shrink-0" />
                  <a href={`mailto:${profile.email}`} className="hover:text-[#0284c7] hover:underline truncate font-medium">
                    {profile.email}
                  </a>
                </div>

                {profile.phone && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-600 p-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50/60 border border-emerald-100">
                    <Phone className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                    <a 
                      href={formatWhatsAppUrl(profile.phone)} 
                      target="_blank" 
                      rel="noreferrer" 
                      className="hover:text-emerald-700 hover:underline font-semibold text-emerald-800 truncate"
                    >
                      WhatsApp: {profile.phone}
                    </a>
                  </div>
                )}

                {profile.linkedinUrl && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-600 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                    <Globe className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7] shrink-0" />
                    <a 
                      href={profile.linkedinUrl.startsWith('http') ? profile.linkedinUrl : `https://${profile.linkedinUrl}`} 
                      target="_blank" 
                      rel="noreferrer" 
                      className="hover:text-[#0284c7] hover:underline truncate font-medium"
                    >
                      LinkedIn Profil
                    </a>
                  </div>
                )}

                {(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).instagramUrl && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-600 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                    <InstagramIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-pink-600 shrink-0" />
                    <a 
                      href={(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).instagramUrl!.startsWith('http') ? (profile as ExtendedUserProfile).instagramUrl : `https://${(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).instagramUrl}`} 
                      target="_blank" 
                      rel="noreferrer" 
                      className="hover:text-pink-600 hover:underline truncate font-medium"
                    >
                      Instagram Portofolio
                    </a>
                  </div>
                )}

                {(profile as ExtendedUserProfile).customLinks?.map((link) => (
                  <div key={link.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-600 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                    <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 text-sky-600 shrink-0" />
                    <a 
                      href={link.url.startsWith('http') ? link.url : `https://${link.url}`} 
                      target="_blank" 
                      rel="noreferrer" 
                      className="hover:text-[#0284c7] hover:underline truncate font-medium flex-1"
                    >
                      {link.title || 'Tautan Eksternal'}
                    </a>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT CONTENT: BIO & SHOWREEL EMBEDS (SUSUN 2 KOLOM DI DESKTOP) */}
          <div className="flex-1 w-full space-y-6">
            {profile.bio && (
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-2.5 shadow-2xs">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900">
                  Tentang Alumni / Bio Singkat
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                  {profile.bio}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* SHOWREELS EMBED SECTION: SUSUN 2 KOLOM DESKTOP */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-6 shadow-2xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Video className="w-5 h-5 text-[#0284c7]" />
                  <h3 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                    Galeri Showreel &amp; Portofolio Karya
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium">
                  {profile.portfolio?.length || 0} Karya Tersemat
                </span>
              </div>

              {profile.portfolio?.length ? (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                  {profile.portfolio.map((item) => (
                    <div 
                      key={item.id} 
                      className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/90 border border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 transition space-y-3 shadow-2xs flex flex-col justify-between"
                    >
                      <div className="space-y-2.5">
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 block truncate" title={item.title}>
                          {item.title}
                        </span>
                        <div className="w-full">
                          {renderEmbed(item)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-center py-14 text-slate-400 text-xs border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-2xl space-y-2">
                  <Video className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-300 mx-auto" />
                  <p className="font-semibold text-slate-600">Alumni belum menyematkan karya showreel.</p>
                  <p className="text-slate-400">Portofolio video YouTube, Pinterest, atau Behance akan tampil di sini.</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* AKTIVITAS & RIWAYAT POSTINGAN ALUMNI (LINKEDIN / FACEBOOK STYLE) */}
            <AlumniActivityHistory 
              authorUid={rawUid}
              authorName={profile.namaLengkap}
              authorProdi={profile.prodi}
              authorAvatar={profile.fotoProfil}
              isOwner={false}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CINEMA LIGHTBOX MODAL */}
        <CinemaLightbox
          isOpen={Boolean(cinemaVideo)}
          onClose={() => setCinemaVideo(null)}
          title={cinemaVideo?.title || 'Showreel Alumni STMM MMTC'}
          videoId={cinemaVideo?.videoId || ''}
        />

        {/* SHARE MODAL */}
        <ShareModal
          isOpen={isShareOpen}
          onClose={() => setIsShareOpen(false)}
          title={profile.namaLengkap}
          subtitle={`${profile.prodi} • Angkatan ${profile.angkatan}`}
          shareUrl={typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.href : ''}
        />
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\app\register\page.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { registerAlumni, VERIFIED_ALUMNI_LIST, getRegisteredNomorAlumni } from '@/lib/auth';
import { PRODI_LIST, ProdiType } from '@/types';
import Navbar from '@/components/layout/Navbar';
import Footer from '@/components/layout/Footer';
import { UserPlus, AlertCircle, Loader2, CheckCircle2, Lock, Mail, User, Hash, Calendar, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, profile, setProfileData, logout } = useAuth();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [nomorAlumni, setNomorAlumni] = useState('');
  const [namaLengkap, setNamaLengkap] = useState('');
  const [prodi, setProdi] = useState<ProdiType>('Animasi');
  const [angkatan, setAngkatan] = useState('2020');
  
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState('');
  const [registeredNumbers, setRegisteredNumbers] = useState<string[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadRegisteredNumbers() {
      const list = await getRegisteredNomorAlumni();
      setRegisteredNumbers(list);
    }
    loadRegisteredNumbers();
  }, []);

  // Auto-fill jika user memilih nomor alumni dari whitelist
  const handleNomorAlumniChange = (value: string) => {
    setNomorAlumni(value);
    const clean = value.trim().replace(/[/._]/g, '-').toUpperCase();
    const found = VERIFIED_ALUMNI_LIST.find(
      (a) => a.nomorAlumni.toUpperCase() === clean
    );
    if (found) {
      if (found.nama) setNamaLengkap(found.nama);
      if (found.prodi) setProdi(found.prodi as ProdiType);
    }
    // Auto infer angkatan dari nomor MMTC-YYYY-XXX
    const parts = clean.split('-');
    if (parts.length >= 2 && /^\d{4}$/.test(parts[1])) {
      setAngkatan(parts[1]);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setSuccessMsg('');

    if (!email.trim() || !password || !nomorAlumni.trim() || !namaLengkap.trim()) {
      setError('Mohon lengkapi semua kolom yang wajib diisi.');
      return;
    }

    if (password.length < 6) {
      setError('Password minimal harus terdiri dari 6 karakter.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const newProfile = await registerAlumni({
        email: email.trim(),
        password,
        nomorAlumni: nomorAlumni.trim(),
        namaLengkap: namaLengkap.trim(),
        prodi,
        angkatan: angkatan.trim()
      });
      
      setProfileData(newProfile);
      setSuccessMsg('Pendaftaran berhasil! Mengarahkan ke halaman Lowongan Kerja...');
      setTimeout(() => {
        router.push('/lowongan');
      }, 1200);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.error('Registration failed:', err);
      const e = err as Error;
      setError(e.message || 'Gagal mendaftar. Pastikan nomor alumni valid dan email belum pernah dipakai.');
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between font-sans">
      <Navbar />

      <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4 py-12 sm:py-16">
        <div className="w-full max-w-xl bg-white border border-slate-200/90 p-8 sm:p-9 rounded-3xl shadow-sm space-y-6">
          <div className="text-center space-y-2">
            <img 
              src="/logo_alumnihub.png" 
              alt="AlumniHub Sekolah Tinggi Multi Media" 
              className="h-11 w-auto mx-auto object-contain mb-3"
            />
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Pendaftaran Akun Alumni</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Registrasi resmi menggunakan Nomor Alumni terdaftar kampus STMM MMTC Yogyakarta
            </p>
          </div>

          {user && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-xs text-amber-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span>Anda saat ini masuk sebagai <strong>{profile?.namaLengkap || user.email}</strong>.</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                <Link href="/lowongan" className="font-bold underline text-amber-900">
                  Ke Dashboard
                </Link>
                <span>•</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => logout()}
                  className="text-red-600 hover:underline font-bold"
                >
                  Keluar Dulu
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {successMsg && (
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs flex items-center gap-2.5 animate-fade-in">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
              <span className="font-medium">{successMsg}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {error && (
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-800 text-xs flex flex-col gap-2 animate-fade-in">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span className="font-medium leading-relaxed">{error}</span>
              </div>
              {(error.includes('sudah terdaftar') || error.includes('Login') || error.includes('Masuk')) && (
                <div className="pt-1 pl-6.5">
                  <Link
                    href="/login"
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-[#0284c7] hover:underline"
                  >
                    <span>Masuk Sekarang →</span>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Hash className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  Nomor Alumni *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={nomorAlumni}
                  onChange={(e) => handleNomorAlumniChange(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="MMTC-2018-001"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition font-mono uppercase"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  Tahun Angkatan *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={angkatan}
                  onChange={(e) => setAngkatan(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="2020"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                Nama Lengkap *
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={namaLengkap}
                onChange={(e) => setNamaLengkap(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Budi Santoso"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                Program Studi *
              </label>
              <select
                value={prodi}
                onChange={(e) => setProdi(e.target.value as ProdiType)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition font-medium"
              >
                {PRODI_LIST.map((p) => (
                  <option key={p.id} value={p.id} className="text-slate-900">
                    {p.label} - {p.desc}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                Email Aktif *
              </label>
              <input
                type="email"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="nama@email.com"
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                Kata Sandi (min 6 karakter) *
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  required
                  minLength={6}
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full pl-3.5 pr-10 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute right-3 top-3 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition"
                  aria-label={showPassword ? 'Sembunyikan password' : 'Tampilkan password'}
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-xs transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-2 disabled:opacity-50 active:scale-[0.98]"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                  Mendaftarkan Akun...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
                  Daftar Alumni Sekarang
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="pt-2 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 border-t border-slate-100">
            Sudah memiliki akun alumni?{' '}
            <Link href="/login" className="text-[#0284c7] font-bold hover:underline">
              Masuk di sini
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}
