// FILE: src\components\feed\LinkedInMessagingWidget.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';
/* eslint-disable react-hooks/purity */
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  ChevronUp, 
  ChevronDown, 
  Search, 
  Send, 
  ArrowLeft, 
  Circle, 
  X
} from 'lucide-react';
import AlumniAvatar from '@/components/profile/AlumniAvatar';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';
import { 
  collection, 
  doc, 
  query, 
  where, 
  onSnapshot, 
  setDoc, 
  arrayUnion 
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db } from '@/lib/firebase';

interface Message {
  id: string;
  sender: 'me' | 'them';
  text: string;
  time: string;
}

interface Conversation {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  prodi: string;
  role: string;
  avatar?: string;
  online: boolean;
  unreadCount?: number;
  lastMessage: string;
  lastTime: string;
  messages: Message[];
}

const INITIAL_CONVERSATIONS: Conversation[] = [
  {
    id: 'conv-1',
    name: 'Sarah Pradipta',
    prodi: 'Animasi',
    role: '3D Animator di Infinite Studios',
    online: true,
    unreadCount: 1,
    lastMessage: 'Boleh minta kontak WhatsApp-nya Bud buat diskusi rig Blender?',
    lastTime: '10m',
    messages: [
      { id: 'm1', sender: 'them', text: 'Halo Budi! Showreel rigging kamu di feed keren banget.', time: '14:20' },
      { id: 'm2', sender: 'me', text: 'Halo Sar! Makasih banyak ya, kemarin coba explore auto-rigging add-on.', time: '14:22' },
      { id: 'm3', sender: 'them', text: 'Boleh minta kontak WhatsApp-nya Bud buat diskusi rig Blender?', time: '14:25' }
    ]
  },
  {
    id: 'conv-2',
    name: 'Dimas Wicaksono',
    prodi: 'Matekstosi',
    role: 'Broadcast Director di MNC Media',
    online: true,
    unreadCount: 1,
    lastMessage: 'Halo Mas Budi, lowongan broadcast engineer di MNC masih buka ya.',
    lastTime: '1j',
    messages: [
      { id: 'm4', sender: 'them', text: 'Halo Mas Budi, lowongan broadcast engineer di MNC masih buka ya. Kalau ada alumni MMTC yang mau apply bisa langsung info saya.', time: '13:30' }
    ]
  },
  {
    id: 'conv-3',
    name: 'Rian Kusuma',
    prodi: 'Manaprod',
    role: 'Producer di Lumina Lab',
    online: false,
    lastMessage: 'Showreel kamu udah aku tonton, mantap banget visualnya!',
    lastTime: '1h',
    messages: [
      { id: 'm5', sender: 'them', text: 'Showreel kamu udah aku tonton, mantap banget visualnya!', time: 'Kemarin' },
      { id: 'm6', sender: 'me', text: 'Siap Mas Rian, terima kasih banyak apresiasinya!', time: 'Kemarin' }
    ]
  },
  {
    id: 'conv-4',
    name: 'Gilang Ramadhan',
    prodi: 'Game',
    role: 'Unity Gameplay Programmer',
    online: false,
    lastMessage: 'Thank you sudah baca GDD game indie kami!',
    lastTime: '2h',
    messages: [
      { id: 'm7', sender: 'them', text: 'Thank you sudah baca GDD game indie kami!', time: '2 hari lalu' }
    ]
  }
];

interface LinkedInMessagingWidgetProps {
  userAvatar?: string;
  userName?: string;
}

export default function LinkedInMessagingWidget({
  userAvatar: propAvatar,
  userName: propName
}: LinkedInMessagingWidgetProps = {}) {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();
  const userName = propName || profile?.namaLengkap || user?.displayName || 'Budi Santoso';
  const userAvatar = propAvatar || profile?.fotoProfil || user?.photoURL;

  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeChatId, setActiveChatId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [conversations, setConversations] = useState<Conversation[]>(INITIAL_CONVERSATIONS);
  const [inputMessage, setInputMessage] = useState('');

  // Real-time synchronization with Firestore collection 'conversations'
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.uid) return;

    try {
      const q = query(
        collection(db, 'conversations'),
        where('participants', 'array-contains', user.uid)
      );

      const unsub = onSnapshot(
        q,
        (snapshot) => {
          if (!snapshot.empty) {
            const firestoreConvs: Conversation[] = snapshot.docs.map((d) => {
              const data = d.data();
              const participants = (data.participants as string[]) || [];
              const otherUid = participants.find((uid) => uid !== user.uid) || 'peer';
              const otherProfile = data.participantProfiles?.[otherUid] || {};

              const messages: Message[] = (data.messages || []).map(
                (m: { id?: string; senderUid?: string; text?: string; createdAt?: string }) => ({
                  id: m.id || `m-${Date.now()}`,
                  sender: m.senderUid === user.uid ? 'me' : 'them',
                  text: m.text || '',
                  time: m.createdAt
                    ? new Date(m.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString('id-ID', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
                    : 'Baru saja'
                })
              );

              return {
                id: d.id,
                name: otherProfile.namaLengkap || data.otherName || 'Alumni MMTC',
                prodi: otherProfile.prodi || data.otherProdi || 'STMM MMTC',
                role: otherProfile.role || data.otherRole || 'Alumni',
                avatar: otherProfile.fotoProfil || data.otherAvatar || undefined,
                online: true,
                unreadCount: 0,
                lastMessage: data.lastMessage || (messages[messages.length - 1]?.text || ''),
                lastTime: data.updatedAt
                  ? new Date(data.updatedAt).toLocaleTimeString('id-ID', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
                  : 'Baru saja',
                messages
              };
            });

            // Merge with initial conversations so starter contacts are always available for demo presentation
            setConversations(() => {
              const fsIds = new Set(firestoreConvs.map((c) => c.id));
              const remainingInitials = INITIAL_CONVERSATIONS.filter((c) => !fsIds.has(c.id));
              return [...firestoreConvs, ...remainingInitials];
            });
          }
        },
        (error) => {
          console.warn('LinkedInMessagingWidget conversations listener fallback:', error);
        }
      );

      return () => unsub();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('LinkedInMessagingWidget Firestore query error:', err);
    }
  }, [user?.uid]);

  const activeConv = conversations.find(c => c.id === activeChatId);

  const totalUnread = conversations.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.unreadCount || 0), 0);

  const handleSelectConv = (id: string) => {
    setActiveChatId(id);
    // Mark as read
    setConversations(prev => prev.map(c => {
      if (c.id === id) {
        return { ...c, unreadCount: 0 };
      }
      return c;
    }));
  };

  const handleSendMessage = async () => {
    if (!inputMessage.trim() || !activeChatId) return;

    const textToSend = inputMessage.trim();
    const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
    const newMsg: Message = {
      id: `m-${Date.now()}`,
      sender: 'me',
      text: textToSend,
      time: 'Baru saja'
    };

    setConversations(prev => prev.map(c => {
      if (c.id === activeChatId) {
        return {
          ...c,
          lastMessage: newMsg.text,
          lastTime: 'Baru saja',
          messages: [...c.messages, newMsg]
        };
      }
      return c;
    }));

    setInputMessage('');

    // If user is logged in, sync to Firestore collection 'conversations'
    if (user?.uid) {
      const convDocId = activeChatId.startsWith('conv_') ? activeChatId : `conv_${user.uid}_${activeChatId}`;
      const otherParty = activeConv ? {
        namaLengkap: activeConv.name,
        prodi: activeConv.prodi,
        fotoProfil: activeConv.avatar || '',
        role: activeConv.role
      } : {
        namaLengkap: 'Alumni MMTC',
        prodi: 'STMM MMTC',
        fotoProfil: '',
        role: 'Alumni'
      };

      try {
        await setDoc(doc(db, 'conversations', convDocId), {
          id: convDocId,
          participants: [user.uid, activeChatId],
          participantProfiles: {
            [user.uid]: {
              namaLengkap: userName,
              prodi: profile?.prodi || 'Animasi',
              fotoProfil: userAvatar || '',
              role: profile?.pekerjaanSaatIni || 'Alumni STMM MMTC'
            },
            [activeChatId]: otherParty
          },
          lastMessage: textToSend,
          updatedAt: nowIso,
          messages: arrayUnion({
            id: newMsg.id,
            senderUid: user.uid,
            senderName: userName,
            text: textToSend,
            createdAt: nowIso
          })
        }, { merge: true });
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('LinkedInMessagingWidget Firestore write warning:', err);
      }
    }

    // Simulated short auto reply after 1.5s for presentation demo reliability
    setTimeout(async () => {
      const replyIso = new Date().toISOString();
      const replyMsg: Message = {
        id: `reply-${Date.now()}`,
        sender: 'them',
        text: 'Siap Mas, terima kasih ya! Nanti kita sambung lagi diskusinya.',
        time: 'Baru saja'
      };

      setConversations(prev => prev.map(c => {
        if (c.id === activeChatId) {
          return {
            ...c,
            lastMessage: replyMsg.text,
            lastTime: 'Baru saja',
            messages: [...c.messages, replyMsg]
          };
        }
        return c;
      }));

      if (user?.uid) {
        const convDocId = activeChatId.startsWith('conv_') ? activeChatId : `conv_${user.uid}_${activeChatId}`;
        try {
          await setDoc(doc(db, 'conversations', convDocId), {
            lastMessage: replyMsg.text,
            updatedAt: replyIso,
            messages: arrayUnion({
              id: replyMsg.id,
              senderUid: activeChatId,
              senderName: activeConv?.name || 'Alumni STMM',
              text: replyMsg.text,
              createdAt: replyIso
            })
          }, { merge: true });
        } catch {
          // ignore
        }
      }
    }, 1500);
  };

  const filteredConversations = conversations.filter(c => 
    c.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
    c.prodi.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
    c.lastMessage.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div 
      className={`fixed bottom-0 right-4 sm:right-6 z-40 w-80 sm:w-88 bg-white rounded-t-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 border-b-0 overflow-hidden flex flex-col transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out ${
        isOpen ? 'h-[460px]' : 'h-13'
      }`}
    >
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      {/* WIDGET HEADER (ALWAYS VISIBLE, CLICK TO TOGGLE)                */}
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      <div 
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        className="h-13 px-4 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer select-none transition shrink-0"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="relative">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shadow-2xs border border-white">
              <AlumniAvatar 
                src={userAvatar || undefined}
                name={userName}
                className="w-full h-full object-cover rounded-full"
              />
            </div>
            <Circle className="w-2.5 h-2.5 fill-emerald-500 text-white absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5" />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
              Pesan
            </span>
            {totalUnread > 0 && !isOpen && (
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 text-white text-[10px] font-bold shadow-xs">
                {totalUnread}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-400">
          <button 
            type="button" 
            className="p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-600 transition"
            title={isOpen ? 'Tutup Pesan' : 'Buka Pesan'}
          >
            {isOpen ? <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4" />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================== */}
      {/* WIDGET CONTENT (VISIBLE WHEN EXPANDED)                         */}
      {/* ============================================================== */}
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden bg-slate-50/50">
          
          {/* VIEW A: CONVERSATION LIST */}
          {!activeConv ? (
            <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
              {/* SEARCH INPUT */}
              <div className="p-3 bg-white border-b border-slate-100">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
                  <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={searchQuery}
                    onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Cari obrolan alumni..."
                    className="w-full bg-transparent text-xs text-slate-700 placeholder-slate-400 outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* CONVERSATION LIST */}
              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {filteredConversations.map(conv => (
                  <div
                    key={conv.id}
                    onClick={() => handleSelectConv(conv.id)}
                    className="p-3.5 bg-white hover:bg-sky-50/50 transition cursor-pointer flex items-start gap-3 group"
                  >
                    <div className="relative shrink-0">
                      <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shadow-2xs">
                        <AlumniAvatar src={conv.avatar} name={conv.name} />
                      </div>
                      {conv.online && (
                        <Circle className="w-2.5 h-2.5 fill-emerald-500 text-white absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5" />
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0284c7] transition truncate">
                          {conv.name}
                        </p>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 shrink-0 font-medium">
                          {conv.lastTime}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-[#0284c7] font-semibold truncate">
                        {conv.prodi}
                      </p>
                      <p className={`text-xs truncate mt-0.5 ${conv.unreadCount ? 'font-bold text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>
                        {conv.lastMessage}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    {Boolean(conv.unreadCount) && (
                      <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 self-center shrink-0" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}

                {filteredConversations.length === 0 && (
                  <div className="p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
                    Tidak ada percakapan ditemukan.
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* FOOTER SHORTCUT */}
              <div className="p-2.5 bg-white border-t border-slate-100 text-center text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                <span>Terhubung langsung dengan alumni STMM MMTC</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            
            /* VIEW B: ACTIVE CHAT ROOM */
            <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden bg-white">
              {/* CHAT HEADER */}
              <div className="px-3 py-2.5 bg-white border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between shadow-2xs shrink-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 truncate">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveChatId(null)}
                    className="p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 transition cursor-pointer"
                    title="Kembali ke daftar pesan"
                  >
                    <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>

                  <div className="relative shrink-0">
                    <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[10px]">
                      <AlumniAvatar src={activeConv.avatar} name={activeConv.name} />
                    </div>
                    {activeConv.online && (
                      <Circle className="w-2 h-2 fill-emerald-500 text-white absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5" />
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="truncate">
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 truncate leading-tight">
                      {activeConv.name}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-emerald-600 font-medium leading-tight">
                      {activeConv.online ? 'Aktif sekarang' : 'Offline'} • {activeConv.prodi}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveChatId(null)}
                  className="p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 transition cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* MESSAGES BUBBLE AREA */}
              <div className="flex-1 p-3 overflow-y-auto space-y-2.5 bg-slate-50/50">
                {activeConv.messages.map(msg => (
                  <div
                    key={msg.id}
                    className={`flex flex-col ${msg.sender === 'me' ? 'items-end' : 'items-start'}`}
                  >
                    <div
                      className={`max-w-[85%] p-2.5 rounded-2xl text-xs leading-relaxed shadow-2xs ${
                        msg.sender === 'me'
                          ? 'bg-[#0284c7] text-white rounded-br-xs'
                          : 'bg-white text-slate-800 border border-slate-200/80 rounded-bl-xs'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {msg.text}
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 px-1 mt-0.5">
                      {msg.time}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* CHAT INPUT FORM */}
              <div className="p-2.5 bg-white border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={inputMessage}
                  onChange={(e) => setInputMessage(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSendMessage();
                  }}
                  placeholder="Ketik pesan..."
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0284c7] focus:bg-white transition"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleSendMessage}
                  disabled={!inputMessage.trim()}
                  className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] disabled:opacity-40 text-white transition shadow-2xs cursor-pointer"
                  title="Kirim Pesan"
                >
                  <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\components\layout\Navbar.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext';
import { 
  Briefcase, 
  PlusCircle, 
  LogOut, 
  Menu, 
  X, 
  FileText, 
  User, 
  Compass, 
  Users,
  ChevronDown,
  Settings,
  Bell,
  Heart,
  MessageSquare,
  CheckCheck
} from 'lucide-react';
import AlumniAvatar from '@/components/profile/AlumniAvatar';

interface ExtendedProfile {
  namaLengkap?: string;
  prodi?: string;
  angkatan?: string;
  fotoProfil?: string;
  pekerjaanSaatIni?: string;
}

interface NotificationItem {
  id: string;
  type: 'like' | 'comment' | 'job' | 'application';
  title: string;
  description: string;
  time: string;
  link: string;
  read: boolean;
  name?: string;
}

const INITIAL_NOTIFICATIONS: NotificationItem[] = [
  {
    id: 'notif-1',
    type: 'like',
    title: 'Farhan Dito menyukai postingan showreel kamu',
    description: '"Showreel 3D Character Rigging & Animation 2024"',
    time: '5 menit lalu',
    link: '/feed',
    read: false,
    name: 'Farhan Dito'
  },
  {
    id: 'notif-2',
    type: 'job',
    title: 'Lowongan baru relevan: TRANS7',
    description: 'PT Duta Visual Nusantara Tivi Tujuh membuka posisi "Video Editor Program Magazine"',
    time: '2 jam lalu',
    link: '/lowongan',
    read: false,
    name: 'TRANS7'
  },
  {
    id: 'notif-3',
    type: 'comment',
    title: 'Anindya Larasati mengomentari postingan kamu',
    description: '"Keren banget breakdown lighting & render-nya mas!"',
    time: '4 jam lalu',
    link: '/feed',
    read: false,
    name: 'Anindya Larasati'
  },
  {
    id: 'notif-4',
    type: 'application',
    title: 'Update Status Lamaran Kerja',
    description: 'Lamaran di PT Net Mediatama masuk ke tahap "Review Portofolio"',
    time: '1 hari lalu',
    link: '/lamaran',
    read: true,
    name: 'NET TV'
  }
];

export default function Navbar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, profile, logout } = useAuth();
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [profileDropdownOpen, setProfileDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notificationOpen, setNotificationOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>(INITIAL_NOTIFICATIONS);

  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const notificationRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mobileNotificationRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length;

  const markAllAsRead = () => {
    setNotifications((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, read: true })));
  };

  const handleNotificationClick = (item: NotificationItem) => {
    setNotifications((prev) =>
      prev.map((n) => (n.id === item.id ? { ...n, read: true } : n))
    );
    setNotificationOpen(false);
    router.push(item.link);
  };

  // Close dropdowns on click outside
  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
      const target = event.target as Node;
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(target)) {
        setProfileDropdownOpen(false);
      }
      if (
        notificationRef.current && !notificationRef.current.contains(target) &&
        (!mobileNotificationRef.current || !mobileNotificationRef.current.contains(target))
      ) {
        setNotificationOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const demoStorageRaw = React.useSyncExternalStore(
    (onStoreChange) => {
      if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};
      window.addEventListener('storage', onStoreChange);
      window.addEventListener('alumnihub_profile_updated', onStoreChange);
      return () => {
        window.removeEventListener('storage', onStoreChange);
        window.removeEventListener('alumnihub_profile_updated', onStoreChange);
      };
    },
    () => (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('mmtc_demo_profile') : null),
    () => null
  );

  const demoStorageProfile = React.useMemo<ExtendedProfile | null>(() => {
    if (!demoStorageRaw) return null;
    try {
      return JSON.parse(demoStorageRaw) as ExtendedProfile;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }, [demoStorageRaw]);

  // User login state (hanya aktif jika user benar-benar login)
  const effectiveUser = user;
  const displayProfile: ExtendedProfile | null = profile 
    ? (profile as unknown as ExtendedProfile)
    : (user && demoStorageProfile ? demoStorageProfile : null);

  const isActive = (path: string) => {
    if (path === '/') return pathname === '/';
    return pathname.startsWith(path);
  };

  const renderNotificationDropdownContent = () => (
    <>
      {/* Notification Header */}
      <div className="px-4 py-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Notifikasi</h4>
          {unreadCount > 0 ? (
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-sky-100 text-[#0284c7]">
              {unreadCount} Baru
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">
              Semua dibaca
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        {unreadCount > 0 && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={markAllAsRead}
            className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#0284c7] hover:text-[#0369a1] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1 transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            <CheckCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Tandai dibaca</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Notification Items List */}
      <div className="max-h-[350px] overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-slate-50">
        {notifications.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
            Belum ada notifikasi baru untukmu.
          </div>
        ) : (
          notifications.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              onClick={() => handleNotificationClick(item)}
              className={`p-3.5 flex items-start gap-3 hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer transition select-none ${
                !item.read ? 'bg-sky-50/40' : 'bg-white'
              }`}
            >
              {/* Notification Category Icon */}
              <div className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 border ${
                item.type === 'like' ? 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-100 text-rose-500' :
                item.type === 'comment' ? 'bg-sky-50 border-sky-100 text-sky-600' :
                item.type === 'job' ? 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-100 text-amber-600' :
                'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-100 text-emerald-600'
              }`}>
                {item.type === 'like' && <Heart className="w-4 h-4 fill-rose-500" />}
                {item.type === 'comment' && <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4" />}
                {item.type === 'job' && <Briefcase className="w-4 h-4" />}
                {item.type === 'application' && <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </div>

              {/* Notification Text */}
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-1">
                  <p className={`text-xs leading-snug ${!item.read ? 'font-bold text-slate-900' : 'font-medium text-slate-700'}`}>
                    {item.title}
                  </p>
                  {!item.read && (
                    <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-sky-500 shrink-0 mt-1" />
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                  {item.description}
                </p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium mt-1">
                  {item.time}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Notification Footer Link */}
      <div className="p-2 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/60">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            setNotificationOpen(false);
            router.push('/feed');
          }}
          className="w-full py-1.5 text-center text-xs font-bold text-[#0284c7] hover:text-[#0369a1] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
        >
          Lihat Semua Aktivitas di Linimasa &rarr;
        </button>
      </div>
    </>
  );

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-sky-100 text-slate-800 transition-all">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between min-h-[72px] sm:min-h-[78px] py-1.5">
          
          {/* Brand Logo - Official AlumniHub Graphic (-10px to h-[52px] sm:h-[57px]) */}
          <Link href="/" className="flex items-center group focus:outline-none py-0.5 shrink-0">
            <img 
              src="/logo_alumnihub.png" 
              alt="AlumniHub Sekolah Tinggi Multi Media" 
              className="h-[52px] sm:h-[57px] w-auto object-contain group-hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity"
            />
          </Link>

          {/* Desktop Navigation - Clean Text Navigation without pill containers */}
          <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-6 lg:gap-8">
            <Link 
              href="/" 
              className={`text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                pathname === '/' 
                  ? 'text-[#0284c7] font-bold' 
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              <Compass className={`w-4 h-4 ${pathname === '/' ? 'text-[#0284c7]' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
              <span>Beranda</span>
            </Link>

            <Link 
              href="/feed" 
              className={`text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                isActive('/feed') || isActive('/beranda')
                  ? 'text-[#0284c7] font-bold' 
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              <Users className={`w-4 h-4 ${isActive('/feed') || isActive('/beranda') ? 'text-[#0284c7]' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
              <span>Feed</span>
            </Link>

            <Link 
              href="/lowongan" 
              className={`text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                isActive('/lowongan') 
                  ? 'text-[#0284c7] font-bold' 
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              <Briefcase className={`w-4 h-4 ${isActive('/lowongan') ? 'text-[#0284c7]' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
              <span>Lowongan</span>
            </Link>

            <Link 
              href="/open-to-work" 
              className={`text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                isActive('/open-to-work') 
                  ? 'text-emerald-600 font-bold' 
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
              <span>Open to Work</span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          {/* User Profile / Auth CTA */}
          <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2.5">
            {effectiveUser ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {/* Notification Bell Trigger & Dropdown Popover */}
                <div className="relative" ref={notificationRef}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setNotificationOpen(!notificationOpen);
                      setProfileDropdownOpen(false);
                    }}
                    className={`relative p-2 rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer select-none flex items-center justify-center ${
                      notificationOpen
                        ? 'text-[#0284c7]'
                        : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100/60'
                    }`}
                    aria-label="Lihat Notifikasi"
                    aria-expanded={notificationOpen}
                  >
                    <Bell className="w-4 h-4" />
                    {unreadCount > 0 && (
                      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 min-w-[17px] h-[17px] px-1 bg-red-500 text-white text-[9px] font-bold rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-white shadow-xs animate-pulse">
                        {unreadCount}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </button>

                  {/* Desktop Notification Popover */}
                  {notificationOpen && (
                    <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-80 sm:w-[380px] bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200/90 py-1 z-50 animate-fade-in overflow-hidden">
                      {renderNotificationDropdownContent()}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Profile Trigger Button & Dropdown Popover */}
                <div className="relative" ref={dropdownRef}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setProfileDropdownOpen(!profileDropdownOpen)}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer select-none ${
                      profileDropdownOpen || isActive('/profil')
                        ? 'text-[#0284c7] font-bold'
                        : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                    }`}
                    aria-expanded={profileDropdownOpen}
                    aria-haspopup="true"
                  >
                    <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-[11px] font-bold overflow-hidden shadow-2xs">
                      <AlumniAvatar src={displayProfile?.fotoProfil} name={displayProfile?.namaLengkap} />
                    </div>
                    <span className="hidden lg:inline leading-none">
                      Me
                    </span>
                    <ChevronDown className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-200 ${profileDropdownOpen ? 'rotate-180 text-[#0284c7]' : ''}`} />
                  </button>

                  {/* LinkedIn-style Profile Menu Dropdown */}
                  {profileDropdownOpen && (
                    <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-80 sm:w-[350px] bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200/90 py-2 z-50 animate-fade-in overflow-hidden">
                      {/* USER PROFILE SUMMARY CARD (LEGA & PROPORSI ELEGAN) */}
                      <div className="p-5 border-b border-slate-100 space-y-4">
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-3.5">
                          <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-bold shadow-xs ring-4 ring-sky-50 shrink-0 border border-slate-100">
                            <AlumniAvatar src={displayProfile?.fotoProfil} name={displayProfile?.namaLengkap} />
                          </div>
                          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 leading-snug truncate">
                              {displayProfile?.namaLengkap || 'Budi Santoso'}
                            </h4>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-medium leading-relaxed mt-0.5 line-clamp-2">
                              {displayProfile?.pekerjaanSaatIni || `${displayProfile?.prodi || 'Animasi'} • Angkatan ${displayProfile?.angkatan || '2020'}`}
                            </p>
                            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 mt-2 rounded-lg bg-sky-50 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#0284c7] border border-sky-100">
                              <span>{displayProfile?.prodi || 'Animasi'} MMTC</span>
                              <span>•</span>
                              <span>Angkatan {displayProfile?.angkatan || '2020'}</span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* SECTION: AKUN */}
                      <div className="py-2 px-3 border-b border-slate-100">
                        <p className="px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                          Akun
                        </p>
                        <Link
                          href="/profil"
                          onClick={() => setProfileDropdownOpen(false)}
                          className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 transition"
                        >
                          <Settings className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                          <span>Setting Profil &amp; Privasi</span>
                        </Link>
                        <Link
                          href="/open-to-work"
                          onClick={() => setProfileDropdownOpen(false)}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 transition"
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                            <Briefcase className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                            <span>Status Open to Work</span>
                          </div>
                          <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 shadow-2xs" />
                        </Link>
                      </div>

                      {/* SECTION: KELOLA */}
                      <div className="py-2 px-3 border-b border-slate-100">
                        <p className="px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                          Kelola
                        </p>
                        <Link
                          href="/aktivitas"
                          onClick={() => setProfileDropdownOpen(false)}
                          className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 transition"
                        >
                          <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                          <span>Kelola Postingan &amp; Aktivitas</span>
                        </Link>
                        <Link
                          href="/lamaran"
                          onClick={() => setProfileDropdownOpen(false)}
                          className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 transition"
                        >
                          <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                          <span>Status Lamaran Terkirim</span>
                        </Link>
                        <Link
                          href="/post-lowongan"
                          onClick={() => setProfileDropdownOpen(false)}
                          className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 transition"
                        >
                          <Briefcase className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                          <span>Pasang Lowongan Kerja</span>
                        </Link>
                      </div>

                      {/* SECTION: SIGN OUT / KELUAR (SELALU MUNCUL) */}
                      <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50/60 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setProfileDropdownOpen(false);
                            if (user) {
                              logout();
                            } else {
                              router.push('/login');
                            }
                          }}
                          className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-red-600 hover:text-white hover:bg-red-600 border border-red-200 hover:border-red-600 transition shadow-2xs cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99]"
                        >
                          <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          <span>Keluar (Sign Out)</span>
                        </button>

                        {!user && (
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400 px-1 pt-0.5">
                            <span>Mode Pratinjau Demo</span>
                            <Link
                              href="/login"
                              onClick={() => setProfileDropdownOpen(false)}
                              className="font-bold text-[#0284c7] hover:underline"
                            >
                              Masuk Akun Riil
                            </Link>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <Link 
                  href="/login" 
                  className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 hover:text-[#0284c7] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full hover:bg-slate-50 transition"
                >
                  Masuk
                </Link>
                <Link 
                  href="/register" 
                  className="text-sm font-bold px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] hover:from-sky-600 hover:to-[#0369a1] text-white shadow-md shadow-sky-500/25 transition active:scale-[0.98]"
                >
                  Daftar Alumni
                </Link>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Mobile Actions: Notification Bell + Menu Toggle */}
          <div className="md:hidden flex items-center gap-2">
            {effectiveUser && (
              <div className="relative" ref={mobileNotificationRef}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setNotificationOpen(!notificationOpen);
                    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
                  }}
                  className={`relative p-2 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer ${
                    notificationOpen
                      ? 'text-[#0284c7]'
                      : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100/60'
                  }`}
                  aria-label="Notifikasi"
                >
                  <Bell className="w-5 h-5" />
                  {unreadCount > 0 && (
                    <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 min-w-[16px] h-[16px] px-1 bg-red-500 text-white text-[9px] font-bold rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-white">
                      {unreadCount}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>

                {/* Mobile Notification Popover */}
                {notificationOpen && (
                  <div className="fixed inset-x-3 top-[76px] bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200/90 py-1 z-50 animate-fade-in overflow-hidden max-h-[75vh] flex flex-col">
                    {renderNotificationDropdownContent()}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Mobile Menu Toggle Button */}
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen);
                setNotificationOpen(false);
              }}
              className="p-2.5 rounded-2xl text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 transition"
              aria-label="Buka Menu"
            >
              {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="w-6 h-6" /> : <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Mobile Menu Dropdown */}
      {mobileMenuOpen && (
        <div className="md:hidden bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-b border-sky-100 px-4 pt-3 pb-6 space-y-2 animate-fade-in shadow-lg">
          <Link 
            href="/" 
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm font-medium transition ${
              pathname === '/' ? 'bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] font-bold' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
            }`}
          >
            <Compass className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
            Beranda
          </Link>
          <Link 
            href="/feed" 
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm font-medium transition ${
              isActive('/feed') || isActive('/beranda') ? 'bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] font-bold' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
            }`}
          >
            <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
            Feed Alumni
          </Link>
          <Link 
            href="/lowongan" 
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm font-medium transition ${
              isActive('/lowongan') ? 'bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] font-bold' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
            }`}
          >
            <Briefcase className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
            Lowongan Kerja
          </Link>
          <Link 
            href="/open-to-work" 
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
            className={`flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm font-semibold transition ${
              isActive('/open-to-work') ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-bold border border-emerald-200' : 'text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50/50 hover:bg-emerald-50'
            }`}
          >
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
              Open to Work (Katalog Talenta)
            </span>
          </Link>

          {effectiveUser ? (
            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2">
              <Link 
                href="/post-lowongan" 
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
              >
                <PlusCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
                Pasang Lowongan
              </Link>
              <Link 
                href="/lamaran" 
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
              >
                <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
                Lamaran Saya
              </Link>
              <Link 
                href="/profil" 
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-sm font-bold bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] border border-sky-100"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold overflow-hidden">
                    <AlumniAvatar src={displayProfile?.fotoProfil} name={displayProfile?.namaLengkap} />
                  </div>
                  <span>Setting Profil ({displayProfile?.namaLengkap || 'Alumni'})</span>
                </div>
                <User className="w-4 h-4" />
              </Link>
              {user ? (
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    logout();
                    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
                  }}
                  className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-2xl transition"
                >
                  <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
                  Keluar
                </button>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-col gap-2">
              <Link 
                href="/login" 
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className="w-full text-center py-3 rounded-full border border-slate-200 text-slate-800 font-bold hover:bg-slate-50 text-sm transition"
              >
                Masuk
              </Link>
              <Link 
                href="/register" 
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className="w-full text-center py-3 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-500 to-[#0284c7] text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-md shadow-sky-500/25 transition"
              >
                Daftar Alumni
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

// FILE: src\components\profile\AlumniActivityHistory.tsx

'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { 
  Heart, 
  MessageSquare, 
  Share2, 
  FileText, 
  PlusCircle, 
  Maximize2, 
  Calendar, 
  Video,
  Users,
  Award,
  ExternalLink, 
  Check 
} from 'lucide-react';
import AlumniAvatar from '@/components/profile/AlumniAvatar';
import { CinemaLightbox } from '@/components/ui/Modal';
import { parseYouTubeVideoId } from '@/lib/media';
import { getUserFeedPosts } from '@/lib/feed';

export interface UserActivityPost {
  id: string;
  authorName: string;
  authorProdi: string;
  authorAngkatan?: string;
  authorRole?: string;
  authorAvatar?: string;
  timeAgo: string;
  category: 'showreel' | 'kolaborasi' | 'diskusi' | 'pencapaian';
  content: string;
  hashtags?: string[];
  mediaType?: 'youtube' | 'image' | 'pdf';
  mediaUrl?: string;
  mediaTitle?: string;
  mediaSize?: string;
  likesCount: number;
  commentsCount?: number;
}

const DEFAULT_USER_POSTS: UserActivityPost[] = [
  {
    id: 'user-post-1',
    authorName: 'Budi Santoso',
    authorProdi: 'Animasi',
    authorAngkatan: '2020',
    authorRole: '3D Animator di Infinite Studios',
    timeAgo: '1 hari yang lalu',
    category: 'showreel',
    content: 'Baru saja merilis breakdown rigging character stylized untuk project open-source animasi MMTC. Fokus pada face controls dan flexible spine setup di Blender. Silakan tonton showreel-nya di bawah ya rekan-rekan!',
    hashtags: ['#Blender3D', '#RiggingBreakdown', '#AnimasiMMTC', '#CharacterArt'],
    mediaType: 'youtube',
    mediaUrl: 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ',
    mediaTitle: '3D Facial & Body Rigging Breakdown 2026',
    likesCount: 38,
    commentsCount: 6
  },
  {
    id: 'user-post-2',
    authorName: 'Budi Santoso',
    authorProdi: 'Animasi',
    authorAngkatan: '2020',
    authorRole: '3D Animator di Infinite Studios',
    timeAgo: '4 hari yang lalu',
    category: 'diskusi',
    content: 'Sharing tips workflow animasi kamera di Unreal Engine 5 untuk keperluan sinematik broadcast. Menggabungkan teknik blocking kamera klasik MMTC dengan Sequencer UE5. Ada yang tertarik adakan sharing session santai weekend ini?',
    hashtags: ['#UnrealEngine5', '#Cinematography', '#MMTCAlumni', '#VirtualProduction'],
    mediaType: 'image',
    mediaUrl: '/kampus_stmm.jpg',
    mediaTitle: 'Virtual Production Studio Setup Memories',
    likesCount: 52,
    commentsCount: 11
  }
];

interface AlumniActivityHistoryProps {
  authorUid?: string;
  authorName?: string;
  authorProdi?: string;
  authorAvatar?: string;
  isOwner?: boolean;
}

export default function AlumniActivityHistory({
  authorUid,
  authorName = 'Budi Santoso',
  authorProdi = 'Animasi',
  authorAvatar = '',
  isOwner = true
}: AlumniActivityHistoryProps) {
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<UserActivityPost[]>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      try {
        const stored = localStorage.getItem('alumni_user_posts');
        if (stored) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {
            const existingIds = new Set(parsed.map((p: UserActivityPost) => p.id));
            return [
              ...parsed,
              ...DEFAULT_USER_POSTS.filter(p => !existingIds.has(p.id))
            ];
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }
    return DEFAULT_USER_POSTS;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;
    if (authorUid) {
      getUserFeedPosts(authorUid).then((userPosts) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        if (userPosts.length > 0) {
          const mapped: UserActivityPost[] = userPosts.map(p => ({
            id: p.id,
            authorName: p.authorName,
            authorProdi: p.authorProdi,
            authorAngkatan: p.authorAngkatan,
            authorRole: p.authorRole,
            authorAvatar: p.authorAvatar,
            timeAgo: p.createdAt ? new Date(p.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }) : 'Baru saja',
            category: p.category,
            content: p.content,
            hashtags: p.hashtags,
            mediaType: p.mediaType,
            mediaUrl: p.mediaUrl,
            mediaTitle: p.mediaTitle,
            mediaSize: p.mediaSize,
            likesCount: p.likesCount,
            commentsCount: p.commentsCount
          }));
          setPosts(mapped);
        }
      }).catch(err => {
        console.warn('AlumniActivityHistory load posts error:', err);
      });
    }
    return () => { isMounted = false; };
  }, [authorUid]);

  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>('semua');
  const [likedPosts, setLikedPosts] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [cinemaVideo, setCinemaVideo] = useState<{ videoId: string; title: string } | null>(null);
  const [copyAlert, setCopyAlert] = useState(false);

  const handleToggleLike = (id: string) => {
    setLikedPosts(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [id]: !prev[id]
    }));
  };

  const handleShare = (post: UserActivityPost) => {
    if (navigator.clipboard) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(`${window.location.origin}/feed#${post.id}`);
      setCopyAlert(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopyAlert(false), 2500);
    }
  };

  const filteredPosts = posts.filter(post => {
    if (selectedCategory === 'semua') return true;
    return post.category === selectedCategory;
  });

  const getCategoryBadge = (category: string) => {
    switch (category) {
      case 'showreel':
        return <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200 text-[10px] font-semibold"><Video className="w-3 h-3" />Showreel</span>;
      case 'kolaborasi':
        return <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 text-[10px] font-semibold"><Users className="w-3 h-3" />Kolaborasi</span>;
      case 'pencapaian':
        return <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 border border-sky-200 text-[10px] font-semibold"><Award className="w-3 h-3" />Capaian</span>;
      default:
        return <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 text-[10px] font-semibold"><MessageSquare className="w-3 h-3" />Diskusi</span>;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div id="aktivitas" className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-6 shadow-sm scroll-mt-24">
      {/* HEADER */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0284c7]" />
              Aktivitas &amp; Riwayat Postingan
            </h3>
            <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-sky-50 text-[#0284c7] font-bold text-xs border border-sky-100">
              {posts.length} Postingan
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Riwayat publikasi showreel, kolaborasi tim, dan diskusi yang Anda bagikan di komunitas alumni STMM MMTC
          </p>
        </div>

        {isOwner && (
          <Link
            href="/feed"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold transition shadow-2xs shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <PlusCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Buat Postingan Baru</span>
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* FILTER TABS (LINKEDIN / FACEBOOK STYLE) */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {[
          { id: 'semua', label: 'Semua' },
          { id: 'showreel', label: 'Karya & Showreel' },
          { id: 'diskusi', label: 'Diskusi' },
          { id: 'kolaborasi', label: 'Kolaborasi' },
          { id: 'pencapaian', label: 'Kabar & Capaian' }
        ].map(tab => (
          <button
            key={tab.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(tab.id)}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer select-none ${
              selectedCategory === tab.id
                ? 'bg-[#0284c7] text-white font-bold shadow-2xs'
                : 'bg-slate-100/80 hover:bg-slate-200/70 text-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            {tab.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* COPY ALERT NOTIFICATION */}
      {copyAlert && (
        <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 animate-fade-in">
          <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
          <span>Tautan postingan berhasil disalin ke clipboard!</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* POSTS LIST */}
      {filteredPosts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-2">
          <MessageSquare className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-300 mx-auto" />
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">Tidak ada postingan dalam kategori ini</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-sm mx-auto">
            Bagikan karya portofolio, diskusi teknologi, atau update terbaru Anda langsung ke feed alumni.
          </p>
          <div className="pt-2">
            <Link
              href="/feed"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white text-xs font-bold transition shadow-2xs"
            >
              <PlusCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Publikasikan Sekarang</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {filteredPosts.map((post) => {
            const isLiked = likedPosts[post.id];
            const currentLikes = post.likesCount + (isLiked ? 1 : 0);
            const ytId = post.mediaType === 'youtube' && post.mediaUrl ? parseYouTubeVideoId(post.mediaUrl) : null;

            return (
              <div 
                key={post.id}
                className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200/90 space-y-3.5 hover:border-sky-200 hover:shadow-xs transition"
              >
                {/* POST HEADER */}
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-[#0284c7] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm shadow-2xs shrink-0">
                      <AlumniAvatar 
                        src={post.authorAvatar || authorAvatar} 
                        name={post.authorName || authorName} 
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">
                          {post.authorName || authorName}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-[#0284c7] font-semibold">
                          • {post.authorProdi || authorProdi}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                        <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-300" />
                        <span>{post.timeAgo}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    {getCategoryBadge(post.category)}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* POST CONTENT */}
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                  {post.content}
                </p>

                {/* HASHTAGS */}
                {post.hashtags && post.hashtags.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
                    {post.hashtags.map((tag) => (
                      <span key={tag} className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#0284c7] hover:underline cursor-pointer">
                        {tag}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* MEDIA ATTACHMENT */}
                {post.mediaType === 'youtube' && post.mediaUrl && ytId && (
                  <div className="relative rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-900 group aspect-video">
                    <iframe
                      src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${ytId}?rel=0`}
                      title={post.mediaTitle || 'Showreel'}
                      allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                      allowFullScreen
                      className="w-full h-full border-0"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setCinemaVideo({ videoId: ytId, title: post.mediaTitle || 'Showreel' })}
                      className="absolute top-2.5 right-2.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-black/75 hover:bg-black/90 text-white text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 backdrop-blur-md opacity-90 hover:opacity-100 transition shadow-sm cursor-pointer z-10"
                      title="Mode Bioskop Layar Penuh"
                    >
                      <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3 text-sky-400" />
                      <span>Mode Bioskop</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}

                {post.mediaType === 'image' && post.mediaUrl && (
                  <div className="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 max-h-72 bg-slate-100">
                    <img 
                      src={post.mediaUrl} 
                      alt={post.mediaTitle || 'Media Foto'}
                      className="w-full h-auto max-h-72 object-cover"
                    />
                  </div>
                )}

                {post.mediaType === 'pdf' && (
                  <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                      <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-red-100 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0">
                        <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 truncate">
                          {post.mediaTitle || 'Dokumen PDF'}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                          {post.mediaSize || 'PDF Document'} • Terverifikasi Alumni MMTC
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {post.mediaUrl && post.mediaUrl !== '#' && (
                      <a
                        href={post.mediaUrl}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        className="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 text-[#0284c7] hover:bg-sky-50 text-[11px] font-bold transition shrink-0"
                      >
                        Buka PDF
                      </a>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* POST FOOTER / INTERACTION BAR */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 text-slate-500 text-xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleToggleLike(post.id)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer select-none ${
                        isLiked ? 'text-red-500 font-bold' : 'hover:text-red-500'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Heart className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isLiked ? 'fill-red-500 text-red-500' : ''}`} />
                      <span>{currentLikes} Suka</span>
                    </button>

                    <Link
                      href="/feed"
                      className="flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-[#0284c7] transition"
                    >
                      <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                      <span>{post.commentsCount || 0} Komentar</span>
                    </Link>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleShare(post)}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-[#0284c7] transition cursor-pointer"
                      title="Salin Tautan Postingan"
                    >
                      <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>

                    <Link
                      href="/feed"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#0284c7] hover:underline"
                    >
                      <span>Buka di Feed</span>
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CINEMA LIGHTBOX */}
      <CinemaLightbox
        isOpen={Boolean(cinemaVideo)}
        onClose={() => setCinemaVideo(null)}
        title={cinemaVideo?.title || 'Preview Showreel'}
        videoId={cinemaVideo?.videoId || ''}
      />
    </div>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\components\profile\AlumniAvatar.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';

interface AlumniAvatarProps {
  src?: string;
  name?: string;
  className?: string;
}

export default function AlumniAvatar({ 
  src, 
  name, 
  className = 'w-full h-full object-cover' 
}: AlumniAvatarProps) {
  const [errorSrc, setErrorSrc] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const initial = (name || 'A').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || 'A';
  const hasError = Boolean(src && errorSrc === src);

  if (src && !hasError) {
    return (
      <img
        key={src}
        src={src}
        alt={name || 'Foto Alumni'}
        className={className}
        onError={() => setErrorSrc(src)}
      />
    );
  }

  return <span>{initial}</span>;
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\components\profile\skills.ts
// ============================================================================
export const POPULAR_SKILLS = [
  'Blender', 'Maya', '3D Animation', 'Unity', 'Unreal Engine',
  'Premiere', 'After Effects', 'DaVinci', 'Photoshop', 'Illustrator',
  'Figma', 'UI/UX', 'Broadcasting', 'Directing', 'Sound Design', 'C#', 'Motion Graphics'
];

export function extractSkills(bio = '', prodi = ''): string[] {
  const detected: string[] = [];
  const text = `${bio} ${prodi}`.toLowerCase();
  POPULAR_SKILLS.forEach((skill) => {
    if (text.includes(skill.toLowerCase())) {
      detected.push(skill);
    }
  });

  if (detected.length === 0) {
    if (prodi.includes('Animasi')) return ['Blender', '3D Animation', 'Motion Graphic'];
    if (prodi.includes('Game')) return ['Unity 3D', 'Game Art', 'C#'];
    if (prodi.includes('MIK')) return ['UI/UX', 'Web System', 'Media Tech'];
    if (prodi.includes('Matekstosi')) return ['Studio Control', 'Audio Mix', 'Broadcasting'];
    if (prodi.includes('Manarita')) return ['Journalism', 'News Anchor', 'Scriptwriting'];
    if (prodi.includes('Manaprodsi')) return ['Producer', 'Directing', 'Broadcasting'];
    return ['Creative Media', 'Multimedia'];
  }

  return detected.slice(0, 5);
}
