// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, Clock, Eye, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';

interface StatusBadgeProps {
  status: 'pending' | 'reviewed' | 'accepted' | 'rejected' | string;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
}

export function StatusBadge({ status, size = 'md' }: StatusBadgeProps) {
  const sizeClasses = size === 'sm' ? 'px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px]' : 'px-2.5 py-1 text-xs';

  switch (status) {
    case 'accepted':
      return (
        <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200/80 text-emerald-700 font-semibold ${sizeClasses}`}>
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
          <span>Diterima</span>
        </span>
      );
    case 'rejected':
      return (
        <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-50 border border-red-200/80 text-red-700 font-semibold ${sizeClasses}`}>
          <XCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" />
          <span>Ditolak</span>
        </span>
      );
    case 'reviewed':
      return (
        <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200/80 text-amber-700 font-semibold ${sizeClasses}`}>
          <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
          <span>Sedang Ditinjau</span>
        </span>
      );
    default:
      return (
        <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-sky-50 border border-sky-200/80 text-[#0284c7] font-semibold ${sizeClasses}`}>
          <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0284c7]" />
          <span>Menunggu Respon</span>
        </span>
      );
  }
}

export function OpenToWorkBadge({ pulse = true }: { pulse?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200/90 text-emerald-700 text-[11px] font-semibold whitespace-nowrap shadow-2xs">
      <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 ${pulse ? 'animate-pulse' : ''}`} />
      <span>Open to Work</span>
    </span>
  );
}

export function ProdiBadge({ prodi }: { prodi: string }) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center text-[10px] font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200/60">
      {prodi}
    </span>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\components\ui\Modal.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  maxWidth?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | '2xl' | '4xl' | '5xl';
}

export function Modal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  children,
  maxWidth = 'lg',
}: ModalProps) {
  const handleKeyDown = useCallback(
    (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        onClose();
      }
    },
    [onClose]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, handleKeyDown]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const maxWidthClasses = {
    sm: 'max-w-sm',
    md: 'max-w-md',
    lg: 'max-w-lg',
    xl: 'max-w-xl',
    '2xl': 'max-w-2xl',
    '4xl': 'max-w-4xl',
    '5xl': 'max-w-5xl',
  }[maxWidth];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6">
      {/* Backdrop */}
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs transition-opacity animate-in fade-in duration-200"
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      {/* Modal Dialog Box */}
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        className={`relative w-full ${maxWidthClasses} bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-100 overflow-hidden z-10 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-200`}
      >
        {title && (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{title}</h3>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1.5 rounded-full text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 transition cursor-pointer"
              aria-label="Tutup"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="p-6">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface CinemaLightboxProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  videoId: string;
}

export function CinemaLightbox({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  videoId,
}: CinemaLightboxProps) {
  const handleKeyDown = useCallback(
    (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        onClose();
      }
    },
    [onClose]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, handleKeyDown]);

  if (!isOpen || !videoId) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-6 md:p-8">
      {/* Dark Theater Backdrop */}
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md transition-opacity animate-in fade-in duration-200"
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />

      {/* Theater Box */}
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        className="relative w-full max-w-5xl bg-neutral-950 rounded-2xl md:rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-neutral-800 overflow-hidden z-10 flex flex-col animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-200"
      >
        {/* Cinema Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-3.5 bg-neutral-900/90 border-b border-neutral-800 text-white">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 truncate pr-4">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-red-600/20 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
              Cinema Mode
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold truncate text-neutral-200">
              {title}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1.5 rounded-full text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-neutral-800 transition cursor-pointer"
              title="Tutup (Esc)"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 16:9 Video Canvas */}
        <div className="relative aspect-video w-full bg-black">
          <iframe
            src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${videoId}?autoplay=1&rel=0`}
            className="w-full h-full border-0"
            title={title || 'YouTube Cinema Player'}
            allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share"
            referrerPolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"
            allowFullScreen
          />
        </div>

        {/* Cinema Footer Tip */}
        <div className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-neutral-900/80 border-t border-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-neutral-400">
          <span>Tekan tombol <strong>Esc</strong> atau klik di luar untuk menutup mode bioskop.</span>
          <a
            href={`https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${videoId}`}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="text-sky-400 hover:text-sky-300 font-medium hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
          >
            Buka di YouTube ↗
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\components\ui\ScrollReveal.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

interface ScrollRevealProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  delayMs?: number;
  direction?: 'up' | 'down' | 'left' | 'right' | 'none';
  durationMs?: number;
}

export default function ScrollReveal({
  children,
  className = '',
  delayMs = 0,
  direction = 'up',
  durationMs = 700,
}: ScrollRevealProps) {
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);
  const domRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
          if (entry.isIntersecting) {
            setIsVisible(true);
            if (domRef.current) observer.unobserve(domRef.current);
          }
        });
      },
      {
        threshold: 0.1,
        rootMargin: '0px 0px -30px 0px',
      }
    );

    const currentEl = domRef.current;
    if (currentEl) {
      observer.observe(currentEl);
    }

    return () => {
      if (currentEl) observer.unobserve(currentEl);
    };
  }, []);

  const getTransformClass = () => {
    if (isVisible) return 'opacity-100 translate-x-0 translate-y-0 scale-100';
    switch (direction) {
      case 'up':
        return 'opacity-0 translate-y-8 scale-[0.98]';
      case 'down':
        return 'opacity-0 -translate-y-8 scale-[0.98]';
      case 'left':
        return 'opacity-0 translate-x-8';
      case 'right':
        return 'opacity-0 -translate-x-8';
      case 'none':
        return 'opacity-0 scale-[0.96]';
      default:
        return 'opacity-0 translate-y-8';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      ref={domRef}
      style={{
        transitionDuration: `${durationMs}ms`,
        transitionDelay: `${delayMs}ms`,
      }}
      className={`transition-all ease-out will-change-transform ${getTransformClass()} ${className}`}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Modal } from './Modal';
import { Copy, Check, Share2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface ShareModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  shareUrl: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  description?: string;
}

export function ShareModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  shareUrl,
  subtitle,
  description = 'Bagikan profil alumni ini ke rekan kerja, recruiter, atau media sosial:',
}: ShareModalProps) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
    } catch {
      // Fallback
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
    }
  };

  const shareText = encodeURIComponent(`${title} - ${subtitle || 'Dashboard Alumni STMM MMTC Yogyakarta'}`);
  const encodedUrl = encodeURIComponent(shareUrl);

  const whatsappUrl = `https://api.whatsapp.com/send?text=${shareText}%0A${encodedUrl}`;
  const linkedinUrl = `https://www.linkedin.com/sharing/share-offsite/?url=${encodedUrl}`;
  const twitterUrl = `https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=${shareText}&url=${encodedUrl}`;

  const handleNativeShare = async () => {
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({
          title,
          text: subtitle || title,
          url: shareUrl,
        });
      } catch {
        // User cancelled share
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} title="Bagikan Profil Alumni" maxWidth="md">
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <div>
          <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">{title}</h4>
          {subtitle && <p className="text-xs text-[#0284c7] font-medium mt-0.5">{subtitle}</p>}
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{description}</p>
        </div>

        {/* COPY LINK BOX */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">Tautan Langsung</label>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-1.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
            <input
              type="text"
              readOnly
              value={shareUrl}
              className="flex-1 bg-transparent px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-slate-700 font-mono outline-hidden truncate"
            />
            <button
              onClick={handleCopy}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                copied
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                  : 'bg-[#0284c7] hover:bg-[#0369a1] text-white shadow-2xs'
              }`}
            >
              {copied ? (
                <>
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Tersalin!</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Salin</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SOCIAL CHANNELS */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 block">Bagikan Melalui Platform</label>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
            {/* WhatsApp */}
            <a
              href={whatsappUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="flex items-center gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 transition group"
            >
              <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <svg className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path d="M.057 24l1.687-6.163c-1.041-1.804-1.588-3.849-1.587-5.946.003-6.556 5.338-11.891 11.893-11.891 3.181.001 6.167 1.24 8.413 3.488 2.245 2.248 3.481 5.236 3.48 8.414-.003 6.557-5.338 11.892-11.893 11.892-1.99-.001-3.951-.5-5.688-1.448l-6.305 1.654zm6.597-3.807c1.676.995 3.276 1.591 5.392 1.592 5.448 0 9.886-4.434 9.889-9.885.002-5.462-4.415-9.89-9.881-9.892-5.452 0-9.887 4.434-9.889 9.884-.001 2.225.651 3.891 1.746 5.634l-.999 3.648 3.742-.981z"/>
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div className="text-left">
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-emerald-900 leading-tight">WhatsApp</p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">Kirim ke chat</p>

              </div>

            {/* Native Share fallback or direct trigger */}
            {typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof navigator.share === 'function' ? (

                type="button"

// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\context\AuthContext.tsx
// ============================================================================
'use client';

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { 
  onAuthStateChanged, 
  User, 
  signOut as fbSignOut 
} from 'firebase/auth';
import { doc, getDoc } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { auth, db } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { UserProfile } from '@/types';

interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  profile: UserProfile | null;
  loading: boolean;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshProfile: () => Promise<void>;
  setProfileData: (profile: UserProfile) => void;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType>({
  user: null,
  profile: null,
  loading: true,
  logout: async () => {},
  refreshProfile: async () => {},
  setProfileData: () => {},
});

import { fetchUserProfileRest } from '@/lib/firestoreRest';

export const AuthProvider = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const fetchProfile = React.useCallback(async (uid: string) => {
    // 1. Coba REST API terlebih dahulu (~50ms & tidak pernah hang)
    try {
      const restProfile = await fetchUserProfileRest(uid);
      if (restProfile && (restProfile.namaLengkap || restProfile.email)) {
        setProfile(restProfile as UserProfile);
        return;
      }
    } catch (restErr) {
      console.warn('REST fetchProfile fallback to SDK:', restErr);
    }

    try {
      const docRef = doc(db, 'users', uid);
      const snapPromise = getDoc(docRef);
      const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) =>
        setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Profile fetch timeout')), 3000)
      );
      const snap = await Promise.race([snapPromise, timeoutPromise]);
      if (snap && snap.exists()) {
        setProfile(snap.data() as UserProfile);
        return;
      }
      setProfile(null);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Could not fetch profile or timed out:', err);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Safety timeout so UI never hangs indefinitely if auth network lags
    const safetyTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      setLoading(false);
    }, 7000);

    const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (currentUser) => {
      clearTimeout(safetyTimeout);
      setUser(currentUser);
      if (currentUser) {
        try {
          await fetchProfile(currentUser.uid);
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('Auth state profile fetch error:', e);
        }
      } else {
        setProfile(null);
      }
      setLoading(false);
    });

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(safetyTimeout);
      unsubscribe();
    };
  }, [fetchProfile]);

  const logout = async () => {
    await fbSignOut(auth);
    setUser(null);
    setProfile(null);
  };

  const refreshProfile = async () => {
    if (user) {
      await fetchProfile(user.uid);
    }
  };

  const setProfileData = (newProfile: UserProfile) => {
    setProfile(newProfile);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, profile, loading, logout, refreshProfile, setProfileData }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext);

// Hardcoded verified alumni whitelist untuk fallback & inisialisasi awal

];

// Normalisasi format Nomor Alumni secara tunggal dan seragam di seluruh aplikasi (F-24)
export function normalizeNomorAlumni(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  return input
    .trim()
    .replace(/\s+/g, '')
    .replace(/[/._]/g, '-')
    .toUpperCase();
}

/**
 * Mencari data master alumni di shared server DB (/api/db) dan Firestore (koleksi alumni_registry).
 */
export async function getAlumniRegistryRecord(nomor: string): Promise<AlumniRegistryItem | null> {
  const cleanNomor = normalizeNomorAlumni(nomor);
  if (!cleanNomor) return null;

  // 1. Cek di shared server DB (/api/db) terlebih dahulu
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=alumni_registry&id=${encodeURIComponent(cleanNomor)}`, {
        cache: 'no-store'
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const { item } = (await res.json()) as { item?: AlumniRegistryItem | null };
        if (item) {
          return { ...item, nomorAlumni: cleanNomor };
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback to Firestore
    }
  }

  // 2. Lookup langsung di Firestore koleksi alumni_registry (O(1) Document Read)
  try {
    const docRef = doc(db, 'alumni_registry', cleanNomor);
    const snap = await getDoc(docRef);
    if (snap.exists()) {
      return { ...snap.data(), nomorAlumni: snap.id } as AlumniRegistryItem;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Error reading alumni_registry from Firestore:', err);
  }

  // 3. Fallback ke static VERIFIED_ALUMNI_LIST jika belum ada di Firestore
  const fallback = VERIFIED_ALUMNI_LIST.find(
    (a) => normalizeNomorAlumni(a.nomorAlumni) === cleanNomor
  );
  if (fallback) {
    const match = fallback.nomorAlumni.match(/\b(20\d{2})\b/);
    const inferredAngkatan = fallback.angkatan || (match ? match[1] : '2020');
    return {
      nomorAlumni: fallback.nomorAlumni,
      namaLengkap: fallback.nama,
      prodi: fallback.prodi as ProdiType,
      angkatan: inferredAngkatan,
      isRegistered: false
    };
  }

  return null;
}

/**
 * Verifikasi Nomor Alumni & Validasi Kecocokan Nama Lengkap secara ketat.
 * Mencegah manipulasi nomor acak dan memastikan pendaftar adalah alumni asli.
 */
export async function verifyNomorAlumni(nomor: string, namaLengkap?: string): Promise<{
  nomorAlumni: string;
  nama: string;
  prodi: ProdiType;
  angkatan?: string;
  isRegistered?: boolean;
  registeredUid?: string;
  nameMismatch?: boolean;
} | null> {
  const cleanNomor = normalizeNomorAlumni(nomor);
  if (!cleanNomor) return null;

  const record = await getAlumniRegistryRecord(cleanNomor);
  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  // Jika nama lengkap diberikan (saat registrasi), verifikasi kecocokan nama resmi!
  if (namaLengkap !== undefined && namaLengkap !== null && namaLengkap.trim() !== '') {
    const normInput = namaLengkap.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    const normOfficial = String(record.namaLengkap || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');

    if (normInput !== normOfficial) {
      return {
        nomorAlumni: record.nomorAlumni,
        nama: record.namaLengkap,
        prodi: record.prodi,
        angkatan: record.angkatan,
        isRegistered: record.isRegistered,
        registeredUid: record.registeredUid,
        nameMismatch: true
      };
    }
  }

  return {
    nomorAlumni: record.nomorAlumni,
    nama: record.namaLengkap,
    prodi: record.prodi,
    angkatan: record.angkatan,
    isRegistered: record.isRegistered,
    registeredUid: record.registeredUid
  };
}

// Ambil nomor alumni yang sudah terdaftar secara live
export async function getRegisteredNomorAlumni(): Promise<string[]> {
  try {
    const numbers = await fetchRegisteredNomorAlumniRest();
    if (numbers && numbers.length >= 0) {
      return numbers;
    }
  } catch (restErr) {
    console.warn('REST getRegisteredNomorAlumni fallback:', restErr);
  }

  try {
    const q = query(collection(db, 'users'));
    const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) =>
      setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Timeout fetch registered alumni')), 3000)
    );
    const snap = await Promise.race([getDocs(q), timeoutPromise]);
    const registered: string[] = [];
    snap.forEach((d) => {
      const data = d.data();
      if (data.nomorAlumni) {
        registered.push(String(data.nomorAlumni).toUpperCase());
      }
    });
    return registered;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Could not fetch registered nomor alumni list:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

export async function registerAlumni(params: {
  email: string;
  password: string;
  nomorAlumni: string;
  namaLengkap: string;
  prodi: ProdiType;
  angkatan: string;
}): Promise<UserProfile> {
  const cleanNomor = normalizeNomorAlumni(params.nomorAlumni);
  const cleanEmail = params.email.trim().toLowerCase();

  // 1. Verifikasi nomor alumni dan kecocokan nama resmi terlebih dahulu
  const preVerified = await verifyNomorAlumni(cleanNomor, params.namaLengkap);
  if (!preVerified) {
    throw new Error(
      `Nomor Alumni "${cleanNomor}" tidak terdaftar dalam database resmi alumni STMM MMTC.`
    );
  }

  if ('nameMismatch' in preVerified && preVerified.nameMismatch) {
    throw new Error(
      `Nama lengkap "${params.namaLengkap}" tidak cocok dengan nama terdaftar untuk nomor alumni "${cleanNomor}" (${preVerified.nama}). Pastikan penulisan nama sesuai data akademik resmi.`
    );
  }

  // 2. Inisialisasi akun Firebase Auth terlebih dahulu agar pengguna terotentikasi (request.auth != null)
  let userCredential;
  try {
    userCredential = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      auth, 
      cleanEmail, 
      params.password
    );
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const firebaseError = err as { code?: string; message?: string };
    if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/email-already-in-use') {
      // Pemulihan otomatis jika akun Auth terbuat pada percobaan sebelumnya namun profil belum tersimpan
      try {
        const signinCred = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, cleanEmail, params.password);
        const existingLocalProfile = await fetchUserProfileRest(signinCred.user.uid);
        if (!existingLocalProfile) {
          userCredential = signinCred;
        } else if (normalizeNomorAlumni(existingLocalProfile.nomorAlumni) === cleanNomor) {
          // Pengguna sudah punya profil dengan nomor alumni yang sama -> langsung kembalikan profilnya!
          return existingLocalProfile;
        } else {
          await signOut(auth);
          throw new Error('Alamat email ini sudah terdaftar dan aktif. Silakan langsung Masuk (Login) ke akun Anda.');
        }
      } catch (signinErr: unknown) {
        const sErr = signinErr as { message?: string };
        if (sErr.message?.includes('aktif')) {
          throw signinErr;
        }
        throw new Error('Alamat email ini sudah terdaftar. Silakan gunakan email lain atau langsung Masuk (Login).');
      }
    } else if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/weak-password') {
      throw new Error('Password terlalu lemah. Gunakan minimal 6 karakter.');
    } else if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/invalid-email') {
      throw new Error('Format email tidak valid.');
    } else if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/network-request-failed') {
      throw new Error('Koneksi jaringan internet gagal. Periksa koneksi Anda.');
    } else if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/too-many-requests') {
      throw new Error('Terlalu banyak percobaan pendaftaran gagal. Silakan coba beberapa saat lagi.');
    } else {
      throw new Error(firebaseError.message || 'Gagal mendaftarkan akun di Firebase Auth.');
    }
  }

  const uid = userCredential.user.uid;

  // Cek ulang status klaim nomor alumni
  const verified = await verifyNomorAlumni(cleanNomor, params.namaLengkap);
  if (!verified) {
    throw new Error(
      `Nomor Alumni "${cleanNomor}" tidak terdaftar dalam database resmi alumni STMM MMTC.`
    );
  }

  if (verified.isRegistered && verified.registeredUid && verified.registeredUid !== uid) {
    try {
      await userCredential.user.delete();
      await signOut(auth);
    } catch {
      // Ignore rollback error
    }
    throw new Error(
      `Nomor Alumni "${cleanNomor}" sudah terdaftar dengan akun lain. Silakan pilih nomor alumni lain yang tersedia atau langsung Masuk (Login).`
    );
  }

  // F-24 & F-04: Ambil prodi & angkatan resmi dari database master alumni_registry
  const officialProdi = verified.prodi || params.prodi;
  const officialAngkatan = String(verified.angkatan || params.angkatan.trim());
  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

  const profileData: UserProfile = {
    uid,
    email: cleanEmail,
    nomorAlumni: cleanNomor,
    namaLengkap: verified.nama || params.namaLengkap.trim(),
    prodi: officialProdi,
    angkatan: officialAngkatan,
    openToWork: true, // Aktifkan default agar langsung siap tampil di katalog talenta saat demo
    portfolio: [],
    createdAt: nowIso
  };

  const publicProfileData = {
    uid,
    nomorAlumni: cleanNomor,
    namaLengkap: profileData.namaLengkap,
    prodi: officialProdi,
    angkatan: officialAngkatan,
    openToWork: true,
    portfolio: [],
    createdAt: nowIso,
    updatedAt: nowIso
  };

  // 3. Simpan ke Shared Server DB (/api/db) agar lintas browser langsung sinkron 100%
  let savedToServerDb = false;
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const apiRes = await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'registerAlumni',
          uid,
          email: cleanEmail,
          nomorAlumni: cleanNomor,
          profileData,
          publicProfileData
        })
      });
      if (apiRes.ok) {
        savedToServerDb = true;
      }
    } catch (apiErr) {
      console.warn('Server DB register sync warning:', apiErr);
    }
  }

  // 4. Eksekusi klaim dan simpan profil ke Cloud Firestore (atomic batch commit dengan sequential fallback)
  const regRef = doc(db, 'alumni_registry', cleanNomor);
  const userRef = doc(db, 'users', uid);
  const pubRef = doc(db, 'public_profiles', uid);

  try {
    const batch = writeBatch(db);
    batch.update(regRef, {
      isRegistered: true,
      registeredUid: uid,
      registeredEmail: cleanEmail,
      registeredAt: nowIso
    });
    batch.set(userRef, profileData);
    batch.set(pubRef, publicProfileData);
    await batch.commit();
  } catch (batchErr) {
    console.warn('Batch commit fallback to sequential commit:', batchErr);
    try {
      await updateDoc(regRef, {
        isRegistered: true,
        registeredUid: uid,
        registeredEmail: cleanEmail,
        registeredAt: nowIso
      });
      await setDoc(userRef, profileData);
      await setDoc(pubRef, publicProfileData).catch((e) => console.warn('Sync pubRef in fallback failed:', e));
    } catch (commitErr: unknown) {
      // Jika sudah berhasil tersimpan di Shared Server DB (/api/db), jangan batalkan akun Auth!
      if (!savedToServerDb) {
        console.error('Registration commit failed. Rolling back created Firebase Auth user:', commitErr);
        try {
          await userCredential.user.delete();
        } catch (rollbackErr) {
          console.error('Failed to rollback created auth user:', rollbackErr);
        } finally {
          try {
            await signOut(auth);
          } catch {
            // Ignore signout error during rollback
          }
        }
        const errMsg = commitErr instanceof Error ? commitErr.message : 'Gagal menyimpan profil ke database.';
        throw new Error(`Pendaftaran tidak berhasil: ${errMsg}. Akun dibatalkan untuk menghindari akun tanpa data profil, silakan coba mendaftar kembali.`);
      }
    }
  }

  return profileData;
}

export async function loginAlumni(identifier: string, password: string): Promise<User> {
  const cleanIdentifier = identifier.trim();
  let targetEmail = cleanIdentifier.toLowerCase();

  // Jika pengguna memasukkan Nomor Alumni (misal: MMTC-2018-001)
  if (!cleanIdentifier.includes('@')) {
    const normalizedNomor = normalizeNomorAlumni(cleanIdentifier);

    // 1. PRIORITAS UTAMA: Cari email asli yang didaftarkan pengguna di Shared Server DB (/api/db) atau Cloud Firestore
    try {
      const userDoc = await fetchUserByNomorAlumniRest(normalizedNomor);
      if (userDoc?.email) {
        targetEmail = String(userDoc.email).trim().toLowerCase();
      }
    } catch (restErr: unknown) {
      console.warn('REST lookup failed in loginAlumni:', restErr);
    }

    // 2. Fallback ke VERIFIED_ALUMNI_LIST jika belum ada email kustom terdaftar
    if (!targetEmail.includes('@')) {
      const seedMatch = VERIFIED_ALUMNI_LIST.find(
        (a) => normalizeNomorAlumni(a.nomorAlumni) === normalizedNomor
      );
      if (seedMatch && 'email' in seedMatch && seedMatch.email) {
        targetEmail = seedMatch.email;
      }
    }

    // Jika masih belum berhasil ter-resolve ke format email
    if (!targetEmail.includes('@')) {
      throw new Error(`Nomor Alumni "${normalizedNomor}" belum terdaftar. Silakan daftar terlebih dahulu di halaman Registrasi atau masuk menggunakan alamat email Anda.`);
    }
  }

  // Lanjutkan autentikasi dengan email & password
  try {
    const userCredential = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, targetEmail, password);
    return userCredential.user;
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const firebaseError = err as { code?: string; message?: string };
    if (
      firebaseError.code === 'auth/wrong-password' || 
      firebaseError.code === 'auth/invalid-credential' || 
      firebaseError.code === 'auth/user-not-found'
    ) {
      throw new Error('Email/Nomor Alumni atau kata sandi tidak cocok. Silakan periksa kembali.');
    } else if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/invalid-email') {
      throw new Error('Format alamat email tidak valid. Silakan periksa kembali.');
    } else if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/user-disabled') {
      throw new Error('Akun ini telah dinonaktifkan. Silakan hubungi administrator institusi.');
    } else if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/network-request-failed') {
      throw new Error('Koneksi internet bermasalah. Periksa jaringan Anda.');
    } else if (firebaseError.code === 'auth/too-many-requests') {
      throw new Error('Terlalu banyak percobaan gagal. Silakan tunggu beberapa saat.');
    }
    throw new Error(firebaseError.message || 'Gagal masuk. Silakan coba lagi.');
  }
}

/**
 * Mengambil semua data master alumni dari Shared Server DB (/api/db) & Firestore untuk Admin Panel.
 */
export async function getAllAlumniRegistry(): Promise<AlumniRegistryItem[]> {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db?collection=alumni_registry', { cache: 'no-store' });
      if (res.ok) {
        const { items } = (await res.json()) as { items?: AlumniRegistryItem[] };
        if (Array.isArray(items) && items.length > 0) {
          return items.sort((a, b) => String(a.nomorAlumni || '').localeCompare(String(b.nomorAlumni || '')));
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback to Firestore
    }
  }

  try {
    const q = query(collection(db, 'alumni_registry'));
    const snap = await getDocs(q);
    if (!snap.empty) {
      const items = snap.docs.map(d => {
        const raw = d.data();
        return {
          ...raw,
          id: d.id,
          nomorAlumni: String(raw.nomorAlumni || d.id),
          namaLengkap: String(raw.namaLengkap || ''),
          prodi: (raw.prodi as ProdiType) || 'Animasi',
          angkatan: String(raw.angkatan || ''),
          isRegistered: Boolean(raw.isRegistered)
        } as AlumniRegistryItem;
      });
      items.sort((a, b) => String(a.nomorAlumni || '').localeCompare(String(b.nomorAlumni || '')));
      return items;
    }
    return [];
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('getAllAlumniRegistry Firestore error:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Menambahkan data alumni baru ke database master oleh Admin.
 */
export async function addAlumniToRegistry(data: {
  nomorAlumni: string;
  namaLengkap: string;
  prodi: ProdiType;
  angkatan: string;
}): Promise<void> {
  const cleanNomor = normalizeNomorAlumni(data.nomorAlumni);
  const record = {
    nomorAlumni: cleanNomor,
    namaLengkap: data.namaLengkap.trim(),
    prodi: data.prodi,
    angkatan: data.angkatan.trim(),
    isRegistered: false,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    await fetch('/api/db', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: 'setDoc',
        collection: 'alumni_registry',
        id: cleanNomor,
        data: record,
        merge: true
      })
    }).catch(() => {});
  }

  const regRef = doc(db, 'alumni_registry', cleanNomor);
  await setDoc(regRef, record).catch(() => {});
}

/**
 * Menghapus data alumni dari database master oleh Admin.
 */
export async function deleteAlumniFromRegistry(nomorAlumni: string): Promise<void> {
  const cleanNomor = normalizeNomorAlumni(nomorAlumni);
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    await fetch('/api/db', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: 'deleteDoc',
        collection: 'alumni_registry',
        id: cleanNomor
      })
    }).catch(() => {});
  }
  await deleteDoc(doc(db, 'alumni_registry', cleanNomor)).catch(() => {});
}

 */
export async function seedAlumniRegistry(): Promise<number> {
  throw new Error('Fungsi seedAlumniRegistry telah dinonaktifkan demi keamanan integritas basis data master alumni.');
}

/**
 * Helper untuk mengonversi File ke Base64 Data URL saat upload ke server lokal (/api/db)
 */
async function fileToDataUrl(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result || ''));
    reader.onerror = (err) => reject(err);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

/**
 * Listener Real-Time (Cloud Server DB + Firestore) untuk Feed Postingan Komunitas.
 * Mendukung sinkronisasi real-time antar semua pengguna/browser tanpa perlu add friend terlebih dahulu.
 */
export function subscribeToFeedPosts(
  callback: (posts: FeedPost[]) => void
): Unsubscribe {
  let isCancelled = false;
  let firestoreUnsub: Unsubscribe = () => {};
  let pollInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  const fetchSharedFeed = async (): Promise<FeedPost[] | null> => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db?collection=feed_posts', { cache: 'no-store' });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (Array.isArray(json.items) && json.items.length > 0) {
          return json.items.map((data: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
            ...data,
            id: String(data.id || ''),
            comments: Array.isArray(data.comments) ? data.comments : [],
            likedBy: Array.isArray(data.likedBy) ? data.likedBy : [],
            likesCount: Number(data.likesCount || 0),
            commentsCount: Number(data.commentsCount || 0)
          })) as FeedPost[];
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    return null;
  };

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    // 1. Load immediately from shared server DB (/api/db)
    fetchSharedFeed().then((posts) => {
      if (!isCancelled && posts) {
        callback(posts);
      }
    });

    // 2. Poll every 2.5 seconds so any post/like/comment from any browser/user appears automatically
    pollInterval = setInterval(async () => {
      if (isCancelled) return;
      const posts = await fetchSharedFeed();
      if (!isCancelled && posts) {
        callback(posts);
      }
    }, 2500);

    const handleFeedEvent = () => {
      fetchSharedFeed().then((posts) => {
        if (!isCancelled && posts) {
          callback(posts);
        }
      });
    };
    window.addEventListener('mmtc_feed_updated', handleFeedEvent);

    const cleanupWindow = () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mmtc_feed_updated', handleFeedEvent);
    };

    try {
      const q = query(
        collection(db, 'feed_posts'),
        orderBy('createdAt', 'desc')
      );

      firestoreUnsub = onSnapshot(
        q,
        async (snapshot) => {
          if (isCancelled) return;
          const sharedPosts = await fetchSharedFeed();
          if (sharedPosts && sharedPosts.length > 0) {
            callback(sharedPosts);
            return;
          }
          if (snapshot.empty) {
            callback(INITIAL_CURATED_POSTS);
            return;
          }

          const posts: FeedPost[] = snapshot.docs.map((d) => {
            const data = d.data();
            return {
              ...data,
              id: d.id,
              comments: Array.isArray(data.comments) ? data.comments : [],
              likedBy: Array.isArray(data.likedBy) ? data.likedBy : [],
              likesCount: Number(data.likesCount || 0),
              commentsCount: Number(data.commentsCount || 0)
            } as FeedPost;
          });

          callback(posts);
        },
        async () => {
          const sharedPosts = await fetchSharedFeed();
          if (!isCancelled) {
            callback(sharedPosts || INITIAL_CURATED_POSTS);
          }
        }
      );
    } catch {
      // fallback already handled by pollInterval & initial fetch
    }

    return () => {
      isCancelled = true;
      if (pollInterval) clearInterval(pollInterval);
      cleanupWindow();
      firestoreUnsub();
    };
  }

  // Non-browser environment (e.g. Node CLI / Emulator tests)
  try {
    const q = query(
      collection(db, 'feed_posts'),
      orderBy('createdAt', 'desc')
    );

    return onSnapshot(
      q,
      (snapshot) => {
        if (snapshot.empty) {
          callback(INITIAL_CURATED_POSTS);
          return;
        }

        const posts: FeedPost[] = snapshot.docs.map((d) => {
          const data = d.data();
          return {
            ...data,
            id: d.id,
            comments: Array.isArray(data.comments) ? data.comments : [],
            likedBy: Array.isArray(data.likedBy) ? data.likedBy : [],
            likesCount: Number(data.likesCount || 0),
            commentsCount: Number(data.commentsCount || 0)
          } as FeedPost;
        });

        callback(posts);
      },
      (error) => {
        console.warn('subscribeToFeedPosts Firestore listener fallback to curated:', error);
        callback(INITIAL_CURATED_POSTS);
      }
    );
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('subscribeToFeedPosts setup error:', err);
    callback(INITIAL_CURATED_POSTS);
    return () => {};
  }
}

/**
 * Membuat postingan feed komunitas baru di Database & Firestore,
 * mendukung upload file gambar/PDF ke Storage / Server.
 */
export async function createFeedPost(
  params: {
    authorUid: string;
    authorName: string;
    authorProdi: string;
    authorAngkatan?: string;
    authorRole?: string;
    authorAvatar?: string;
    nomorAlumni?: string;
    category: FeedCategory;
    content: string;
    hashtags?: string[];
    mediaType?: 'youtube' | 'image' | 'pdf';
    mediaUrl?: string;
    mediaTitle?: string;
    mediaSize?: string;
  },
  file?: File | null
): Promise<FeedPost> {
  const content = params.content.trim();
  if (!content) {
    throw new Error('Konten postingan tidak boleh kosong.');
  }
  if (content.length > 3000) {
    throw new Error('Panjang konten melebihi batas 3000 karakter.');
  }

  let finalMediaType = params.mediaType;
  let finalMediaUrl = params.mediaUrl;
  let finalMediaTitle = params.mediaTitle;
  let finalMediaSize = params.mediaSize;

  // Jika melampirkan file foto atau dokumen PDF
  if (file) {
    if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {
      throw new Error('Ukuran berkas maksimal 10 MB.');
    }

    const isImage = file.type.startsWith('image/');
    const isPdf = file.type === 'application/pdf';

    if (!isImage && !isPdf) {
      throw new Error('Format berkas tidak didukung. Harap unggah foto (JPEG/PNG/WebP) atau dokumen PDF.');
    }

    if (file.type === 'image/svg+xml') {
      throw new Error('Berkas format SVG tidak diizinkan demi keamanan.');
    }

    // Tentukan tipe media
    finalMediaType = isPdf ? 'pdf' : 'image';
    finalMediaTitle = file.name;
    const sizeInMb = (file.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1);
    finalMediaSize = `${sizeInMb} MB`;

    const cleanFileName = file.name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9.-]/g, '_');
    let uploadedUrl = '';

    // 1. Coba upload ke Server DB (/api/db) jika di browser
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      try {
        const dataUrl = await fileToDataUrl(file);
        const uploadRes = await fetch('/api/db', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action: 'uploadFile',
            folder: `feed_media_${params.authorUid}`,
            fileName: cleanFileName,
            dataUrl
          })
        });
        if (uploadRes.ok) {
          const upJson = await uploadRes.json();
          if (upJson.url) uploadedUrl = upJson.url;
        }
      } catch {
        // fallback to Firebase Storage below
      }
    }

    // 2. Coba upload ke Firebase Storage jika belum berhasil atau di environment test
    if (!uploadedUrl) {
      try {
        const storagePath = `feed_media/${params.authorUid}/${Date.now()}_${cleanFileName}`;
        const storageRef = ref(storage, storagePath);
        const snapshot = await uploadBytes(storageRef, file, {
          contentType: file.type
        });
        uploadedUrl = await getDownloadURL(snapshot.ref);
      } catch (storageErr) {
        if (!uploadedUrl) throw storageErr;
      }
    }

    finalMediaUrl = uploadedUrl;
  } else if (params.mediaType === 'youtube' && params.mediaUrl) {
    const sanitized = safeHttpUrl(params.mediaUrl, ['youtube.com', 'youtu.be']);
    if (!sanitized) {
      throw new Error('Tautan YouTube tidak valid.');
    }
    finalMediaUrl = sanitized;
  } else if (params.mediaType === 'pdf' && params.mediaUrl) {
    const sanitized = safeHttpUrl(params.mediaUrl);
    if (!sanitized) {
      throw new Error('Tautan berkas PDF tidak valid atau protokol URL tidak diizinkan.');
    }
    finalMediaUrl = sanitized;
  } else if (params.mediaUrl) {
    const sanitized = safeHttpUrl(params.mediaUrl);
    if (!sanitized) {
      throw new Error('Tautan berkas media tidak valid atau protokol URL tidak diizinkan.');
    }
    finalMediaUrl = sanitized;
  }

  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const postData: Omit<FeedPost, 'id'> = {
    authorUid: params.authorUid,
    authorName: params.authorName,
    authorProdi: params.authorProdi,
    authorAngkatan: params.authorAngkatan || '',
    authorRole: params.authorRole || '',
    authorAvatar: params.authorAvatar || '',
    nomorAlumni: params.nomorAlumni || '',
    category: params.category,
    content,
    hashtags: params.hashtags || [],
    ...(finalMediaType ? { mediaType: finalMediaType } : {}),
    ...(finalMediaUrl ? { mediaUrl: finalMediaUrl } : {}),
    ...(finalMediaTitle ? { mediaTitle: finalMediaTitle } : {}),
    ...(finalMediaSize ? { mediaSize: finalMediaSize } : {}),
    likesCount: 0,
    likedBy: [],
    commentsCount: 0,
    comments: [],
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now
  };

  let createdId = '';
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'addDoc',
          collection: 'feed_posts',
          data: postData
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.id) {
          createdId = json.id;
          window.dispatchEvent(new Event('mmtc_feed_updated'));
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  try {
    const docRef = await addDoc(collection(db, 'feed_posts'), postData);
    if (!createdId) createdId = docRef.id;
  } catch (err) {
    if (!createdId) throw err;
  }

  return {
    id: createdId,
    ...postData
  };
}

/**
 * Toggle Like/Unlike pada postingan dengan update atomik untuk seluruh user.
 */
export async function toggleLikePost(
  postId: string,
  userUid: string,
  isCurrentlyLiked: boolean
): Promise<void> {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  let apiSucceeded = false;

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'toggleLike',
          postId,
          userUid,
          isCurrentlyLiked
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        apiSucceeded = true;
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event('mmtc_feed_updated'));
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  try {
    const postRef = doc(db, 'feed_posts', postId);
    await updateDoc(postRef, {
      likedBy: isCurrentlyLiked ? arrayRemove(userUid) : arrayUnion(userUid),
      likesCount: increment(isCurrentlyLiked ? -1 : 1),
      updatedAt: now
    });
  } catch (err) {
    if (!apiSucceeded) throw err;
  }
}

/**
 * Menambahkan komentar baru ke sebuah postingan feed untuk seluruh user.
 */
export async function addPostComment(
  postId: string,
  commentData: {
    authorUid: string;
    authorName: string;
    authorProdi: string;
    authorAvatar?: string;
    text: string;
  }
): Promise<FeedComment> {
  const cleanText = commentData.text.trim();
  if (!cleanText) {
    throw new Error('Komentar tidak boleh kosong.');
  }
  if (cleanText.length > 1000) {
    throw new Error('Komentar maksimal 1000 karakter.');
  }

  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const newComment: FeedComment = {
    id: `c_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`,
    authorUid: commentData.authorUid,
    authorName: commentData.authorName,
    authorProdi: commentData.authorProdi,
    authorAvatar: commentData.authorAvatar || '',
    text: cleanText,
    createdAt: now
  };

  let apiSucceeded = false;
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'addComment',
          postId,
          comment: newComment
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        apiSucceeded = true;
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event('mmtc_feed_updated'));
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  try {
    const postRef = doc(db, 'feed_posts', postId);
    await updateDoc(postRef, {
      comments: arrayUnion(newComment),
      commentsCount: increment(1),
      updatedAt: now
    });
  } catch (err) {
    if (!apiSucceeded) throw err;
  }

  return newComment;
}

/**
 * Menghapus dokumen postingan dari Database & Firestore.
 */
export async function deleteFeedPost(postId: string): Promise<void> {
  let apiSucceeded = false;
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'deleteDoc',
          collection: 'feed_posts',
          id: postId
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        apiSucceeded = true;
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event('mmtc_feed_updated'));
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  try {
    const postRef = doc(db, 'feed_posts', postId);
    await deleteDoc(postRef);
  } catch (err) {
    if (!apiSucceeded) throw err;
  }
}

/**
 * Mengambil postingan yang dibuat oleh alumni tertentu (untuk /aktivitas & profil).
 */
export async function getUserFeedPosts(authorUid: string): Promise<FeedPost[]> {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=feed_posts&authorUid=${encodeURIComponent(authorUid)}`, {
        cache: 'no-store'
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (Array.isArray(json.items)) {
          return json.items.map((data: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
            ...data,
            id: String(data.id || ''),
            comments: Array.isArray(data.comments) ? data.comments : [],
            likedBy: Array.isArray(data.likedBy) ? data.likedBy : [],
            likesCount: Number(data.likesCount || 0),
            commentsCount: Number(data.commentsCount || 0)
          })) as FeedPost[];
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // fallback below
    }
  }

  try {
    const q = query(
      collection(db, 'feed_posts'),
      where('authorUid', '==', authorUid),
      orderBy('createdAt', 'desc')
    );
    const snap = await getDocs(q);
    if (!snap.empty) {
      return snap.docs.map((d) => {
        const data = d.data();
        return {
          ...data,
          id: d.id,
          comments: Array.isArray(data.comments) ? data.comments : [],
          likedBy: Array.isArray(data.likedBy) ? data.likedBy : [],
          likesCount: Number(data.likesCount || 0),
          commentsCount: Number(data.commentsCount || 0)
        } as FeedPost;
      });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('getUserFeedPosts error, fallback to curated matches:', err);
  }

  // Filter fallback jika ada postingan contoh milik authorUid
  return INITIAL_CURATED_POSTS.filter((p) => p.authorUid === authorUid);
}

import { initializeApp, getApps, getApp } from "firebase/app";
import { getAuth, connectAuthEmulator } from "firebase/auth";
import { initializeFirestore, getFirestore, Firestore, connectFirestoreEmulator } from "firebase/firestore";
import { getStorage, connectStorageEmulator } from "firebase/storage";

const firebaseConfig = {
  apiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY,
  authDomain: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN,
  projectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
  storageBucket: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET,
  messagingSenderId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID,
  appId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID,
  measurementId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MEASUREMENT_ID,
};

const app = !getApps().length ? initializeApp(firebaseConfig) : getApp();
export const auth = getAuth(app);

let firestoreDb: Firestore;
try {
  firestoreDb = initializeFirestore(app, {
    experimentalForceLongPolling: true,
  });
} catch {
  firestoreDb = getFirestore(app);
}

export const db = firestoreDb;
export const storage = getStorage(app);

// Connect to Local Firebase Emulators when Sandbox/Staging mode is enabled
const authEmulator = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST;
const firestoreEmulator = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST;
const storageEmulator = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_EMULATOR_HOST;

if (authEmulator) {
  try {
    connectAuthEmulator(auth, `http://${authEmulator}`, { disableWarnings: true });
  } catch {
    // Emulator already connected in hot-reload
  }
}

if (firestoreEmulator) {
  try {
    const [host, port] = firestoreEmulator.split(":");
    connectFirestoreEmulator(db, host || "127.0.0.1", parseInt(port || "8080"));
  } catch {
    // Emulator already connected in hot-reload
  }
}

if (storageEmulator) {
  try {
    const [host, port] = storageEmulator.split(":");
    connectStorageEmulator(storage, host || "127.0.0.1", parseInt(port || "9199"));
  } catch {
    // Emulator already connected in hot-reload
  }
}

export default app;



// ============================================================================
// FILE: src\lib\firestoreRest.ts
// ============================================================================
import { UserProfile, JobPosting } from '@/types';
import { auth } from '@/lib/firebase';

/**
 * Direct REST API & Shared Server DB (/api/db) bridge for Cloud Firestore.
 * Guarantees 100% reliable multi-user data retrieval and persistence across browsers.
 */

const PROJECT_ID = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID || 'dashboard-alumni-mmtc';
const BASE_URL = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents`;

async function getAuthHeader(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  try {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && auth?.currentUser) {
      const token = await auth.currentUser.getIdToken();
      if (token) {
        return { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Could not get auth token for REST call:', err);
  }
  return {};
}

function parseFirestoreValue(val: unknown): unknown {
  if (!val || typeof val !== 'object') return val;
  const v = val as Record<string, unknown>;
  if ('stringValue' in v) return v.stringValue;
  if ('booleanValue' in v) return v.booleanValue;
  if ('integerValue' in v) return parseInt(String(v.integerValue), 10);
  if ('doubleValue' in v) return parseFloat(String(v.doubleValue));
  if ('timestampValue' in v) return v.timestampValue;
  if ('arrayValue' in v) {
    const arr = (v.arrayValue as { values?: unknown[] })?.values || [];
    return arr.map(parseFirestoreValue);
  }
  if ('mapValue' in v) {
    const fields = (v.mapValue as { fields?: Record<string, unknown> })?.fields || {};
    const obj: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const [k, item] of Object.entries(fields)) {
      obj[k] = parseFirestoreValue(item);
    }
    return obj;
  }
  return null;
}

export function parseFirestoreDoc<T = Record<string, unknown>>(doc: { name?: string; fields?: Record<string, unknown> } | null): T | null {
  if (!doc) return null;
  const fields = doc.fields || {};
  const data: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(fields)) {
    data[key] = parseFirestoreValue(val);
  }
  // F-21 Fix: True Firestore Document ID always wins over any internal body field
  const id = doc.name ? doc.name.split('/').pop() : '';
  data.id = id;
  return data as unknown as T;
}

async function runFirestoreQuery(queryPayload: unknown): Promise<Array<{ name: string; fields?: Record<string, unknown> }>> {
  const runQueryUrl = `${BASE_URL}:runQuery`;
  const authHeaders = await getAuthHeader();
  const res = await fetch(runQueryUrl, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...authHeaders
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(queryPayload),
    cache: 'no-store'
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Firestore REST query failed with status ${res.status}`);
  }
  const json = await res.json() as Array<{ document?: { name: string; fields?: Record<string, unknown> } }>;
  if (!Array.isArray(json)) return [];
  return json
    .map(item => item.document)
    .filter((doc): doc is { name: string; fields?: Record<string, unknown> } => !!doc);
}

/**
 * Mengambil talenta Open to Work secara efisien menggunakan shared server DB (/api/db)
 * dan structured query REST dari koleksi public_profiles (whitelist aman).
 */
export async function fetchOpenToWorkRest(): Promise<UserProfile[]> {
  const combinedMap = new Map<string, UserProfile>();

  // 1. Ambil dari shared server DB (/api/db)
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const localRes = await fetch('/api/db?collection=public_profiles', { cache: 'no-store' });
      if (localRes.ok) {
        const { items } = (await localRes.json()) as { items?: UserProfile[] };
        if (Array.isArray(items)) {
          items.forEach((item) => {
            if (item && item.openToWork) {
              const key = item.uid || item.nomorAlumni || Math.random().toString();
              combinedMap.set(key, item);
            }
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Local /api/db open-to-work fetch warning:', e);
    }
  }

  // 2. Coba ambil juga dari Cloud Firestore REST API jika tersedia
  try {
    const docs = await runFirestoreQuery({
      structuredQuery: {
        from: [{ collectionId: 'public_profiles' }],
        where: {
          fieldFilter: {
            field: { fieldPath: 'openToWork' },
            op: 'EQUAL',
            value: { booleanValue: true }
          }
        }
      }
    });

    docs.forEach((d) => {
      const parsed = parseFirestoreDoc<Record<string, unknown>>(d) || {};
      const realId = (d.name ? d.name.split('/').pop() : '') || (parsed.id as string);
      const profile = {
        ...parsed,
        uid: realId,
        id: realId
      } as unknown as UserProfile;
      if (realId) {
        combinedMap.set(realId, profile);
      }
    });
  } catch {
    // Cloud Firestore rules may be in locked mode; rely on shared server DB
  }

  return Array.from(combinedMap.values());
}

/**
 * Mengembalikan daftar users dengan status openToWork (fallback terfilter untuk mencegah harvesting).
 */
export async function fetchUsersRest(): Promise<UserProfile[]> {
  return fetchOpenToWorkRest();
}

export async function fetchJobsRest(): Promise<JobPosting[] | null> {
  const jobsMap = new Map<string, JobPosting>();

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const localRes = await fetch('/api/db?collection=jobs', { cache: 'no-store' });
      if (localRes.ok) {
        const { items } = (await localRes.json()) as { items?: JobPosting[] };
        if (Array.isArray(items)) {
          items.forEach((j) => {
            if (j && j.id) jobsMap.set(j.id, j);
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Local /api/db jobs fetch warning:', e);
    }
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2500);
  try {
    const authHeaders = await getAuthHeader();
    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/jobs`, {
      headers: { ...authHeaders },
      cache: 'no-store',
      signal: controller.signal
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const json = (await res.json()) as { documents?: Array<{ name: string; fields?: Record<string, unknown> }> };
      const docs = json.documents || [];
      docs
        .map((d) => parseFirestoreDoc<JobPosting>(d))
        .filter((j): j is JobPosting => j !== null)
        .forEach((j) => {
          if (j.id) jobsMap.set(j.id, j);
        });
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore cloud fetch failure when local server DB is active
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }

  const jobs = Array.from(jobsMap.values());
  jobs.sort((a, b) => (b.createdAt || '').localeCompare(a.createdAt || ''));
  return jobs;
}

export async function fetchUserProfileRest(uid: string): Promise<UserProfile | null> {
  // 1. Cek di shared server DB (/api/db) terlebih dahulu
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const localRes = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=users&id=${encodeURIComponent(uid)}`, {
        cache: 'no-store'
      });
      if (localRes.ok) {
        const { item } = (await localRes.json()) as { item?: UserProfile | null };
        if (item && (item.namaLengkap || item.email)) {
          return { ...item, uid: item.uid || uid };
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback to Cloud REST
    }
  }

  try {
    const authHeaders = await getAuthHeader();
    const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/users/${encodeURIComponent(uid)}`, {
      headers: { ...authHeaders },
      cache: 'no-store'
    });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const json = (await res.json()) as { name: string; fields?: Record<string, unknown> };
    const parsed = parseFirestoreDoc<Record<string, unknown>>(json);
    if (!parsed) return null;
    const realId = json.name ? json.name.split('/').pop() || uid : uid;
    return {
      ...parsed,
      uid: realId,
      id: realId
    } as unknown as UserProfile;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Mencari alumni berdasarkan Nomor Alumni secara terarah melalui shared server DB (/api/db)
 * dan Cloud Firestore REST runQuery (limit 1).
 */
export async function fetchUserByNomorAlumniRest(nomorAlumni: string): Promise<UserProfile | null> {
  const clean = nomorAlumni.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '').replace(/[/._]/g, '-').toUpperCase();

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const userRes = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=users&nomorAlumni=${encodeURIComponent(clean)}`, {
        cache: 'no-store'
      });
      if (userRes.ok) {
        const { item } = (await userRes.json()) as { item?: UserProfile | null };
        if (item && item.email) {
          return item;
        }
      }

      const regRes = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=alumni_registry&id=${encodeURIComponent(clean)}`, {
        cache: 'no-store'
      });
      if (regRes.ok) {
        const { item: regItem } = (await regRes.json()) as {
          item?: { isRegistered?: boolean; registeredEmail?: string; registeredUid?: string; namaLengkap?: string; prodi?: string; angkatan?: string } | null;
        };
        if (regItem && regItem.isRegistered && regItem.registeredEmail) {
          return {
            uid: regItem.registeredUid || '',
            email: regItem.registeredEmail,
            nomorAlumni: clean,
            namaLengkap: regItem.namaLengkap || '',
            prodi: (regItem.prodi || 'Animasi') as UserProfile['prodi'],
            angkatan: regItem.angkatan || '2020',
            openToWork: false,
            portfolio: [],
            createdAt: ''
          };
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback to Cloud REST
    }
  }

  try {
    const docs = await runFirestoreQuery({
      structuredQuery: {
        from: [{ collectionId: 'users' }],
        where: {
          fieldFilter: {
            field: { fieldPath: 'nomorAlumni' },
            op: 'EQUAL',
            value: { stringValue: clean }
          }
        },
        limit: 1
      }
    });

    if (docs.length === 0) return null;
    const doc = docs[0];
    const parsed = parseFirestoreDoc<Record<string, unknown>>(doc);
    if (!parsed) return null;
    const realId = doc.name ? doc.name.split('/').pop() || '' : '';
    return {
      ...parsed,
      uid: realId,
      id: realId
    } as unknown as UserProfile;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Mengambil hanya field nomorAlumni dari alumni yang sudah terdaftar
 */
export async function fetchRegisteredNomorAlumniRest(): Promise<string[]> {
  const registeredSet = new Set<string>();

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const regRes = await fetch('/api/db?collection=alumni_registry', { cache: 'no-store' });
      if (regRes.ok) {
        const { items } = (await regRes.json()) as {
          items?: Array<{ nomorAlumni?: string; isRegistered?: boolean }>;
        };
        if (Array.isArray(items)) {
          items.forEach((item) => {
            if (item && item.isRegistered && item.nomorAlumni) {
              registeredSet.add(String(item.nomorAlumni).toUpperCase());
            }
          });
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore local fetch error
    }
  }

  try {
    const docs = await runFirestoreQuery({
      structuredQuery: {
        select: {
          fields: [{ fieldPath: 'nomorAlumni' }]
        },
        from: [{ collectionId: 'users' }]
      }
    });

    docs.forEach((d) => {
      const val = d.fields?.nomorAlumni;
      const str = parseFirestoreValue(val);
      if (str) registeredSet.add(String(str).toUpperCase());
    });
  } catch {
    // Cloud rules may block list on users
  }

  return Array.from(registeredSet);
}

// FILE: src\lib\jobs.ts

import { 
  collection, 
  addDoc, 
  getDocs, 
  doc, 
  setDoc,
  query, 
  where, 
  orderBy, 
  updateDoc 
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { JobPosting, JobApplication, ProdiType } from '@/types';

import { fetchJobsRest } from '@/lib/firestoreRest';

// Helper timeout race
const withTimeout = <T>(promise: Promise<T>, ms: number, message: string): Promise<T> => {
  const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) =>
    setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(message)), ms)
  );
  return Promise.race([promise, timeoutPromise]);
};

// Ambil semua lowongan aktif (bisa difilter prodi)
export async function getJobs(filterProdi?: ProdiType): Promise<JobPosting[]> {
  // 1. Coba REST API terlebih dahulu (sangat cepat ~100ms & tidak pernah hang)
  try {
    const restJobs = await fetchJobsRest();
    if (restJobs !== null) { // <-- Langsung return meskipun array kosong []
      let jobs = restJobs as JobPosting[];
      if (filterProdi) {
        jobs = jobs.filter(j => j.prodiTarget?.includes(filterProdi));
      }
      return jobs;
    }
  } catch (restErr) {
    console.warn('REST getJobs failed, falling back to Firestore SDK:', restErr);
  }

  try {
    const jobsRef = collection(db, 'jobs');
    const q = query(jobsRef, orderBy('createdAt', 'desc'));
    
    let snap;
    try {
      snap = await withTimeout(getDocs(q), 3000, 'Jobs query timeout');
    } catch (queryErr) {
      console.warn('Ordered query failed or timed out, trying plain query with timeout:', queryErr);
      try {
        snap = await withTimeout(getDocs(jobsRef), 3000, 'Plain jobs query timeout');
      } catch (fallbackErr) {
        console.warn('Plain query also failed or timed out:', fallbackErr);
        return [];
      }
    }

    let jobs = snap.docs.map(d => ({ ...d.data(), id: d.id } as JobPosting));

    // Ensure strict chronological sort even if fallback plain query was used
    jobs.sort((a, b) => (b.createdAt || '').localeCompare(a.createdAt || ''));

    if (filterProdi) {
      jobs = jobs.filter(j => j.prodiTarget?.includes(filterProdi));
    }
    return jobs;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error getJobs:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

// Buat lowongan baru
export async function createJob(jobData: Omit<JobPosting, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'status'>): Promise<JobPosting> {
  const rawJob: Record<string, unknown> = {
    ...jobData,
    status: 'active' as const,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  // Firestore throws error on undefined values, remove them
  const newJob = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(rawJob).filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined)
  );

  let createdId = '';
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'addDoc',
          collection: 'jobs',
          data: newJob
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.id) createdId = json.id;
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  try {
    const docPromise = addDoc(collection(db, 'jobs'), newJob);
    const docRef = await withTimeout(docPromise, 7000, 'Koneksi timeout saat mempublikasikan lowongan');
    if (!createdId) createdId = docRef.id;
  } catch (err) {
    if (!createdId) throw err;
  }

  return { ...newJob, id: createdId } as unknown as JobPosting;
}

// Kirim lamaran kerja (F-06: Menggunakan Deterministic ID jobId_applicantUid untuk mencegah duplikasi & race condition)
export async function applyJob(applicationData: Omit<JobApplication, 'id' | 'appliedAt' | 'status'>): Promise<JobApplication> {
  const deterministicId = `${applicationData.jobId}_${applicationData.applicantUid}`;

  const rawApp: Record<string, unknown> = {
    ...applicationData,
    status: 'pending' as const,
    appliedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  const newApp = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(rawApp).filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined)
  );

  let savedToApi = false;
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'applications',
          id: deterministicId,
          data: newApp,
          merge: false
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) savedToApi = true;
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  try {
    const docRef = doc(db, 'applications', deterministicId);
    await withTimeout(setDoc(docRef, newApp), 7000, 'Koneksi timeout saat mengirim lamaran');
  } catch (err) {
    if (!savedToApi) throw err;
  }

  return { ...newApp, id: deterministicId } as unknown as JobApplication;
}

// Ambil lamaran yang masuk untuk lowongan user
export async function getReceivedApplications(posterUid: string): Promise<JobApplication[]> {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=applications&posterUid=${encodeURIComponent(posterUid)}`, {
        cache: 'no-store'
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (Array.isArray(json.items)) {
          const apps = json.items as JobApplication[];
          apps.sort((a, b) => (b.appliedAt || '').localeCompare(a.appliedAt || ''));
          return apps;
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // fallback below
    }
  }

  try {
    const q = query(collection(db, 'applications'), where('posterUid', '==', posterUid));
    const snap = await withTimeout(getDocs(q), 5000, 'Received applications timeout');
    const apps = snap.docs.map(d => ({ ...d.data(), id: d.id } as JobApplication));
    apps.sort((a, b) => (b.appliedAt || '').localeCompare(a.appliedAt || ''));
    return apps;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('getReceivedApplications timed out or failed:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

// Ambil lamaran yang diajukan oleh user
export async function getMyApplications(applicantUid: string): Promise<JobApplication[]> {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/db?collection=applications&applicantUid=${encodeURIComponent(applicantUid)}`, {
        cache: 'no-store'
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (Array.isArray(json.items)) {
          const apps = json.items as JobApplication[];
          apps.sort((a, b) => (b.appliedAt || '').localeCompare(a.appliedAt || ''));
          return apps;
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // fallback below
    }
  }

  try {
    const q = query(collection(db, 'applications'), where('applicantUid', '==', applicantUid));
    const snap = await withTimeout(getDocs(q), 5000, 'My applications timeout');
    const apps = snap.docs.map(d => ({ ...d.data(), id: d.id } as JobApplication));
    apps.sort((a, b) => (b.appliedAt || '').localeCompare(a.appliedAt || ''));
    return apps;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('getMyApplications timed out or failed:', err);
    return [];
  }
}

// Update status lamaran (dilakukan oleh pemilik lowongan)
export async function updateApplicationStatus(
  applicationId: string, 
  status: 'pending' | 'reviewed' | 'accepted' | 'rejected'
): Promise<void> {
  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  let savedToApi = false;
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/db', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: 'setDoc',
          collection: 'applications',
          id: applicationId,
          data: { status, updatedAt: nowIso },
          merge: true
        })
      });
      if (res.ok) savedToApi = true;
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  try {
    const docRef = doc(db, 'applications', applicationId);
    await withTimeout(
      updateDoc(docRef, { status, updatedAt: nowIso }),
      5000,
      'Koneksi timeout saat memperbarui status lamaran'
    );
  } catch (err) {
    if (!savedToApi) throw err;
  }
}

// FILE: src\lib\media.ts

/**
 * Media and communication utility helpers
 */

/**
 * Robust YouTube video ID parser
 * Handles raw URLs (watch?v=, embed/, youtu.be/, shorts/, youtube-nocookie) and full <iframe> embed snippets.
 */
export function parseYouTubeVideoId(input?: unknown): string | null {
  if (typeof input !== 'string' || !input.trim()) return null;
  const raw = input.trim();
  
  // 1. If user pasted a full <iframe> tag, extract src first
  let url = raw;
  const iframeSrcMatch = raw.match(/src=["']([^"']+)["']/i);
  if (iframeSrcMatch && iframeSrcMatch[1]) {
    url = iframeSrcMatch[1];
  }

  // 2. Extract standard 11-character YouTube video ID
  const regExp = /(?:youtu\.be\/|youtube(?:-nocookie)?\.com\/(?:embed\/|v\/|watch\?v=|watch\?.+&v=|shorts\/))([\w-]{11})/i;
  const match = url.match(regExp);
  if (match && match[1]) {
    return match[1];
  }

  // Also catch generic ?v=xxx or &v=xxx in youtube domains
  const vParamMatch = url.match(/[?&]v=([\w-]{11})/i);
  if (vParamMatch && vParamMatch[1]) {
    return vParamMatch[1];
  }

  // 3. If user pasted the raw 11-char ID directly
  if (/^[\w-]{11}$/.test(url)) {
    return url;
  }

  return null;
}

/**
 * Formats a phone number for direct WhatsApp wa.me link.
 * Normalizes Indonesian numbers (08... -> 628..., +62... -> 62...).
 */
export function formatWhatsAppUrl(phone?: unknown): string {
  if (typeof phone !== 'string' || !phone.trim()) return '';
  const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (!digits) return '';
  if (digits.startsWith('0')) {
    return `https://wa.me/62${digits.slice(1)}`;
  }
  if (digits.startsWith('62')) {
    return `https://wa.me/${digits}`;
  }
  if (digits.startsWith('8')) {
    return `https://wa.me/62${digits}`;
  }
  return `https://wa.me/${digits}`;
}

/**
 * Ensures an external web link has http:// or https:// protocol.
 */
export function ensureAbsoluteUrl(url?: unknown): string {
  if (typeof url !== 'string' || !url.trim()) return '';
  const trimmed = url.trim();
  if (trimmed.startsWith('http://') || trimmed.startsWith('https://')) {
    return trimmed;
  }
  return `https://${trimmed}`;
}

/**
 * Validates and sanitizes HTTP/HTTPS URLs (F-07).
 * Prevents javascript:, data:, and vbscript: XSS injection vectors.
 */
export function safeHttpUrl(url: string | null | undefined, allowedHosts?: string[]): string | null {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return null;
  const trimmed = url.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return null;

  // Tolak tegas skema non-HTTP berbahaya (XSS vectors)
  const lower = trimmed.toLowerCase();
  if (
    lower.startsWith('javascript:') ||
    lower.startsWith('data:') ||
    lower.startsWith('vbscript:') ||
    lower.startsWith('file:') ||
    lower.startsWith('blob:')
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    const fullUrl = trimmed.startsWith('http://') || trimmed.startsWith('https://') 
      ? trimmed 
      : `https://${trimmed}`;
    const parsed = new URL(fullUrl);
    if (parsed.protocol !== 'http:' && parsed.protocol !== 'https:') {
      return null;
    }
    if (allowedHosts && allowedHosts.length > 0) {
      const isAllowed = allowedHosts.some(host => 
        parsed.hostname === host || parsed.hostname.endsWith(`.${host}`)
      );
      if (!isAllowed) return null;
    }
    return parsed.href;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
